Learning Vision-Based Omnidirectional Navigation: A Teacher-Student Approach Using Monocular Depth Estimation

Abstract (English): Reliable obstacle avoidance in industrial settings demands 3D scene understanding, but widely used 2D LiDAR sensors perceive only a single horizontal slice of the environment, missing critical obstacles above or below the scan plane. We present a teacher-student framework for vision-based mobile robot navigation that eliminates the need for LiDAR sensors. A teacher policy trained via Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) in NVIDIA Isaac Lab leverages privileged 2D LiDAR observations that account for the full robot footprint to learn robust navigation. The learned behavior is distilled into a student policy that relies solely on monocular depth maps predicted by a fine-tuned Depth Anything V2 model from four RGB cameras. The complete inference pipeline, comprising monocular depth estimation (MDE), policy execution, and motor control, runs entirely onboard an NVIDIA Jetson Orin AGX mounted on a DJI RoboMaster platform, requiring no external computation for inference. In simulation, the student achieves success rates of 82-96.5%, consistently outperforming the standard 2D LiDAR teacher (50-89%). In real-world experiments, the MDE-based student outperforms the 2D LiDAR teacher when navigating around obstacles with complex 3D geometries, such as overhanging structures and low-profile objects, that fall outside the single scan plane of a 2D LiDAR.

摘要（中文）: 在工业环境中，可靠的避障需要对三维场景的理解，但广泛使用的二维激光雷达传感器仅感知环境的单个水平切片，忽略了扫描平面上方或下方的关键障碍物。我们提出了一种基于视觉的移动机器人导航师生框架，消除了对激光雷达传感器的需求。一个通过近端策略优化(PPO)在NVIDIA Isaac Lab中训练的教师策略，利用特权二维激光雷达观测结果，这些观测结果考虑了机器人的完整足迹，从而学习鲁棒的导航。所学习的行为被提炼成一个学生策略，该策略仅依赖于由四个RGB相机和一个微调的Depth Anything V2模型预测的单目深度图。完整的推理流程，包括单目深度估计(MDE)、策略执行和电机控制，完全在安装在DJI RoboMaster平台上的NVIDIA Jetson Orin AGX上运行，无需外部计算来进行推理。在仿真中，学生策略的成功率达到82-96.5%，始终优于标准二维激光雷达教师策略(50-89%)。在真实世界的实验中，当导航围绕具有复杂三维几何形状的障碍物（例如悬垂结构和低矮物体，这些物体落在二维激光雷达的单个扫描平面之外）时，基于MDE的学生策略的表现优于二维激光雷达教师策略。

TLDR: This paper presents a teacher-student framework for vision-based robot navigation, using monocular depth estimation to surpass LiDAR-based navigation, achieving robust obstacle avoidance in simulation and real-world scenarios.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种基于视觉的机器人导航的师生框架，利用单目深度估计超越基于激光雷达的导航，在仿真和真实场景中实现了鲁棒的避障。

Authors: Jan Finke, Wayne Paul Martis, Adrian Schmelter, Lars Erbach, Christian Jestel, Marvin Wiedemann

Neural Implicit Action Fields: From Discrete Waypoints to Continuous Functions for Vision-Language-Action Models

Abstract (English): Despite the rapid progress of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, the prevailing paradigm of predicting discrete waypoints remains fundamentally misaligned with the intrinsic continuity of physical motion. This discretization imposes rigid sampling rates, lacks high-order differentiability, and introduces quantization artifacts that hinder precise, compliant interaction. We propose Neural Implicit Action Fields (NIAF), a paradigm shift that reformulates action prediction from discrete waypoints to continuous action function regression. By utilizing an MLLM as a hierarchical spectral modulator over a learnable motion prior, NIAF synthesizes infinite-resolution trajectories as continuous-time manifolds. This formulation enables analytical differentiability, allowing for explicit supervision of velocity, acceleration, and jerk to ensure mathematical consistency and physical plausibility. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art results on CALVIN and LIBERO benchmarks across diverse backbones. Furthermore, real-world experiments demonstrate that NIAF enables stable impedance control, bridging the gap between high-level semantic understanding and low-level dynamic execution.

摘要（中文）: 尽管视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型取得了快速进展，但预测离散航路点的主流范式与物理运动的内在连续性存在根本性的错位。这种离散化方式施加了严格的采样率，缺乏高阶可微性，并引入了量化伪影，从而阻碍了精确、顺从的交互。我们提出神经隐式动作场（NIAF），这是一种范式转变，将动作预测从离散航路点重新定义为连续动作函数回归。通过利用多模态大型语言模型（MLLM）作为可学习运动先验的分层谱调制器，NIAF将无限分辨率的轨迹合成为连续时间流形。这种公式化实现了解析可微性，允许对速度、加速度和急动度进行显式监督，以确保数学一致性和物理合理性。我们的方法在CALVIN和LIBERO基准测试中，针对不同的骨干网络，实现了最先进的结果。此外，真实世界实验表明，NIAF能够实现稳定的阻抗控制，弥合了高层语义理解和低层动态执行之间的差距。

TLDR: The paper introduces Neural Implicit Action Fields (NIAF), a new approach for Vision-Language-Action models that predicts continuous action functions instead of discrete waypoints, leading to improved results and stable impedance control.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了神经隐式动作场（NIAF），一种用于视觉-语言-动作模型的新方法，它预测连续的动作函数而不是离散的路径点，从而提高了结果并实现了稳定的阻抗控制。

Authors: Haoyun Liu, Jianzhuang Zhao, Xinyuan Chang, Tianle Shi, Chuanzhang Meng, Jiayuan Tan, Feng Xiong, Tong Lin, Dongjie Huo, Mu Xu, SongLin Dong, Zhiheng Ma, Yihong Gong, Sheng Zhong

Pri4R: Learning World Dynamics for Vision-Language-Action Models with Privileged 4D Representation

Abstract (English): Humans learn not only how their bodies move, but also how the surrounding world responds to their actions. In contrast, while recent Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models exhibit impressive semantic understanding, they often fail to capture the spatiotemporal dynamics governing physical interaction. In this paper, we introduce Pri4R, a simple yet effective approach that endows VLA models with an implicit understanding of world dynamics by leveraging privileged 4D information during training. Specifically, Pri4R augments VLAs with a lightweight point track head that predicts 3D point tracks. By injecting VLA features into this head to jointly predict future 3D trajectories, the model learns to incorporate evolving scene geometry within its shared representation space, enabling more physically aware context for precise control. Due to its architectural simplicity, Pri4R is compatible with dominant VLA design patterns with minimal changes. During inference, we run the model using the original VLA architecture unchanged; Pri4R adds no extra inputs, outputs, or computational overhead. Across simulation and real-world evaluations, Pri4R significantly improves performance on challenging manipulation tasks, including a +10% gain on LIBERO-Long and a +40% gain on RoboCasa. We further show that 3D point track prediction is an effective supervision target for learning action-world dynamics, and validate our design choices through extensive ablations.

摘要（中文）: 人类不仅学习身体如何运动，还学习周围世界如何响应他们的行为。相比之下，虽然最近的视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型展现出令人印象深刻的语义理解能力，但它们通常无法捕捉控制物理交互的时空动态。在本文中，我们提出 Pri4R，一种简单而有效的方法，通过在训练期间利用特权 4D 信息，赋予 VLA 模型对世界动态的隐式理解。具体来说，Pri4R 为 VLA 模型增加了一个轻量级的点轨迹预测头，用于预测 3D 点轨迹。通过将 VLA 特征注入到该预测头中，以联合预测未来的 3D 轨迹，该模型学习将演化的场景几何体整合到其共享表征空间中，从而为精确控制提供更具物理感知能力的环境信息。由于其架构简单，Pri4R 可以与主流 VLA 设计模式兼容，只需进行最小的更改。在推理过程中，我们使用原始 VLA 架构运行模型，无需更改；Pri4R 不增加额外的输入、输出或计算开销。通过仿真和真实世界的评估，Pri4R 显着提高了在具有挑战性的操作任务上的性能，包括在 LIBERO-Long 上提升 +10%，RoBoCasa 上提升 +40%。我们进一步表明，3D 点轨迹预测是学习动作-世界动态的有效监督目标，并通过广泛的消融实验验证了我们的设计选择。

TLDR: The paper introduces Pri4R, a method to improve Vision-Language-Action models by incorporating world dynamics learned through predicting 3D point tracks using privileged information during training, leading to significant performance gains in manipulation tasks.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了Pri4R，一种通过在训练期间使用特权信息预测3D点轨迹来学习世界动态，从而改进视觉-语言-动作模型的方法，从而在操作任务中实现显著的性能提升。

Authors: Jisoo Kim, Jungbin Cho, Sanghyeok Chu, Ananya Bal, Jinhyung Kim, Gunhee Lee, Sihaeng Lee, Seung Hwan Kim, Bohyung Han, Hyunmin Lee, Laszlo A. Jeni, Seungryong Kim

SFCo-Nav: Efficient Zero-Shot Visual Language Navigation via Collaboration of Slow LLM and Fast Attributed Graph Alignment

Abstract (English): Recent advances in large vision-language models (VLMs) and large language models (LLMs) have enabled zero-shot approaches to visual language navigation (VLN), where an agent follows natural language instructions using only ego perception and reasoning. However, existing zero-shot methods typically construct a naive observation graph and perform per-step VLM-LLM inference on it, resulting in high latency and computation costs that limit real-time deployment. To address this, we present SFCo-Nav, an efficient zero-shot VLN framework inspired by the principle of slow-fast cognitive collaboration. SFCo-Nav integrates three key modules: 1) a slow LLM-based planner that produces a strategic chain of subgoals, each linked to an imagined object graph; 2) a fast reactive navigator for real-time object graph construction and subgoal execution; and 3) a lightweight asynchronous slow-fast bridge aligns advanced structured, attributed imagined and perceived graphs to estimate navigation confidence, triggering the slow LLM planner only when necessary. To the best of our knowledge, SFCo-Nav is the first slow-fast collaboration zero-shot VLN system supporting asynchronous LLM triggering according to the internal confidence. Evaluated on the public R2R and REVERIE benchmarks, SFCo-Nav matches or exceeds prior state-of-the-art zero-shot VLN success rates while cutting total token consumption per trajectory by over 50% and running more than 3.5 times faster. Finally, we demonstrate SFCo-Nav on a legged robot in a hotel suite, showcasing its efficiency and practicality in indoor environments.

摘要（中文）: 大型视觉语言模型（VLM）和大型语言模型（LLM）的最新进展使得视觉语言导航（VLN）的零样本方法成为可能，在这种方法中，智能体仅使用自我的感知和推理遵循自然语言指令。然而，现有的零样本方法通常构建一个简单的观察图，并对其执行每步VLM-LLM推理，导致高延迟和计算成本，从而限制了实时部署。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种名为SFCo-Nav的高效零样本VLN框架，它的灵感来自于快慢认知协同的原则。SFCo-Nav集成了三个关键模块：1) 基于LLM的慢速规划器，它生成一个战略性的子目标链，每个目标都链接到一个想象的对象图；2) 用于实时对象图构建和子目标执行的快速反应式导航器；以及 3) 一个轻量级的异步快慢桥，它对齐高级的结构化、带有属性的想象和感知图，以估计导航置信度，仅在必要时触发慢速LLM规划器。据我们所知，SFCo-Nav是第一个支持根据内部置信度异步触发LLM的快慢协同零样本VLN系统。在公共R2R和REVERIE基准上的评估结果表明，SFCo-Nav在达到或超过先前最先进的零样本VLN成功率的同时，将每条轨迹的总token消耗量减少了50%以上，并且运行速度提高了3.5倍以上。最后，我们在酒店套房的足式机器人上演示了SFCo-Nav，展示了它在室内环境中的效率和实用性。

TLDR: SFCo-Nav is a novel zero-shot VLN framework that leverages a slow LLM planner and a fast graph-based navigator with an asynchronous confidence-based bridge, achieving state-of-the-art results with significant efficiency gains and demonstrated on a real robot.

简要说明: SFCo-Nav是一个新颖的零样本VLN框架，它利用慢速LLM规划器和快速基于图的导航器，以及一个基于异步置信度的桥梁，在实现最先进结果的同时，显著提高了效率，并在真实机器人上进行了演示。

Authors: Chaoran Xiong, Litao Wei, Xinhao Hu, Kehui Ma, Ziyi Xia, Zixin Jiang, Zhen Sun, Ling Pei

ROSER: Few-Shot Robotic Sequence Retrieval for Scalable Robot Learning

Abstract (English): A critical bottleneck in robot learning is the scarcity of task-labeled, segmented training data, despite the abundance of large-scale robotic datasets recorded as long, continuous interaction logs. Existing datasets contain vast amounts of diverse behaviors, yet remain structurally incompatible with modern learning frameworks that require cleanly segmented, task-specific trajectories. We address this data utilization crisis by formalizing robotic sequence retrieval: the task of extracting reusable, task-centric segments from unlabeled logs using only a few reference examples. We introduce ROSER, a lightweight few-shot retrieval framework that learns task-agnostic metric spaces over temporal windows, enabling accurate retrieval with as few as 3-5 demonstrations, without any task-specific training required. To validate our approach, we establish comprehensive evaluation protocols and benchmark ROSER against classical alignment methods, learned embeddings, and language model baselines across three large-scale datasets (e.g., LIBERO, DROID, and nuScenes). Our experiments demonstrate that ROSER consistently outperforms all prior methods in both accuracy and efficiency, achieving sub-millisecond per-match inference while maintaining superior distributional alignment. By reframing data curation as few-shot retrieval, ROSER provides a practical pathway to unlock underutilized robotic datasets, fundamentally improving data availability for robot learning.

摘要（中文）: 机器人学习中的一个关键瓶颈是任务标记的、已分割的训练数据的稀缺性，尽管存在大量记录为长期的、连续交互日志的大规模机器人数据集。现有数据集包含大量的多样化行为，但其结构与需要干净分割的、特定于任务的轨迹的现代学习框架不兼容。我们通过形式化机器人序列检索来解决这一数据利用危机：即仅使用少量参考示例，从无标记日志中提取可重用的、以任务为中心的片段的任务。我们引入了ROSER，这是一个轻量级的少样本检索框架，它学习时间窗口上的任务不可知度量空间，从而能够仅用3-5个演示就能进行准确的检索，而无需任何特定于任务的训练。为了验证我们的方法，我们建立了全面的评估协议，并在三个大规模数据集（例如，LIBERO、DROID和nuScenes）上，将ROSER与经典对齐方法、学习的嵌入和语言模型基线进行了基准测试。我们的实验表明，ROSER在准确性和效率方面始终优于所有先前的方法，在保持卓越的分布对齐的同时，实现了亚毫秒级的每次匹配推理。Through reframing data curation as few-shot retrieval, ROSER提供了一种实用的途径来解锁未充分利用的机器人数据集，从根本上改善了机器人学习的数据可用性。

TLDR: This paper introduces ROSER, a few-shot robotic sequence retrieval framework that extracts task-centric segments from unlabeled robot interaction logs using a few reference examples. It achieves state-of-the-art retrieval accuracy and efficiency across several large-scale datasets.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了ROSER，一个少样本机器人序列检索框架，它使用少量参考示例从无标签的机器人交互日志中提取以任务为中心的片段。该方法在多个大型数据集上实现了最先进的检索准确率和效率。

Authors: Zillur Rahman, Eddison Pham, Alejandro Daniel Noel, Cristian Meo

Unifying Language-Action Understanding and Generation for Autonomous Driving

Abstract (English): Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are emerging as a promising paradigm for end-to-end autonomous driving, valued for their potential to leverage world knowledge and reason about complex driving scenes. However, existing methods suffer from two critical limitations: a persistent misalignment between language instructions and action outputs, and the inherent inefficiency of typical auto-regressive action generation. In this paper, we introduce LinkVLA, a novel architecture that directly addresses these challenges to enhance both alignment and efficiency. First, we establish a structural link by unifying language and action tokens into a shared discrete codebook, processed within a single multi-modal model. This structurally enforces cross-modal consistency from the ground up. Second, to create a deep semantic link, we introduce an auxiliary action understanding objective that trains the model to generate descriptive captions from trajectories, fostering a bidirectional language-action mapping. Finally, we replace the slow, step-by-step generation with a two-step coarse-to-fine generation method C2F that efficiently decodes the action sequence, saving 86% inference time. Experiments on closed-loop driving benchmarks show consistent gains in instruction following accuracy and driving performance, alongside reduced inference latency.

摘要（中文）: 视觉-语言-行动 (VLA) 模型正逐渐成为端到端自动驾驶领域一种有前景的范式，因其利用世界知识和推理复杂驾驶场景的潜力而备受重视。然而，现有方法存在两个关键局限性：语言指令和行动输出之间持续存在的错位，以及典型自回归行动生成固有的低效性。在本文中，我们提出了 LinkVLA，一种新颖的架构，旨在直接解决这些挑战，从而提高对齐性和效率。首先，我们通过将语言和行动 tokens 统一到一个共享的离散密码本中，并在一个单一的多模态模型中进行处理，来建立结构性链接。这种结构从根本上强制执行跨模态一致性。其次，为了创建深度语义链接，我们引入了一个辅助行动理解目标，训练模型从轨迹生成描述性文本，从而促进双向语言-行动映射。最后，我们用两步式的粗到精生成方法 C2F 替换了缓慢的、逐步的生成方式，从而高效地解码行动序列，节省 86% 的推理时间。在闭环驾驶基准上的实验表明，在指令遵循准确性和驾驶性能方面均实现了持续的提升，同时也降低了推理延迟。

TLDR: The paper introduces LinkVLA, a novel Vision-Language-Action architecture for autonomous driving that unifies language and action tokens, uses an auxiliary action understanding objective, and employs a coarse-to-fine generation method for improved alignment, efficiency, and driving performance.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种新颖的用于自动驾驶的视觉-语言-动作架构LinkVLA，它统一了语言和动作token，使用辅助动作理解目标，并采用粗到细的生成方法，从而提高对齐性、效率和驾驶性能。

Authors: Xinyang Wang, Qian Liu, Wenjie Ding, Zhao Yang, Wei Li, Chang Liu, Bailin Li, Kun Zhan, Xianpeng Lang, Wei Chen

LaST-VLA: Thinking in Latent Spatio-Temporal Space for Vision-Language-Action in Autonomous Driving

Abstract (English): While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have revolutionized autonomous driving by unifying perception and planning, their reliance on explicit textual Chain-of-Thought (CoT) leads to semantic-perceptual decoupling and perceptual-symbolic conflicts. Recent shifts toward latent reasoning attempt to bypass these bottlenecks by thinking in continuous hidden space. However, without explicit intermediate constraints, standard latent CoT often operates as a physics-agnostic representation. To address this, we propose the Latent Spatio-Temporal VLA (LaST-VLA), a framework shifting the reasoning paradigm from discrete symbolic processing into a physically grounded Latent Spatio-Temporal CoT. By implementing a dual-feature alignment mechanism, we distill geometric constraints from 3D foundation models and dynamic foresight from world models directly into the latent space. Coupled with a progressive SFT training strategy that transitions from feature alignment to trajectory generation, and refined via Reinforcement Learning with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to ensure safety and rule compliance. \method~setting a new record on NAVSIM v1 (91.3 PDMS) and NAVSIM v2 (87.1 EPDMS), while excelling in spatial-temporal reasoning on SURDS and NuDynamics benchmarks.

摘要（中文）: 尽管视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通过统一感知和规划彻底改变了自动驾驶领域，但它们对显式文本思维链（CoT）的依赖会导致语义-感知解耦和感知-符号冲突。 近期向潜在推理的转变试图通过在连续隐藏空间中思考来绕过这些瓶颈。 然而，在没有显式的中间约束的情况下，标准潜在CoT通常作为一种与物理无关的表示形式运行。 为了解决这个问题，我们提出了潜在时空VLA（LaST-VLA），一个将推理范式从离散符号处理转移到具有物理基础的潜在时空CoT的框架。 通过实施双重特征对齐机制，我们将来自3D基础模型的几何约束和来自世界模型的动态预测直接提炼到潜在空间中。 结合从特征对齐过渡到轨迹生成的渐进式SFT训练策略，并通过基于群体相对策略优化（GRPO）的强化学习进行优化，以确保安全性和规则遵守。 LaST-VLA在NAVSIM v1（91.3 PDMS）和NAVSIM v2（87.1 EPDMS）上创造了新的记录，同时在SURDS和NuDynamics基准测试中表现出卓越的时空推理能力。

TLDR: This paper introduces LaST-VLA, a framework for autonomous driving that uses a latent spatio-temporal representation to improve Vision-Language-Action models by incorporating geometric constraints and dynamic foresight, achieving state-of-the-art results on multiple benchmarks.

简要说明: 本文提出了LaST-VLA，一个用于自动驾驶的框架，它使用潜在的时空表示来改进视觉-语言-动作模型，通过整合几何约束和动态预测，在多个基准测试上取得了最先进的结果。

Authors: Yuechen Luo, Fang Li, Shaoqing Xu, Yang Ji, Zehan Zhang, Bing Wang, Yuannan Shen, Jianwei Cui, Long Chen, Guang Chen, Hangjun Ye, Zhi-Xin Yang, Fuxi Wen

SEAR: Sample Efficient Action Chunking Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): Action chunking can improve exploration and value estimation in long horizon reinforcement learning, but makes learning substantially harder since the critic must evaluate action sequences rather than single actions, greatly increasing approximation and data efficiency challenges. As a result, existing action chunking methods, primarily designed for the offline and offline-to-online settings, have not achieved strong performance in purely online reinforcement learning. We introduce SEAR, an off policy online reinforcement learning algorithm for action chunking. It exploits the temporal structure of action chunks and operates with a receding horizon, effectively combining the benefits of small and large chunk sizes. SEAR outperforms state of the art online reinforcement learning methods on Metaworld, training with chunk sizes up to 20.

摘要（中文）: 动作组块能够提升长时序强化学习中的探索和价值估计，但同时也显著增加了学习难度，因为评论家必须评估动作序列而非单个动作，从而极大加剧了近似和数据效率方面的挑战。因此，现有的动作组块方法，主要为离线和离线到在线环境设计，在纯在线强化学习中并未取得优异性能。我们提出了一种用于动作组块的离策略在线强化学习算法SEAR。它利用动作组块的时间结构并采用后退窗口，有效地结合了小块和大块尺寸的优势。在Metaworld上，SEAR优于最先进的在线强化学习方法，训练时块大小最高可达20。

TLDR: SEAR is a novel online reinforcement learning algorithm that leverages action chunking and a receding horizon approach to improve performance in long-horizon tasks, outperforming existing methods on Metaworld.

简要说明: SEAR是一种新颖的在线强化学习算法，它利用动作分块和后退视野方法来提升长程任务的性能，并在Metaworld上优于现有方法。

Authors: C. F. Maximilian Nagy, Onur Celik, Emiliyan Gospodinov, Florian Seligmann, Weiran Liao, Aryan Kaushik, Gerhard Neumann

Robometer: Scaling General-Purpose Robotic Reward Models via Trajectory Comparisons

Abstract (English): General-purpose robot reward models are typically trained to predict absolute task progress from expert demonstrations, providing only local, frame-level supervision. While effective for expert demonstrations, this paradigm scales poorly to large-scale robotics datasets where failed and suboptimal trajectories are abundant and assigning dense progress labels is ambiguous. We introduce Robometer, a scalable reward modeling framework that combines intra-trajectory progress supervision with inter-trajectory preference supervision. Robometer is trained with a dual objective: a frame-level progress loss that anchors reward magnitude on expert data, and a trajectory-comparison preference loss that imposes global ordering constraints across trajectories of the same task, enabling effective learning from both real and augmented failed trajectories. To support this formulation at scale, we curate RBM-1M, a reward-learning dataset comprising over one million trajectories spanning diverse robot embodiments and tasks, including substantial suboptimal and failure data. Across benchmarks and real-world evaluations, Robometer learns more generalizable reward functions than prior methods and improves robot learning performance across a diverse set of downstream applications. Code, model weights, and videos at https://robometer.github.io/.

摘要（中文）: 通用机器人奖励模型通常被训练来预测专家演示中的绝对任务进度，仅提供局部、帧级别的监督。虽然这对于专家演示有效，但这种范式在大型机器人数据集上的扩展性较差，因为在这些数据集中，失败的和次优的轨迹大量存在，并且分配密集的进度标签存在歧义。我们引入 Robometer，一种可扩展的奖励建模框架，它结合了轨迹内进度监督与轨迹间偏好监督。Robometer 通过双重目标进行训练：一个帧级别的进度损失，将奖励幅度锚定在专家数据上；以及一个轨迹比较偏好损失，对相同任务的轨迹施加全局排序约束，从而能够从真实和增强的失败轨迹中进行有效学习。为了在大规模上支持这种公式化，我们策划了 RBM-1M，一个包含超过一百万条轨迹的奖励学习数据集，涵盖了不同的机器人形态和任务，其中包括大量的次优和失败数据。在基准测试和真实世界的评估中，Robometer 学习到的奖励函数比以往的方法更具泛化性，并提高了机器人学习在各种下游应用中的性能。代码、模型权重和视频见 https://robometer.github.io/。

TLDR: Robometer is a framework for learning general-purpose robotic reward models using both intra-trajectory progress supervision and inter-trajectory preference supervision, trained on a new large-scale dataset of robot trajectories including failures, leading to more generalizable rewards and improved downstream performance.

简要说明: Robometer是一个用于学习通用机器人奖励模型的框架，它结合了轨迹内部进度监督和轨迹间偏好监督，并在包含失败的大规模机器人轨迹数据集上进行训练，从而实现更通用的奖励和改进的下游性能。

Authors: Anthony Liang, Yigit Korkmaz, Jiahui Zhang, Minyoung Hwang, Abrar Anwar, Sidhant Kaushik, Aditya Shah, Alex S. Huang, Luke Zettlemoyer, Dieter Fox, Yu Xiang, Anqi Li, Andreea Bobu, Abhishek Gupta, Stephen Tu, Erdem Biyik, Jesse Zhang

ACDC: Adaptive Curriculum Planning with Dynamic Contrastive Control for Goal-Conditioned Reinforcement Learning in Robotic Manipulation

Abstract (English): Goal-conditioned reinforcement learning has shown considerable potential in robotic manipulation; however, existing approaches remain limited by their reliance on prioritizing collected experience, resulting in suboptimal performance across diverse tasks. Inspired by human learning behaviors, we propose a more comprehensive learning paradigm, ACDC, which integrates multidimensional Adaptive Curriculum (AC) Planning with Dynamic Contrastive (DC) Control to guide the agent along a well-designed learning trajectory. More specifically, at the planning level, the AC component schedules the learning curriculum by dynamically balancing diversity-driven exploration and quality-driven exploitation based on the agent's success rate and training progress. At the control level, the DC component implements the curriculum plan through norm-constrained contrastive learning, enabling magnitude-guided experience selection aligned with the current curriculum focus. Extensive experiments on challenging robotic manipulation tasks demonstrate that ACDC consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art baselines in both sample efficiency and final task success rate.

摘要（中文）: 目标条件强化学习在机器人操作领域展现出巨大的潜力。然而，现有方法仍然受限于对已收集经验的优先依赖，导致在各种任务中的性能欠佳。受人类学习行为的启发，我们提出一种更全面的学习范式，ACDC，它将多维自适应课程（AC）规划与动态对比（DC）控制相结合，以引导智能体沿着精心设计的学习轨迹进行学习。更具体地说，在规划层面，AC组件通过动态平衡由多样性驱动的探索和由质量驱动的利用，根据智能体的成功率和训练进度来安排学习课程。在控制层面，DC组件通过范数约束的对比学习来实现课程计划，从而实现与当前课程重点相一致的、幅度引导的经验选择。在具有挑战性的机器人操作任务上的大量实验表明，ACDC在样本效率和最终任务成功率方面均始终优于最先进的基线方法。

TLDR: The paper introduces ACDC, a new goal-conditioned reinforcement learning approach for robotic manipulation that uses Adaptive Curriculum planning and Dynamic Contrastive control to improve sample efficiency and task success.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种新的目标条件强化学习方法ACDC，用于机器人操作。该方法采用自适应课程规划和动态对比控制，以提高样本效率和任务成功率。

Authors: Xuerui Wang, Guangyu Ren, Tianhong Dai, Bintao Hu, Shuangyao Huang, Wenzhang Zhang, Hengyan Liu

$π$-StepNFT: Wider Space Needs Finer Steps in Online RL for Flow-based VLAs

Abstract (English): Flow-based vision-language-action (VLA) models excel in embodied control but suffer from intractable likelihoods during multi-step sampling, hindering online reinforcement learning. We propose \textbf{\textit{$\boldsymbolπ$-StepNFT}} (Step-wise Negative-aware Fine-Tuning), a critic-and-likelihood-free framework that requires only a single forward pass per optimization step and eliminates auxiliary value networks. We identify that wider exploration spaces necessitate finer-grained, step-wise guidance for alignment. Empirically, $π$-StepNFT unlocks latent potential on LIBERO with competitive few-shot robustness. Moreover, it achieves superior generalization on ManiSkill, outperforming value-based baselines in OOD scenarios by preventing overfitting to multimodal features. This property offers a scalable solution promising for complex real-world applications.

摘要（中文）: 基于流的视觉-语言-动作 (VLA) 模型在具身控制方面表现出色，但在多步采样期间面临难以处理的似然性问题，从而阻碍了在线强化学习。我们提出了一种**{\textit{\textbf{{\boldmath$\pi$}}-StepNFT}}**（步进式负感知微调），这是一个无需评论器且无需似然度的框架，每个优化步骤仅需一次前向传递，并消除了辅助价值网络。我们发现更宽广的探索空间需要更精细的、步进式的对齐指导。实验表明，$π$-StepNFT 在 LIBERO 上释放了潜在能力，具备有竞争力的少样本鲁棒性。此外，它在 ManiSkill 上实现了卓越的泛化能力，通过防止对多模态特征的过拟合，在 OOD 场景中优于基于价值的基线方法。这一特性提供了一种可扩展的解决方案，有望应用于复杂的现实世界场景。

TLDR: $π$-StepNFT is a novel reinforcement learning framework for vision-language-action models that addresses the intractable likelihood issue during multi-step sampling by using a critic-and-likelihood-free approach, achieving superior generalization in OOD scenarios.

简要说明: $π$-StepNFT 是一种新的视觉-语言-动作模型的强化学习框架，通过使用无打分器和无似然的方法解决了多步采样过程中难以处理的似然问题，并在 OOD 场景中实现了卓越的泛化能力。

Authors: Siting Wang, Xiaofeng Wang, Zheng Zhu, Minnan Pei, Xinyu Cui, Cheng Deng, Jian Zhao, Guan Huang, Haifeng Zhang, Jun Wang

LAD-Drive: Bridging Language and Trajectory with Action-Aware Diffusion Transformers

Abstract (English): While multimodal large language models (MLLMs) provide advanced reasoning for autonomous driving, translating their discrete semantic knowledge into continuous trajectories remains a fundamental challenge. Existing methods often rely on unimodal planning heads that inherently limit their ability to represent multimodal driving behavior. Furthermore, most generative approaches frequently condition on one-hot encoded actions, discarding the nuanced navigational uncertainty critical for complex scenarios. To resolve these limitations, we introduce LAD-Drive, a generative framework that structurally disentangles high-level intention from low-level spatial planning. LAD-Drive employs an action decoder to infer a probabilistic meta-action distribution, establishing an explicit belief state that preserves the nuanced intent typically lost by one-hot encodings. This distribution, fused with the vehicle's kinematic state, conditions an action-aware diffusion decoder that utilizes a truncated denoising process to refine learned motion anchors into safe, kinematically feasible trajectories. Extensive evaluations on the LangAuto benchmark demonstrate that LAD-Drive achieves state-of-the-art results, outperforming competitive baselines by up to 59% in Driving Score while significantly reducing route deviations and collisions. We will publicly release the code and models on https://github.com/iis-esslingen/lad-drive.

摘要（中文）: 多模态大型语言模型(MLLM)为自动驾驶提供了高级推理能力，但将它们的离散语义知识转化为连续轨迹仍然是一个根本性挑战。现有方法通常依赖于单模态规划头，这固有地限制了它们表示多模态驾驶行为的能力。此外，大多数生成式方法经常以独热编码动作为条件，从而丢弃了复杂场景中至关重要的细微导航不确定性。为了解决这些限制，我们引入了LAD-Drive，一种生成式框架，可在结构上将高级意图与低级空间规划解耦。LAD-Drive采用了一个动作解码器来推断概率性元动作分布，从而建立了一个显式的置信状态，该状态保留了通常被独热编码所丢失的细微意图。该分布与车辆的运动学状态融合后，将用于调节一个动作感知扩散解码器，该解码器利用截断去噪过程将学习到的运动锚点细化为安全、运动学上可行的轨迹。在LangAuto基准上的大量评估表明，LAD-Drive达到了最先进的结果，在驾驶评分方面优于具有竞争力的基线高达59%，同时显著减少了路线偏差和碰撞。我们将在https://github.com/iis-esslingen/lad-drive上公开发布代码和模型。

TLDR: The paper presents LAD-Drive, a novel autonomous driving framework using diffusion transformers conditioned on a probabilistic meta-action distribution to generate continuous trajectories from high-level language instructions, achieving state-of-the-art results on the LangAuto benchmark.

简要说明: 该论文提出了LAD-Drive，一种新颖的自动驾驶框架，使用基于概率元动作分布的扩散Transformer，从高级语言指令生成连续轨迹，并在LangAuto基准测试上取得了最先进的结果。

Authors: Fabian Schmidt, Karol Fedurko, Markus Enzweiler, Abhinav Valada

CHOP: Counterfactual Human Preference Labels Improve Obstacle Avoidance in Visuomotor Navigation Policies

Abstract (English): Visuomotor navigation policies have shown strong perception-action coupling for embodied agents, yet they often struggle with safe navigation and dynamic obstacle avoidance in complex real-world environments. We introduce CHOP, a novel approach that leverages Counterfactual Human Preference Labels to align visuomotor navigation policies towards human intuition of safety and obstacle avoidance in navigation. In CHOP, for each visual observation, the robot's executed trajectory is included among a set of counterfactual navigation trajectories: alternative trajectories the robot could have followed under identical conditions. Human annotators provide pairwise preference labels over these trajectories based on anticipated outcomes such as collision risk and path efficiency. These aggregated preferences are then used to fine-tune visuomotor navigation policies, aligning their behavior with human preferences in navigation. Experiments on the SCAND dataset show that visuomotor navigation policies fine-tuned with CHOP reduce near-collision events by 49.7%, decrease deviation from human-preferred trajectories by 45.0%, and increase average obstacle clearance by 19.8% on average across multiple state-of-the-art models, compared to their pretrained baselines. These improvements transfer to real-world deployments on a Ghost Robotics Vision60 quadruped, where CHOP-aligned policies improve average goal success rates by 24.4%, increase minimum obstacle clearance by 6.8%, reduce collision and intervention events by 45.7%, and improve normalized path completion by 38.6% on average across navigation scenarios, compared to their pretrained baselines. Our results highlight the value of counterfactual preference supervision in bridging the gap between large-scale visuomotor policies and human-aligned, safety-aware embodied navigation.

摘要（中文）: 基于视觉运动的导航策略在具身智能体中展现出强大的感知与行动耦合能力，但它们往往难以在复杂的真实世界环境中实现安全导航和动态避障。我们引入了一种名为CHOP的新方法，它利用反事实人类偏好标签来校准视觉运动导航策略，使其更符合人类关于导航安全性和避障的直觉。在CHOP中，对于每个视觉观测，机器人的已执行轨迹会被纳入一系列反事实导航轨迹中：在相同条件下机器人本可以遵循的替代轨迹。人类标注者基于诸如碰撞风险和路径效率等预期结果，为这些轨迹提供成对偏好标签。这些聚合的偏好随后被用于微调视觉运动导航策略，使其行为与人类在导航中的偏好对齐。在SCAND数据集上的实验表明，与预训练的基线模型相比，通过CHOP微调的视觉运动导航策略可以将近碰撞事件减少49.7%，减少与人类偏好轨迹的偏差45.0%，并在多种最先进模型中平均提高障碍物净空距离19.8%。这些改进迁移到Ghost Robotics Vision60四足机器人的真实部署中，在那里，与预训练的基线模型相比，CHOP对齐的策略在导航场景中平均提高目标成功率24.4%，增加最小障碍物净空距离6.8%，减少碰撞和干预事件45.7%，并提高标准化路径完成度38.6%。我们的结果突显了反事实偏好监督在弥合大规模视觉运动策略与人类对齐的、具有安全意识的具身导航之间的差距的价值。

TLDR: The paper introduces CHOP, a method using counterfactual human preference labels to fine-tune visuomotor navigation policies, improving safety and obstacle avoidance in both simulation and real-world robotic navigation tasks.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了CHOP，一种利用反事实人类偏好标签来微调视觉运动导航策略的方法，从而提高模拟和真实世界机器人导航任务中的安全性和避障能力。

Authors: Gershom Seneviratne, Jianyu An, Vaibhav Shende, Sahire Ellahy, Yaxita Amin, Kondapi Manasanjani, Samarth Chopra, Jonathan Deepak Kannan, Dinesh Manocha

Closed-Loop Action Chunks with Dynamic Corrections for Training-Free Diffusion Policy

Abstract (English): Diffusion-based policies have achieved remarkable results in robotic manipulation but often struggle to adapt rapidly in dynamic scenarios, leading to delayed responses or task failures. We present DCDP, a Dynamic Closed-Loop Diffusion Policy framework that integrates chunk-based action generation with real-time correction. DCDP integrates a self-supervised dynamic feature encoder, cross-attention fusion, and an asymmetric action encoder-decoder to inject environmental dynamics before action execution, achieving real-time closed-loop action correction and enhancing the system's adaptability in dynamic scenarios. In dynamic PushT simulations, DCDP improves adaptability by 19\% without retraining while requiring only 5\% additional computation. Its modular design enables plug-and-play integration, achieving both temporal coherence and real-time responsiveness in dynamic robotic scenarios, including real-world manipulation tasks. The project page is at: https://github.com/wupengyuan/dcdp

摘要（中文）: 基于扩散的策略在机器人操作中取得了显著成果，但通常难以在动态场景中快速适应，导致响应延迟或任务失败。我们提出了DCDP，一个动态闭环扩散策略框架，它集成了基于分块的动作生成与实时校正。DCDP集成了自监督动态特征编码器、交叉注意力融合以及非对称动作编码器-解码器，以在动作执行前注入环境动态信息，实现实时的闭环动作校正，并增强系统在动态场景中的适应性。 在动态PushT模拟中，DCDP在无需重新训练的情况下，将适应性提高了19\%，同时仅需额外的5\%计算量。其模块化设计支持即插即用集成，在动态机器人场景（包括真实世界操作任务）中实现了时间一致性和实时响应。 项目主页：https://github.com/wupengyuan/dcdp

TLDR: This paper introduces DCDP, a diffusion-based policy framework that improves adaptability in dynamic robotic manipulation scenarios by integrating chunk-based action generation with real-time correction, achieving better performance with minimal additional computation and no retraining.

简要说明: 本文介绍了DCDP，一个基于扩散策略的框架，通过将基于块的动作生成与实时校正相结合，提高了动态机器人操作场景中的适应性，以最小的额外计算和无需重新训练实现了更好的性能。

Authors: Pengyuan Wu, Pingrui Zhang, Zhigang Wang, Dong Wang, Bin Zhao, Xuelong Li

SaferPath: Hierarchical Visual Navigation with Learned Guidance and Safety-Constrained Control

Abstract (English): Visual navigation is a core capability for mobile robots, yet end-to-end learning-based methods often struggle with generalization and safety in unseen, cluttered, or narrow environments. These limitations are especially pronounced in dense indoor settings, where collisions are likely and end-to-end models frequently fail. To address this, we propose SaferPath, a hierarchical visual navigation framework that leverages learned guidance from existing end-to-end models and refines it through a safety-constrained optimization-control module. SaferPath transforms visual observations into a traversable-area map and refines guidance trajectories using Model Predictive Stein Variational Evolution Strategy (MP-SVES), efficiently generating safe trajectories in only a few iterations. The refined trajectories are tracked by an MPC controller, ensuring robust navigation in complex environments. Extensive experiments in scenarios with unseen obstacles, dense unstructured spaces, and narrow corridors demonstrate that SaferPath consistently improves success rates and reduces collisions, outperforming representative baselines such as ViNT and NoMaD, and enabling safe navigation in challenging real-world settings.

摘要（中文）: 视觉导航是移动机器人的核心能力，然而，基于端到端学习的方法常常难以在未见过的、拥挤的或狭窄的环境中泛化和保证安全。这些限制在密集的室内环境中尤为显著，在这些环境中碰撞很可能发生，且端到端模型经常失效。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了 SaferPath，一个分层视觉导航框架，它利用现有端到端模型的学习引导，并通过安全约束的优化控制模块对其进行改进。SaferPath 将视觉观测转换成可通行区域地图，并使用基于模型预测斯坦因变分进化策略 (MP-SVES) 改进引导轨迹，从而仅需少量迭代即可高效生成安全轨迹。改进后的轨迹由 MPC 控制器跟踪，确保在复杂环境中实现稳健的导航。在包含未见障碍物、密集非结构化空间和狭窄走廊的场景中进行的大量实验表明，SaferPath 始终能提高成功率并减少碰撞，优于 ViNT 和 NoMaD 等代表性基线，并能够在具有挑战性的真实环境中实现安全导航。

TLDR: SaferPath is a hierarchical visual navigation framework using learned guidance and safety-constrained control, which improves navigation success rates and reduces collisions in complex environments compared to baselines.

简要说明: SaferPath是一个分层视觉导航框架，它利用学习到的指导和安全约束控制，与基线相比，提高了复杂环境中的导航成功率并减少了碰撞。

Authors: Lingjie Zhang, Zeyu Jiang, Changhao Chen

Tiny-DroNeRF: Tiny Neural Radiance Fields aboard Federated Learning-enabled Nano-drones

Abstract (English): Sub-30g nano-sized aerial robots can leverage their agility and form factor to autonomously explore cluttered and narrow environments, like in industrial inspection and search and rescue missions. However, the price for their tiny size is a strong limit in their resources, i.e., sub-100 mW microcontroller units (MCUs) delivering $\sim$100 GOps/s at best, and memory budgets well below 100 MB. Despite these strict constraints, we aim to enable complex vision-based tasks aboard nano-drones, such as dense 3D scene reconstruction: a key robotic task underlying fundamental capabilities like spatial awareness and motion planning. Top-performing 3D reconstruction methods leverage neural radiance fields (NeRF) models, which require GBs of memory and massive computation, usually delivered by high-end GPUs consuming 100s of Watts. Our work introduces Tiny-DroNeRF, a lightweight NeRF model, based on Instant-NGP, and optimized for running on a GAP9 ultra-low-power (ULP) MCU aboard our nano-drones. Then, we further empower our Tiny-DroNeRF by leveraging a collaborative federated learning scheme, which distributes the model training among multiple nano-drones. Our experimental results show a 96% reduction in Tiny-DroNeRF's memory footprint compared to Instant-NGP, with only a 5.7 dB drop in reconstruction accuracy. Finally, our federated learning scheme allows Tiny-DroNeRF to train with an amount of data otherwise impossible to keep in a single drone's memory, increasing the overall reconstruction accuracy. Ultimately, our work combines, for the first time, NeRF training on an ULP MCU with federated learning on nano-drones.

摘要（中文）: 亚30克纳米级无人机可以利用其敏捷性和外形尺寸自主探索拥挤和狭窄的环境，例如在工业检测和搜救任务中。然而，其微小尺寸的代价是资源的严重限制，即功耗低于100毫瓦的微控制器单元（MCU），至多提供约100 GOps/s的算力，以及远低于100 MB的内存预算。尽管存在这些严格的限制，我们的目标是使纳米无人机能够执行复杂的基于视觉的任务，例如稠密3D场景重建：这是一项关键的机器人任务，是空间感知和运动规划等基本能力的基础。性能最佳的3D重建方法利用神经辐射场（NeRF）模型，该模型需要GB级的内存和海量计算，通常由消耗数百瓦功率的高端GPU提供。我们的工作推出了Tiny-DroNeRF，这是一种轻量级的NeRF模型，基于Instant-NGP，并针对在我们的纳米无人机上的GAP9超低功耗（ULP）MCU上运行进行了优化。然后，我们通过利用一种协作的联邦学习方案进一步增强了我们的Tiny-DroNeRF，该方案将模型训练分配到多个纳米无人机之间。我们的实验结果表明，与Instant-NGP相比，Tiny-DroNeRF的内存占用减少了96%，而重建精度仅下降了5.7 dB。最后，我们的联邦学习方案允许Tiny-DroNeRF使用通常不可能存储在单个无人机内存中的大量数据进行训练，从而提高了整体重建精度。最终，我们的工作首次将超低功耗MCU上的NeRF训练与纳米无人机上的联邦学习相结合。

TLDR: This paper introduces Tiny-DroNeRF, a lightweight NeRF model optimized for nano-drones with federated learning, achieving significant memory reduction and improved accuracy in 3D scene reconstruction.

简要说明: 本文介绍了 Tiny-DroNeRF，一种针对纳米无人机优化的轻量级 NeRF 模型，结合了联邦学习，在三维场景重建中实现了显著的内存缩减和精度提升。

Authors: Ilenia Carboni, Elia Cereda, Lorenzo Lamberti, Daniele Malpetti, Francesco Conti, Daniele Palossi

SSMG-Nav: Enhancing Lifelong Object Navigation with Semantic Skeleton Memory Graph

Abstract (English): Navigating to out-of-sight targets from human instructions in unfamiliar environments is a core capability for service robots. Despite substantial progress, most approaches underutilize reusable, persistent memory, constraining performance in lifelong settings. Many are additionally limited to single-modality inputs and employ myopic greedy policies, which often induce inefficient back-and-forth maneuvers (BFMs). To address such limitations, we introduce SSMG-Nav, a framework for object navigation built on a \textit{Semantic Skeleton Memory Graph} (SSMG) that consolidates past observations into a spatially aligned, persistent memory anchored by topological keypoints (e.g., junctions, room centers). SSMG clusters nearby entities into subgraphs, unifying entity- and space-level semantics to yield a compact set of candidate destinations. To support multimodal targets (images, objects, and text), we integrate a vision-language model (VLM). For each subgraph, a multimodal prompt synthesized from memory guides the VLM to infer a target belief over destinations. A long-horizon planner then trades off this belief against traversability costs to produce a visit sequence that minimizes expected path length, thereby reducing backtracking. Extensive experiments on challenging lifelong benchmarks and standard ObjectNav benchmarks demonstrate that, compared to strong baselines, our method achieves higher success rates and greater path efficiency, validating the effectiveness of SSMG-Nav.

摘要（中文）: 从人类指令在不熟悉环境中导航至视线外的目标是服务型机器人的核心能力。尽管取得了显著进展，但大多数方法未能充分利用可重用、持久的记忆，从而限制了在终身环境中的性能。此外，许多方法仅限于单模态输入，并采用短视的贪婪策略，后者经常导致低效的前后移动（BFMs）。为解决这些局限性，我们提出了SSMG-Nav，一个基于\textit{语义骨架记忆图}(SSMG)构建的目标导航框架。该图将过去的观察结果整合到一个空间对齐的持久记忆中，并由拓扑关键点（例如，交叉路口、房间中心）锚定。SSMG将附近的实体聚类成子图，统一实体和空间层面的语义，从而产生一组精简的候选目的地。为了支持多模态目标（图像、物体和文本），我们集成了一个视觉-语言模型（VLM）。对于每个子图，一个由记忆合成的多模态提示引导VLM推断出关于目的地的目标置信度。然后，一个长程规划器将该置信度与可通行性成本进行权衡，以产生一个访问序列，从而最小化预期路径长度，减少回溯。在具有挑战性的终身基准和标准 ObjectNav 基准上的大量实验表明，与强大的基线相比，我们的方法实现了更高的成功率和更高的路径效率，验证了SSMG-Nav的有效性。

TLDR: The paper introduces SSMG-Nav, a framework that uses a Semantic Skeleton Memory Graph (SSMG) to improve object navigation in lifelong settings by consolidating past observations and employing a vision-language model for multimodal target understanding and long-horizon planning to minimize backtracking.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为SSMG-Nav的框架，它使用语义骨架记忆图（SSMG）通过整合过去的观察结果并采用视觉语言模型进行多模态目标理解和长程规划来改进终身环境下的对象导航，从而减少回溯。

Authors: Haochen Niu, Lantao Zhang, Xingwu Ji, Rendong Ying, Peilin Liu, Fei Wen

Rethinking Policy Diversity in Ensemble Policy Gradient in Large-Scale Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): Scaling reinforcement learning to tens of thousands of parallel environments requires overcoming the limited exploration capacity of a single policy. Ensemble-based policy gradient methods, which employ multiple policies to collect diverse samples, have recently been proposed to promote exploration. However, merely broadening the exploration space does not always enhance learning capability, since excessive exploration can reduce exploration quality or compromise training stability. In this work, we theoretically analyze the impact of inter-policy diversity on learning efficiency in policy ensembles, and propose Coupled Policy Optimization which regulates diversity through KL constraints between policies. The proposed method enables effective exploration and outperforms strong baselines such as SAPG, PBT, and PPO across multiple tasks, including challenging dexterous manipulation, in terms of both sample efficiency and final performance. Furthermore, analysis of policy diversity and effective sample size during training reveals that follower policies naturally distribute around the leader, demonstrating the emergence of structured and efficient exploratory behavior. Our results indicate that diverse exploration under appropriate regulation is key to achieving stable and sample-efficient learning in ensemble policy gradient methods. Project page at https://naoki04.github.io/paper-cpo/ .

摘要（中文）: 将强化学习扩展到数万个并行环境需要克服单一策略的有限探索能力。基于集成策略梯度的方法，利用多个策略来收集多样化的样本，最近被提出以促进探索。然而，仅仅拓宽探索空间并不总是能增强学习能力，因为过度探索会降低探索质量或损害训练稳定性。在这项工作中，我们从理论上分析了策略间多样性对策略集成学习效率的影响，并提出了耦合策略优化（Coupled Policy Optimization，CPO），它通过策略间的KL散度约束来调节多样性。所提出的方法能够实现有效的探索，并在包括具有挑战性的灵巧操作在内的多个任务中，在样本效率和最终性能方面都优于强基线方法，如SAPG、PBT和PPO。此外，对训练期间策略多样性和有效样本量的分析表明，跟随者策略自然地分布在领导者策略周围，展示了结构化和高效的探索行为的出现。我们的结果表明，在适当的调节下进行多样化探索是实现集成策略梯度方法中稳定和样本高效学习的关键。项目主页：https://naoki04.github.io/paper-cpo/ 。

TLDR: This paper introduces Coupled Policy Optimization (CPO), a novel ensemble policy gradient method that regulates inter-policy diversity through KL constraints, achieving state-of-the-art performance in large-scale RL tasks, especially dexterous manipulation.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为耦合策略优化 (CPO) 的新型集成策略梯度方法，该方法通过 KL 约束来调节策略间的多样性，从而在大型 RL 任务（尤其是灵巧操作）中实现了最先进的性能。

Authors: Naoki Shitanda, Motoki Omura, Tatsuya Harada, Takayuki Osa

TacMamba: A Tactile History Compression Adapter Bridging Fast Reflexes and Slow VLA Reasoning

Abstract (English): In visually ambiguous manipulation such as detecting button click tactile feedback is often the sole source of ground truth. However, fusing tactile data poses a significant challenge due to a spatiotemporal mismatch: tactile perception requires high-frequency processing with long-horizon memory (System 1), whereas visual policies operate at low control frequencies (System 2). Existing architectures struggle to bridge this gap: Transformers are computationally prohibitive for high-frequency loops (>100Hz), while LSTMs suffer from forgetting over extended interaction histories. In this paper, we introduce TacMamba, a hierarchical architecture that aligns high-bandwidth tactile reflexes with low-frequency visual planning. Our approach comprises three core contributions: (1) a custom high-frequency tactile interface designed for flexible integration; (2) a Mamba-based Tactile History Compressor that encodes continuous force history into a compact state with O(1) inference latency (0.45 ms), enabling plug-and-play fusion with VLA models without joint pre-training and (3) a Tactile-Guided Dual-Stage Training strategy that leverages temporal discrimination for self-supervised representation learning and phase-uniform sampling to mitigate data sparsity. Experiments on discrete counting and implicit state switching demonstrate that TacMamba achieves 100% success rates, significantly outperforming the visual-only pi_0.5 baseline, while strictly satisfying hard real-time constraints.

摘要（中文）: 在视觉上存在歧义的操作中，例如按钮点击检测，触觉反馈往往是唯一的真值来源。然而，融合触觉数据面临着巨大的挑战，这是由于时空不匹配造成的：触觉感知需要高频处理和长时程记忆（系统1），而视觉策略则以低控制频率运行（系统2）。现有的架构难以弥合这一差距：Transformer 对于高频循环（>100Hz）而言计算量过大，而 LSTM 会受到长期交互历史中的遗忘问题困扰。在本文中，我们介绍了一种分层架构 TacMamba，它将高带宽的触觉反射与低频率的视觉规划对齐。我们的方法包含三个核心贡献：（1）为灵活集成而设计的定制高频触觉接口；（2）基于 Mamba 的触觉历史压缩器，可将连续的力历史编码为紧凑状态，并具有 O(1) 的推理延迟（0.45 毫秒），从而无需联合预训练即可与 VLA 模型进行即插即用融合；（3）一种触觉引导的双阶段训练策略，该策略利用时间判别进行自监督表征学习，并利用相位均匀采样来缓解数据稀疏性。离散计数和隐式状态切换的实验表明，TacMamba 实现了 100% 的成功率，显著优于仅使用视觉的 pi_0.5 基线，同时严格满足硬实时约束。

TLDR: TacMamba is a hierarchical architecture using Mamba to compress tactile sensor data into a compact state for real-time robot manipulation tasks, allowing for effective fusion with visual policies.

简要说明: TacMamba是一种分层架构，使用Mamba将触觉传感器数据压缩成紧凑状态，用于实时机器人操作任务，从而可以有效地与视觉策略融合。

Authors: Zhenan Wang, Yanzhe Wang, Meixuan Ren, Peng Li, Yang Liu, Yifei Nie, Limin Long, Yun Ye, Xiaofeng Wang, Zhen Zhu, Huixu Dong

KERV: Kinematic-Rectified Speculative Decoding for Embodied VLA Models

Abstract (English): Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models build a token-domain robot control paradigm, yet suffer from low speed. Speculative Decoding (SD) is an optimization strategy that can boost inference speed. Two key issues emerge when integrating VLA and SD: first, SD relies on re-inference to address token errors, which is computationally expensive; second, to mitigate token errors, the acceptance threshold in SD requires careful adjustment. Existing works fail to address the above two issues effectively. Meanwhile, as the bridge between AI and the physical world, existing embodied intelligence has overlooked the application of robotic kinematics. To address these issues, we innovatively combine token-domain VLA models with kinematic-domain prediction for SD, proposing a kinematic-rectified SD framework named KERV. We employ a kinematics-based Kalman Filter to predict actions and compensate for SD errors, avoiding costly re-inference. Moreover, we design a kinematics-based adjustment strategy to dynamically rectify the acceptance threshold, addressing the difficulty of threshold determination. Experimental results across diverse tasks and environments demonstrate that KERV achieves 27%~37% acceleration with nearly no Success Rate loss.

摘要（中文）: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型构建了一种基于令牌域的机器人控制范式，但存在速度低下的问题。推测性解码（SD）是一种可以提高推理速度的优化策略。将VLA和SD集成时，会出现两个关键问题：首先，SD依赖于重新推理来解决令牌错误，这在计算上是昂贵的；其次，为了缓解令牌错误，SD中的接受阈值需要仔细调整。现有工作未能有效解决上述两个问题。与此同时，作为人工智能与物理世界之间的桥梁，现有具身智能忽略了机器人运动学的应用。为了解决这些问题，我们创新性地将令牌域VLA模型与运动学域预测相结合，用于推测性解码，提出了一个名为KERV的运动学校正SD框架。我们采用基于运动学的卡尔曼滤波器来预测动作并补偿SD错误，从而避免了昂贵的重新推理。此外，我们设计了一种基于运动学的调整策略来动态校正接受阈值，解决了阈值确定的困难。在各种任务和环境中的实验结果表明，KERV实现了27%~37%的加速，且几乎没有成功率损失。

TLDR: The paper introduces KERV, a kinematic-rectified speculative decoding framework for Vision-Language-Action models, leveraging kinematics-based prediction and Kalman filtering to accelerate inference and dynamically adjust acceptance thresholds in speculative decoding, achieving 27-37% speedup with minimal performance loss.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了KERV，一个用于视觉-语言-动作模型的运动学校正推测解码框架。它利用基于运动学的预测和卡尔曼滤波来加速推理，并动态调整推测解码中的接受阈值，在几乎没有性能损失的情况下实现了27-37%的加速。

Authors: Zihao Zheng, Zhihao Mao, Maoliang Li, Jiayu Chen, Xinhao Sun, Zhaobo Zhang, Donggang Cao, Hong Mei, Xiang Chen

RoboGPU: Accelerating GPU Collision Detection for Robotics

Abstract (English): Autonomous robots are increasingly prevalent in our society, emerging in medical care, transportation vehicles, and home assistance. These robots rely on motion planning and collision detection to identify a sequence of movements allowing them to navigate to an end goal without colliding with the surrounding environment. While many specialized accelerators have been proposed to meet the real-time requirements of robotics planning tasks, they often lack the flexibility to adapt to the rapidly changing landscape of robotics and support future advancements. However, GPUs are well-positioned for robotics and we find that they can also tackle collision detection algorithms with enhancements to existing ray tracing accelerator (RTA) units. Unlike intersection tests in ray tracing, collision queries in robotics require control flow mechanisms to avoid unnecessary computations in each query. In this work, we explore and compare different architectural modifications to address the gaps of existing GPU RTAs. Our proposed RoboGPU architecture introduces a RoboCore that computes collision queries 3.1$\times$ faster than RTA implementations and 14.8$\times$ faster than a CUDA baseline. RoboCore is also useful for other robotics tasks, achieving 3.6$\times$ speedup on a state-of-the-art neural motion planner and 1.1$\times$ speedup on Monte Carlo Localization compared to a baseline GPU. RoboGPU matches the performance of dedicated hardware accelerators while being able to adapt to evolving motion planning algorithms and support classical algorithms.

摘要（中文）: 自动机器人在我们的社会中日益普及，出现在医疗保健、运输工具和家庭助理等领域。这些机器人依赖于运动规划和碰撞检测来识别一系列动作，使其能够在不与周围环境发生碰撞的情况下导航至最终目标。虽然已经提出了许多专用加速器来满足机器人规划任务的实时性要求，但它们往往缺乏适应快速变化的机器人领域和支持未来发展的灵活性。然而，GPU在机器人技术领域具有优势，并且我们发现，通过对现有光线追踪加速器 (RTA) 单元的增强，它们也可以处理碰撞检测算法。与光线追踪中的相交测试不同，机器人技术中的碰撞查询需要控制流机制，以避免每次查询中不必要的计算。在这项工作中，我们探索并比较了不同的架构修改方案，以弥补现有 GPU RTA 的不足。我们提出的 RoboGPU 架构引入了一个 RoboCore，与 RTA 实现相比，其碰撞查询计算速度提高了 3.1 倍，与 CUDA 基线相比提高了 14.8 倍。 RoboCore 也适用于其他机器人任务，与基线 GPU 相比，在最先进的神经运动规划器上实现了 3.6 倍的加速，在蒙特卡洛定位上实现了 1.1 倍的加速。RoboGPU 在能够适应不断发展的运动规划算法和支持经典算法的同时，达到了专用硬件加速器的性能。

TLDR: The paper introduces RoboGPU, a GPU architecture with a novel RoboCore, to accelerate collision detection and other robotics tasks, achieving significant speedups over existing GPU and CUDA baselines while maintaining flexibility.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为 RoboGPU 的 GPU 架构，该架构具有新型 RoboCore，旨在加速碰撞检测和其他机器人任务，在保持灵活性的同时，与现有 GPU 和 CUDA 基线相比实现了显著的加速。

Authors: Lufei Liu, Liwei Xue, Youssef Mohammed, Jocelyn Zhao, Yuan Hsi Chou, Tor M. Aamodt

FATE: Closed-Loop Feasibility-Aware Task Generation with Active Repair for Physically Grounded Robotic Curricula

Abstract (English): Recent breakthroughs in generative simulation have harnessed Large Language Models (LLMs) to generate diverse robotic task curricula, yet these open-loop paradigms frequently produce linguistically coherent but physically infeasible goals, stemming from ungrounded task specifications or misaligned objective formulations. To address this critical limitation, we propose FATE (Feasibility-Aware Task gEneration), a closed-loop, self-correcting framework that reimagines task generation as an iterative validation-and-refinement process. Unlike conventional methods that decouple generation and verification into discrete stages, FATE embeds a generalist embodied agent directly into the generation loop to proactively guarantee the physical groundedness of the resulting curriculum. FATE instantiates a sequential auditing pipeline: it first validates static scene attributes (e.g., object affordances, layout compatibility) and subsequently verifies execution feasibility via simulated embodied interaction. Critical to its performance, upon detecting an infeasible task, FATE deploys an active repair module that autonomously adapts scene configurations or policy specifications, converting unworkable proposals into physically valid task instances. Extensive experiments validate that FATE generates semantically diverse, physically grounded task curricula while achieving a substantial reduction in execution failure rates relative to state-of-the-art generative baselines.

摘要（中文）: 生成式模拟技术的最新突破利用大型语言模型（LLMs）生成多种多样的机器人任务课程，然而，这些开环范式常常会产生语言上连贯但物理上不可行的目标，这源于缺乏依据的任务规范或目标公式的错位。为了解决这一关键限制，我们提出了FATE（可行性感知任务生成），一个闭环、自我修正的框架，它将任务生成重新构想为一个迭代的验证与改进过程。与将生成和验证解耦为离散阶段的传统方法不同，FATE将通用的具身智能体直接嵌入到生成循环中，以主动保证所生成课程在物理上的可靠性。FATE实例化了一个顺序审计流程：它首先验证静态场景属性（例如，对象的可供性、布局兼容性），然后通过模拟的具身交互来验证执行可行性。对其性能至关重要的是，在检测到不可行的任务时，FATE会部署一个主动修复模块，该模块可自主调整场景配置或策略规范，将不可行的提议转换为物理上有效的任务实例。大量的实验验证了FATE能够生成语义多样、物理可靠的任务课程，同时相对于最先进的生成基线，显著降低了执行失败率。

TLDR: The paper introduces FATE, a closed-loop framework that uses a generalist embodied agent to generate physically feasible robotic task curricula by iteratively validating and actively repairing infeasible task proposals.

简要说明: 该论文提出了FATE，一个闭环框架，使用一个通用具身智能体来生成物理上可行的机器人任务课程，通过迭代验证和主动修复不可行的任务提议。

Authors: Bingchuan Wei, Bingqi Huang, Jingheng Ma, Zeyu zhang, Sen Cui

Mean-Flow based One-Step Vision-Language-Action

Abstract (English): Recent advances in FlowMatching-based Vision-Language-Action (VLA) frameworks have demonstrated remarkable advantages in generating high-frequency action chunks, particularly for highly dexterous robotic manipulation tasks. Despite these notable achievements, their practical applications are constrained by prolonged generation latency, which stems from inherent iterative sampling requirements and architectural limitations. To address this critical bottleneck, we propose a Mean-Flow based One-Step VLA approach. Specifically, we resolve the noise-induced issues in the action generation process, thereby eliminating the consistency constraints inherent to conventional Flow-Matching methods. This significantly enhances generation efficiency and enables one-step action generation. Real-world robotic experiments show that the generation speed of the proposed Mean-Flow based One-Step VLA is 8.7 times and 83.9 times faster than that of SmolVLA and Diffusion Policy, respectively. These results elucidate its great potential as a high-efficiency backbone for VLA-based robotic manipulation.

摘要（中文）: 基于FlowMatching的视觉-语言-动作(VLA)框架的最新进展已在生成高频动作块方面展示了显著优势，尤其是在高度灵巧的机器人操作任务中。 尽管取得了这些显著成就，但其实际应用仍受到长时间生成延迟的限制，这源于固有的迭代采样需求和架构限制。 为了解决这个关键瓶颈，我们提出了一种基于平均流的单步VLA方法。 具体来说，我们解决了动作生成过程中噪声引起的问题，从而消除了传统FlowMatching方法固有的连贯性约束。这显著提高了生成效率，并实现了单步动作生成。 真实的机器人实验表明，所提出的基于平均流的单步VLA的生成速度分别是SmolVLA和Diffusion Policy的8.7倍和83.9倍。 这些结果阐明了其作为基于VLA的机器人操作的高效主干网络的巨大潜力。

TLDR: The paper introduces a Mean-Flow based One-Step Vision-Language-Action (VLA) approach to significantly improve the generation speed of actions for robotic manipulation, achieving substantial speedups compared to existing methods.

Authors: Yang Chen, Xiaoguang Ma, Bin Zhao

Non-Markovian Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation via Keyframe Chaining

Abstract (English): Existing Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models often struggle to generalize to long-horizon tasks due to their heavy reliance on immediate observations. While recent studies incorporate retrieval mechanisms or extend context windows to handle procedural tasks, they often struggle to capture Non-Markovian dependencies, where optimal actions rely solely on specific past states rather than the current observation. To address this, we introduce Keyframe-Chaining VLA, a framework that extracts and links key historical frames to model long-horizon dependencies. Specifically, we propose an automatic keyframe selector that learns a discriminative embedding space, effectively identifying distinct state transitions. To capture task-critical information, we design a progress-aware query mechanism that dynamically retrieves historical frames based on their temporal relevance to the current execution phase. These selected keyframes are integrated into the VLA as interleaved visual tokens, explicitly grounding the policy in the long-horizon temporal context. Finally, we introduce a suite of four Non-Markovian manipulation tasks built upon the ManiSkill simulator to measure task success rates. Experimental results demonstrate that our method achieves superior performance, effectively tackling robot manipulation tasks characterized by long-horizon temporal dependencies. Code is available at https://github.com/cytoplastm/KC-VLA.

摘要（中文）: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型通常难以泛化到长时程任务中，因为它们过度依赖即时观测。虽然最近的研究整合了检索机制或扩展上下文窗口来处理程序性任务，但它们通常难以捕捉非马尔可夫依赖关系，在这种关系中，最佳动作仅依赖于特定的过去状态，而非当前的观测。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了关键帧链接VLA，一个提取并链接关键历史帧以建模长时程依赖关系的框架。具体而言，我们提出了一个自动关键帧选择器，该选择器学习判别性嵌入空间，有效地识别不同的状态转换。为了捕捉任务关键信息，我们设计了一种进度感知查询机制，该机制基于历史帧与当前执行阶段的时间相关性动态地检索历史帧。这些选定的关键帧作为交错的视觉令牌集成到VLA中，明确地将策略建立在长时程时间上下文中。最后，我们引入了一套基于ManiSkill模拟器的四个非马尔可夫操作任务，以测量任务成功率。实验结果表明，我们的方法实现了卓越的性能，有效地解决了以长时程时间依赖关系为特征的机器人操作任务。代码可在https://github.com/cytoplastm/KC-VLA获取。

TLDR: The paper introduces Keyframe-Chaining VLA, a framework that utilizes key historical frames to address the limitations of VLA models in long-horizon, Non-Markovian robot manipulation tasks, demonstrating superior performance on a new suite of such tasks.

Authors: Yipeng Chen, Wentao Tan, Lei Zhu, Fengling Li, Jingjing Li, Guoli Yang, Heng Tao Shen

Scaling Tasks, Not Samples: Mastering Humanoid Control through Multi-Task Model-Based Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): Developing generalist robots capable of mastering diverse skills remains a central challenge in embodied AI. While recent progress emphasizes scaling model parameters and offline datasets, such approaches are limited in robotics, where learning requires active interaction. We argue that effective online learning should scale the \emph{number of tasks}, rather than the number of samples per task. This regime reveals a structural advantage of model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL). Because physical dynamics are invariant across tasks, a shared world model can aggregate multi-task experience to learn robust, task-agnostic representations. In contrast, model-free methods suffer from gradient interference when tasks demand conflicting actions in similar states. Task diversity therefore acts as a regularizer for MBRL, improving dynamics learning and sample efficiency. We instantiate this idea with \textbf{EfficientZero-Multitask (EZ-M)}, a sample-efficient multi-task MBRL algorithm for online learning. Evaluated on \textbf{HumanoidBench}, a challenging whole-body control benchmark, EZ-M achieves state-of-the-art performance with significantly higher sample efficiency than strong baselines, without extreme parameter scaling. These results establish task scaling as a critical axis for scalable robotic learning. The project website is available \href{https://yewr.github.io/ez_m/}{here}.

摘要（中文）: 开发能够掌握多种技能的通用机器人仍然是具身人工智能的核心挑战。虽然最近的进展强调了模型参数和离线数据集的扩展，但这种方法在机器人领域受到限制，因为在机器人领域学习需要主动交互。我们认为，有效的在线学习应该扩展*任务数量*，而不是每个任务的样本数量。这种模式揭示了基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）的结构性优势。由于物理动力学在各个任务中是不变的，因此共享的世界模型可以聚合多任务经验，从而学习到鲁棒的、任务无关的表示。相反，当任务需要在相似状态下采取冲突行动时，无模型方法会受到梯度干扰的影响。因此，任务多样性充当了MBRL的正则化器，提高了动力学学习和样本效率。我们将这个想法实例化为\textbf{高效零-多任务（EZ-M）}，这是一种用于在线学习的、具有样本高效性的多任务MBRL算法。在具有挑战性的全身控制基准测试\textbf{HumanoidBench}上进行评估，EZ-M实现了最先进的性能，且样本效率远高于强大的基线，而无需极端的参数扩展。这些结果确立了任务扩展作为可扩展机器人学习的关键维度。项目网站请访问\href{https://yewr.github.io/ez_m/}{此处}。

TLDR: The paper introduces EfficientZero-Multitask (EZ-M), a multi-task model-based reinforcement learning algorithm that achieves state-of-the-art performance and improved sample efficiency on the HumanoidBench benchmark by scaling the number of tasks rather than samples.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种多任务的基于模型的强化学习算法 EfficientZero-Multitask (EZ-M)，通过扩展任务数量而非样本数量，在 HumanoidBench 基准测试中实现了最先进的性能和更高的样本效率。

Authors: Shaohuai Liu, Weirui Ye, Yilun Du, Le Xie

PhysGraph: Physically-Grounded Graph-Transformer Policies for Bimanual Dexterous Hand-Tool-Object Manipulation

Abstract (English): Bimanual dexterous manipulation for tool use remains a formidable challenge in robotics due to the high-dimensional state space and complicated contact dynamics. Existing methods naively represent the entire system state as a single configuration vector, disregarding the rich structural and topological information inherent to articulated hands. We present PhysGraph, a physically-grounded graph transformer policy designed explicitly for challenging bimanual hand-tool-object manipulation. Unlike prior works, we represent the bimanual system as a kinematic graph and introduce per-link tokenization to preserve fine-grained local state information. We propose a physically-grounded bias generator that injects structural priors directly into the attention mechanism, including kinematic spatial distance, dynamic contact states, geometric proximity, and anatomical properties. This allows the policy to explicitly reason about physical interactions rather than learning them implicitly from sparse rewards. Extensive experiments show that PhysGraph significantly outperforms baseline - ManipTrans in manipulation precision and task success rates while using only 51% of the parameters of ManipTrans. Furthermore, the inherent topological flexibility of our architecture shows qualitative zero-shot transfer to unseen tool/object geometries, and is sufficiently general to be trained on three robotic hands (Shadow, Allegro, Inspire).

摘要（中文）: 以下是翻译结果： 用于工具使用的双臂灵巧操作因其高维状态空间和复杂接触动力学而仍然是机器人领域的一项巨大挑战。现有方法通常将整个系统状态简单地表示为一个单一配置向量，忽略了铰接手固有的丰富结构和拓扑信息。我们提出 PhysGraph，一种用于具身双臂手-工具-物体操作的、基于物理的图变换器策略。与以往工作不同，我们将双臂系统表示为一个运动学图，并引入了按链接tokenization以保留细粒度的局部状态信息。我们提出了一种基于物理的偏置生成器，将结构先验直接注入到注意力机制中，包括运动学空间距离、动态接触状态、几何邻近度和解剖学属性。这使得策略能够显式地推理物理交互，而不是从稀疏奖励中隐式地学习它们。大量实验表明，PhysGraph 在操作精度和任务成功率方面显著优于基线 ManipTrans，同时仅使用了 ManipTrans 51% 的参数。此外，我们架构固有的拓扑灵活性展示了向未见过的工具/物体几何结构的定性零样本迁移，并且具有足够的通用性，可以训练在三种不同的机器人手上（Shadow, Allegro, Inspire）。

TLDR: The paper introduces PhysGraph, a graph transformer policy for bimanual robotic manipulation that incorporates physical priors into the attention mechanism, achieving higher manipulation precision and zero-shot transferability with fewer parameters.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了 PhysGraph，一种用于双臂机器人操作的图转换器策略，它将物理先验知识融入到注意力机制中，以更少的参数实现了更高的操作精度和零样本迁移能力。

Authors: Runfa Blark Li, David Kim, Xinshuang Liu, Keito Suzuki, Dwait Bhatt, Nikola Raicevic, Xin Lin, Ki Myung Brian Lee, Nikolay Atanasov, Truong Nguyen

Jailbreaking Embodied LLMs via Action-level Manipulation

Abstract (English): Embodied Large Language Models (LLMs) enable AI agents to interact with the physical world through natural language instructions and actions. However, beyond the language-level risks inherent to LLMs themselves, embodied LLMs with real-world actuation introduce a new vulnerability: instructions that appear semantically benign may still lead to dangerous real-world consequences, revealing a fundamental misalignment between linguistic security and physical outcomes. In this paper, we introduce Blindfold, an automated attack framework that leverages the limited causal reasoning capabilities of embodied LLMs in real-world action contexts. Rather than iterative trial-and-error jailbreaking of black-box embodied LLMs, Blindfold adopts an Adversarial Proxy Planning strategy: it compromises a local surrogate LLM to perform action-level manipulations that appear semantically safe but could result in harmful physical effects when executed. Blindfold further conceals key malicious actions by injecting carefully crafted noise to evade detection by defense mechanisms, and it incorporates a rule-based verifier to improve the attack executability. Evaluations on both embodied AI simulators and a real-world 6DoF robotic arm show that Blindfold achieves up to 53% higher attack success rates than SOTA baselines, highlighting the urgent need to move beyond surface-level language censorship and toward consequence-aware defense mechanisms to secure embodied LLMs.

摘要（中文）: 具身大语言模型(LLMs)使人工智能代理能够通过自然语言指令和动作与物理世界互动。然而，除了LLMs本身固有的语言层面的风险之外，具有真实世界驱动能力的具身LLMs引入了一种新的脆弱性：表面上语义良性的指令仍然可能导致危险的现实世界后果，从而揭示了语言安全和物理结果之间根本的不一致。在本文中，我们引入了Blindfold，一种自动化攻击框架，它利用具身LLMs在真实世界行动环境中有限的因果推理能力。Blindfold并非采用对黑盒具身LLMs进行迭代试错式的越狱攻击，而是采用了一种对抗代理规划策略：它破坏了一个本地代理LLM，使其执行表面上语义安全但在执行时可能导致有害物理效果的动作级别的操纵。Blindfold通过注入精心设计的噪声来进一步隐藏关键的恶意行为，从而逃避防御机制的检测，并且它结合了一个基于规则的验证器来提高攻击的可执行性。在具身人工智能仿真器和真实世界的六自由度机械臂上的评估表明，Blindfold的攻击成功率比SOTA基线高出高达53%，这突显了迫切需要超越表面层面的语言审查，并转向具有后果意识的防御机制，以确保具身LLMs的安全。

TLDR: This paper introduces Blindfold, an attack framework that exploits vulnerabilities in embodied LLMs by generating semantically benign but physically harmful action sequences, achieving higher attack success rates compared to existing methods.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一个名为Blindfold的攻击框架，它通过生成语义上无害但物理上具有危害性的动作序列来利用具身LLM中的漏洞，与现有方法相比，攻击成功率更高。

Authors: Xinyu Huang, Qiang Yang, Leming Shen, Zijing Ma, Yuanqing Zheng

Hybrid TD3: Overestimation Bias Analysis and Stable Policy Optimization for Hybrid Action Space

Abstract (English): Reinforcement learning in discrete-continuous hybrid action spaces presents fundamental challenges for robotic manipulation, where high-level task decisions and low-level joint-space execution must be jointly optimized. Existing approaches either discretize continuous components or relax discrete choices into continuous approximations, which suffer from scalability limitations and training instability in high-dimensional action spaces and under domain randomization. In this paper, we propose Hybrid TD3, an extension of Twin Delayed Deep Deterministic Policy Gradient (TD3) that natively handles parameterized hybrid action spaces in a principled manner. We conduct a rigorous theoretical analysis of overestimation bias in hybrid action settings, deriving formal bounds under twin-critic architectures and establishing a complete bias ordering across five algorithmic variants. Building on this analysis, we introduce a weighted clipped Q-learning target that marginalizes over the discrete action distribution, achieving equivalent bias reduction to standard clipped minimization while improving policy smoothness. Experimental results demonstrate that Hybrid TD3 achieves superior training stability and competitive performance against state-of-the-art hybrid action baselines

摘要（中文）: 离散-连续混合动作空间中的强化学习为机器人操作带来了根本性挑战，其中高层任务决策和低层关节空间执行必须联合优化。现有方法要么离散化连续分量，要么将离散选择放宽到连续近似，这在高维动作空间和领域随机化下存在可扩展性限制和训练不稳定。在本文中，我们提出了Hybrid TD3，它是双延迟深度确定性策略梯度 (TD3) 的扩展，能够以合理的方式原生处理参数化混合动作空间。我们对混合动作设置中的过度估计偏差进行了严格的理论分析，推导了双Critic架构下的形式边界，并确定了五种算法变体之间的完整偏差排序。在此分析的基础上，我们引入了一种加权裁剪Q学习目标，它可以对离散动作分布进行边缘化处理，从而实现与标准裁剪最小化相当的偏差减少，同时提高策略的平滑性。实验结果表明，Hybrid TD3 实现了优越的训练稳定性，并与最先进的混合动作基线相比具有竞争优势。

TLDR: The paper introduces Hybrid TD3, an extension of TD3 that natively handles parameterized hybrid action spaces in reinforcement learning, providing a theoretical analysis of overestimation bias and a novel weighted clipped Q-learning target.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了 Hybrid TD3，它是TD3的扩展，可以原生处理强化学习中参数化的混合动作空间，并提供了对过度估计偏差的理论分析以及一种新的加权裁剪Q学习目标。

Authors: Thanh-Tuan Tran, Thanh Nguyen Canh, Nak Young Chong, Xiem HoangVan

Integrating LTL Constraints into PPO for Safe Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): This paper proposes Proximal Policy Optimization with Linear Temporal Logic Constraints (PPO-LTL), a framework that integrates safety constraints written in LTL into PPO for safe reinforcement learning. LTL constraints offer rigorous representations of complex safety requirements, such as regulations that broadly exist in robotics, enabling systematic monitoring of safety requirements. Violations against LTL constraints are monitored by limit-deterministic Büchi automata, and then translated by a logic-to-cost mechanism into penalty signals. The signals are further employed for guiding the policy optimization via the Lagrangian scheme. Extensive experiments on the Zones and CARLA environments show that our PPO-LTL can consistently reduce safety violations, while maintaining competitive performance, against the state-of-the-art methods. The code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/PPO-LTL.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出了一种带有线性时序逻辑约束的近端策略优化（PPO-LTL）框架，该框架将以LTL编写的安全约束集成到PPO中，以实现安全强化学习。LTL约束为复杂的安全需求提供了严格的表示，例如广泛存在于机器人技术中的法规，从而能够系统地监控安全要求。违反LTL约束的情况由极限确定性比奇自动机监控，并通过一种逻辑到成本的机制转化为惩罚信号。这些信号进一步被用于通过拉格朗日方案引导策略优化。在Zones和CARLA环境中的大量实验表明，与最先进的方法相比，我们的PPO-LTL能够持续减少安全违规，同时保持有竞争力的性能。代码可在https://github.com/EVIEHub/PPO-LTL 获取。

TLDR: The paper introduces PPO-LTL, a safe reinforcement learning framework that integrates Linear Temporal Logic constraints into PPO using a logic-to-cost mechanism for penalty signals, demonstrating reduced safety violations in robotic environments.

RMBench: Memory-Dependent Robotic Manipulation Benchmark with Insights into Policy Design

Abstract (English): Robotic manipulation policies have made rapid progress in recent years, yet most existing approaches give limited consideration to memory capabilities. Consequently, they struggle to solve tasks that require reasoning over historical observations and maintaining task-relevant information over time, which are common requirements in real-world manipulation scenarios. Although several memory-aware policies have been proposed, systematic evaluation of memory-dependent manipulation remains underexplored, and the relationship between architectural design choices and memory performance is still not well understood. To address this gap, we introduce RMBench, a simulation benchmark comprising 9 manipulation tasks that span multiple levels of memory complexity, enabling systematic evaluation of policy memory capabilities. We further propose Mem-0, a modular manipulation policy with explicit memory components designed to support controlled ablation studies. Through extensive simulation and real-world experiments, we identify memory-related limitations in existing policies and provide empirical insights into how architectural design choices influence memory performance. The website is available at https://rmbench.github.io/.

摘要（中文）: 近年来，机器人操作策略取得了快速进展，但大多数现有方法对记忆能力的考虑有限。因此，它们难以解决需要在历史观测数据上进行推理以及随着时间推移保持任务相关信息的任务，而这些在现实世界的操作场景中是常见的需求。尽管已经提出了几种具有记忆功能的策略，但对依赖记忆的操作的系统评估仍然不足，并且架构设计选择与记忆性能之间的关系仍然没有被很好地理解。为了解决这一差距，我们引入了RMBench，这是一个包含9个操作任务的模拟基准，这些任务涵盖了多个级别的记忆复杂度，能够对策略的记忆能力进行系统评估。我们进一步提出了Mem-0，一种具有显式记忆组件的模块化操作策略，旨在支持可控消融研究。通过广泛的模拟和真实世界实验，我们识别了现有策略中与记忆相关的局限性，并提供了关于架构设计选择如何影响记忆性能的经验性见解。该网站可在https://rmbench.github.io/ 访问。

TLDR: The paper introduces RMBench, a simulation benchmark for evaluating memory-dependent robotic manipulation policies, and Mem-0, a modular policy for ablation studies, exploring the impact of architectural choices on memory performance in robotic tasks.

Monocular 3D Object Position Estimation with VLMs for Human-Robot Interaction

Abstract (English): Pre-trained general-purpose Vision-Language Models (VLM) hold the potential to enhance intuitive human-machine interactions due to their rich world knowledge and 2D object detection capabilities. However, VLMs for 3D coordinates detection tasks are rare. In this work, we investigate interactive abilities of VLMs by returning 3D object positions given a monocular RGB image from a wrist-mounted camera, natural language input, and robot states. We collected and curated a heterogeneous dataset of more than 100,000 images and finetuned a VLM using QLoRA with a custom regression head. By implementing conditional routing, our model maintains its ability to process general visual queries while adding specialized 3D position estimation capabilities. Our results demonstrate robust predictive performance with a median MAE of 13 mm on the test set and a five-fold improvement over a simpler baseline without finetuning. In about 25% of the cases, predictions are within a range considered acceptable for the robot to interact with objects.

摘要（中文）: 预训练的通用视觉-语言模型（VLM）由于其丰富的世界知识和2D物体检测能力，具有增强直观人机交互的潜力。然而，用于3D坐标检测任务的VLM非常罕见。在这项工作中，我们通过给定来自腕戴式相机的单目RGB图像、自然语言输入和机器人状态，返回3D物体位置，来研究VLM的交互能力。我们收集并整理了一个包含超过10万张图像的异构数据集，并使用QLoRA和自定义回归头对VLM进行了微调。通过实施条件路由，我们的模型在增加专门的3D位置估计能力的同时，保持了处理通用视觉查询的能力。我们的结果表明，在测试集上，我们的模型展现了强大的预测性能，中位数MAE为13毫米，并且比未经微调的简单基线模型提高了五倍。在大约25%的情况下，预测结果在机器人可接受的物体交互范围内。

TLDR: This paper presents a method for monocular 3D object position estimation using a finetuned Vision-Language Model (VLM), achieving promising results for human-robot interaction (HRI) with a custom dataset and conditional routing.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种使用微调的视觉语言模型 (VLM) 进行单目 3D 物体位置估计的方法，通过自定义数据集和条件路由在人机交互 (HRI) 方面取得了可喜的成果。

Authors: Ari Wahl, Dorian Gawlinski, David Przewozny, Paul Chojecki, Felix Bießmann, Sebastian Bosse

Exploring Plan Space through Conversation: An Agentic Framework for LLM-Mediated Explanations in Planning

Abstract (English): When automating plan generation for a real-world sequential decision problem, the goal is often not to replace the human planner, but to facilitate an iterative reasoning and elicitation process, where the human's role is to guide the AI planner according to their preferences and expertise. In this context, explanations that respond to users' questions are crucial to improve their understanding of potential solutions and increase their trust in the system. To enable natural interaction with such a system, we present a multi-agent Large Language Model (LLM) architecture that is agnostic to the explanation framework and enables user- and context-dependent interactive explanations. We also describe an instantiation of this framework for goal-conflict explanations, which we use to conduct a user study comparing the LLM-powered interaction with a baseline template-based explanation interface.

摘要（中文）: 在为现实世界的序贯决策问题自动化规划生成时，目标通常不是取代人类规划者，而是促进一个迭代推理和启发过程，人类的角色是根据他们的偏好和专业知识来指导AI规划器。在这种背景下，响应用户问题的解释对于增进他们对潜在解决方案的理解并提高他们对系统的信任至关重要。为了实现与此类系统的自然交互，我们提出了一种与解释框架无关的多Agent大型语言模型(LLM)架构，该架构支持用户和上下文相关的交互式解释。我们还描述了该框架在目标冲突解释方面的实例化，并使用它进行了一项用户研究，对LLM驱动的交互与基于模板的基线解释界面进行了比较。

Authors: Guilhem Fouilhé, Rebecca Eifler, Antonin Poché, Sylvie Thiébaux, Nicholas Asher

Temporal Representations for Exploration: Learning Complex Exploratory Behavior without Extrinsic Rewards

Abstract (English): Effective exploration in reinforcement learning requires not only tracking where an agent has been, but also understanding how the agent perceives and represents the world. To learn powerful representations, an agent should actively explore states that contribute to its knowledge of the environment. Temporal representations can capture the information necessary to solve a wide range of potential tasks while avoiding the computational cost associated with full state reconstruction. In this paper, we propose an exploration method that leverages temporal contrastive representations to guide exploration, prioritizing states with unpredictable future outcomes. We demonstrate that such representations can enable the learning of complex exploratory x in locomotion, manipulation, and embodied-AI tasks, revealing capabilities and behaviors that traditionally require extrinsic rewards. Unlike approaches that rely on explicit distance learning or episodic memory mechanisms (e.g., quasimetric-based methods), our method builds directly on temporal similarities, yielding a simpler yet effective strategy for exploration.

摘要（中文）: 强化学习中有效的探索不仅需要追踪智能体去过的地方，还需要理解智能体如何感知和表示世界。为了学习强大的表征，智能体应主动探索有助于其环境知识的状态。时间表征能够捕捉解决各种潜在任务所需的信息，同时避免与完全状态重构相关的计算成本。在本文中，我们提出了一种利用时间对比表征来引导探索的探索方法，优先考虑具有不可预测未来结果的状态。我们证明，这种表征能够实现对运动、操控和具身人工智能任务中复杂探索动作的学习，揭示了传统上需要外在奖励才能实现的能力和行为。与依赖于显式距离学习或情景记忆机制（例如，基于准度量的方法）的方法不同，我们的方法直接构建于时间相似性之上，从而产生了一种更简单但有效的探索策略。

TLDR: This paper introduces a novel reinforcement learning exploration method using temporal contrastive representations to guide agents toward states with unpredictable futures, achieving complex exploratory behaviors without relying on explicit rewards.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种新的强化学习探索方法，该方法利用时间对比表示来引导智能体探索具有不可预测未来的状态，从而在不依赖外部奖励的情况下实现复杂的探索行为。

Authors: Faisal Mohamed, Catherine Ji, Benjamin Eysenbach, Glen Berseth

LiveCultureBench: a Multi-Agent, Multi-Cultural Benchmark for Large Language Models in Dynamic Social Simulations

Abstract (English): Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents, yet evaluations focus primarily on task success rather than cultural appropriateness or evaluator reliability. We introduce LiveCultureBench, a multi-cultural, dynamic benchmark that embeds LLMs as agents in a simulated town and evaluates them on both task completion and adherence to socio-cultural norms. The simulation models a small city as a location graph with synthetic residents having diverse demographic and cultural profiles. Each episode assigns one resident a daily goal while others provide social context. An LLM-based verifier generates structured judgments on norm violations and task progress, which we aggregate into metrics capturing task-norm trade-offs and verifier uncertainty. Using LiveCultureBench across models and cultural profiles, we study (i) cross-cultural robustness of LLM agents, (ii) how they balance effectiveness against norm sensitivity, and (iii) when LLM-as-a-judge evaluation is reliable for automated benchmarking versus when human oversight is needed.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型（LLM）正日益被部署为自主智能体，然而评估主要集中在任务成功率上，而忽略了文化适宜性或评估者可靠性。我们推出了 LiveCultureBench，这是一个多文化、动态的基准测试，它将 LLM 作为智能体嵌入到一个模拟城镇中，并根据任务完成情况和社会文化规范的遵守情况来评估它们。该模拟将一个小城市建模为一个位置图，其中包含具有不同人口统计和文化特征的合成居民。每个任务都为一个居民分配一个日常目标，而其他人则提供社会背景。一个基于 LLM 的验证器生成关于规范违反和任务进度的结构化判断，我们将其聚合成指标，以捕捉任务-规范权衡和验证器不确定性。通过在不同的模型和文化背景下使用 LiveCultureBench，我们研究了（i）LLM 智能体的跨文化稳健性，（ii）它们如何在有效性和规范敏感性之间取得平衡，以及（iii）何时 LLM 作为评判者的评估对于自动化基准测试是可靠的，以及何时需要人工监督。

TLDR: The paper introduces LiveCultureBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLM agents in dynamic social simulations, focusing on task completion and adherence to socio-cultural norms, and analyzes cross-cultural robustness and evaluation reliability.

Authors: Viet-Thanh Pham, Lizhen Qu, Thuy-Trang Vu, Gholamreza Haffari, Dinh Phung

CoVe: Training Interactive Tool-Use Agents via Constraint-Guided Verification

Abstract (English): Developing multi-turn interactive tool-use agents is challenging because real-world user needs are often complex and ambiguous, yet agents must execute deterministic actions to satisfy them. To address this gap, we introduce \textbf{CoVe} (\textbf{Co}nstraint-\textbf{Ve}rification), a post-training data synthesis framework designed for training interactive tool-use agents while ensuring both data complexity and correctness. CoVe begins by defining explicit task constraints, which serve a dual role: they guide the generation of complex trajectories and act as deterministic verifiers for assessing trajectory quality. This enables the creation of high-quality training trajectories for supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and the derivation of accurate reward signals for reinforcement learning (RL). Our evaluation on the challenging $τ^2$-bench benchmark demonstrates the effectiveness of the framework. Notably, our compact \textbf{CoVe-4B} model achieves success rates of 43.0\% and 59.4\% in the Airline and Retail domains, respectively; its overall performance significantly outperforms strong baselines of similar scale and remains competitive with models up to $17\times$ its size. These results indicate that CoVe provides an effective and efficient pathway for synthesizing training data for state-of-the-art interactive tool-use agents. To support future research, we open-source our code, trained model, and the full set of 12K high-quality trajectories used for training.

摘要（中文）: 开发多轮交互式工具使用智能体极具挑战性，因为现实世界中用户的需求往往复杂且模糊，而智能体必须执行确定性动作才能满足这些需求。 为了解决这一差距，我们引入了 \textbf{CoVe} (\textbf{Co}nstraint-\textbf{Ve}rification，约束验证)，这是一个后训练数据合成框架，旨在训练交互式工具使用智能体，同时确保数据复杂性和正确性。 CoVe 首先定义显式任务约束，这些约束具有双重作用：它们引导复杂轨迹的生成，并充当评估轨迹质量的确定性验证器。 这使得能够为有监督微调 (SFT) 创建高质量的训练轨迹，并推导出用于强化学习 (RL) 的精确奖励信号。 我们在具有挑战性的 $τ^2$-bench 基准测试上的评估证明了该框架的有效性。 值得注意的是，我们紧凑的 \textbf{CoVe-4B} 模型在 Airline 和 Retail 领域分别实现了 43.0\% 和 59.4\% 的成功率； 其整体性能显著优于类似规模的强大基线模型，并与高达其规模 $17\times$ 的模型相比仍具有竞争力。 这些结果表明，CoVe 为合成最先进交互式工具使用智能体的训练数据提供了一种有效且高效的途径。 为了支持未来的研究，我们开源了我们的代码、训练好的模型以及用于训练的完整 12K 高质量轨迹集。

TLDR: The paper introduces CoVe, a post-training data synthesis framework for interactive tool-use agents that uses constraint-guided verification to generate high-quality training data, achieving strong performance compared to larger models. The code, model, and training data are open-sourced.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了CoVe，一个交互式工具使用代理的后训练数据合成框架，它使用约束引导的验证来生成高质量的训练数据，与更大的模型相比，实现了强大的性能。代码、模型和训练数据已开源。
