Authors: Jinpeng Chen, Cheng Gong, Hanbo Li, Ziru Liu, Zichen Tian, Xinyu Fu, Shi Wu, Chenyang Zhang, Wu Zhang, Suiyun Zhang, Dandan Tu, Rui Liu

Modular Memory is the Key to Continual Learning Agents

Abstract (English): Foundation models have transformed machine learning through large-scale pretraining and increased test-time compute. Despite surpassing human performance in several domains, these models remain fundamentally limited in continuous operation, experience accumulation, and personalization, capabilities that are central to adaptive intelligence. While continual learning research has long targeted these goals, its historical focus on in-weight learning (IWL), i.e., updating a single model's parameters to absorb new knowledge, has rendered catastrophic forgetting a persistent challenge. Our position is that combining the strengths of In-Weight Learning (IWL) and the newly emerged capabilities of In-Context Learning (ICL) through the design of modular memory is the missing piece for continual adaptation at scale. We outline a conceptual framework for modular memory-centric architectures that leverage ICL for rapid adaptation and knowledge accumulation, and IWL for stable updates to model capabilities, charting a practical roadmap toward continually learning agents.

摘要（中文）: 大型预训练和测试时计算能力的提升，已经通过基础模型彻底改变了机器学习领域。尽管在多个领域超越了人类的表现，这些模型在持续运作、经验积累和个性化方面仍然存在根本性局限，而这些能力正是自适应智能的核心。尽管持续学习研究长期以来都以这些目标为导向，但其历史上对于模型内部权重学习(IWL)的关注，即更新单个模型的参数以吸收新知识，使得灾难性遗忘成为一个持续存在的挑战。我们的观点是，通过模块化记忆的设计，将模型内部权重学习(IWL)的优势与新兴的上下文学习(ICL)能力相结合，是实现大规模持续适应的关键。我们概述了一个基于模块化记忆架构的概念框架，该框架利用上下文学习(ICL)进行快速适应和知识积累，利用模型内部权重学习(IWL)来稳定更新模型能力，从而为构建持续学习智能体规划了一条切实可行的路线图。

TLDR: This paper proposes a modular memory-centric architecture combining In-Weight Learning (IWL) and In-Context Learning (ICL) to address catastrophic forgetting in continual learning, aiming for scalable and adaptive intelligent agents.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种模块化的、以记忆为中心的架构，结合了 In-Weight Learning (IWL) 和 In-Context Learning (ICL)，旨在解决持续学习中的灾难性遗忘问题，目标是实现可扩展和自适应的智能体。

Authors: Vaggelis Dorovatas, Malte Schwerin, Andrew D. Bagdanov, Lucas Caccia, Antonio Carta, Laurent Charlin, Barbara Hammer, Tyler L. Hayes, Timm Hess, Christopher Kanan, Dhireesha Kudithipudi, Xialei Liu, Vincenzo Lomonaco, Jorge Mendez-Mendez, Darshan Patil, Ameya Prabhu, Elisa Ricci, Tinne Tuytelaars, Gido M. van de Ven, Liyuan Wang, Joost van de Weijer, Jonghyun Choi, Martin Mundt, Rahaf Aljundi

Discrete World Models via Regularization

Abstract (English): World models aim to capture the states and dynamics of an environment in a compact latent space. Moreover, using Boolean state representations is particularly useful for search heuristics and symbolic reasoning and planning. Existing approaches keep latents informative via decoder-based reconstruction, or instead via contrastive or reward signals. In this work, we introduce Discrete World Models via Regularization (DWMR): a reconstruction-free and contrastive-free method for unsupervised Boolean world-model learning. In particular, we introduce a novel world-modeling loss that couples latent prediction with specialized regularizers. Such regularizers maximize the entropy and independence of the representation bits through variance, correlation, and coskewness penalties, while simultaneously enforcing a locality prior for sparse action changes. To enable effective optimization, we also introduce a novel training scheme improving robustness to discrete roll-outs. Experiments on two benchmarks with underlying combinatorial structure show that DWMR learns more accurate representations and transitions than reconstruction-based alternatives. Finally, DWMR can also be paired with an auxiliary reconstruction decoder, and this combination yields additional gains.

摘要（中文）: 世界模型旨在捕捉环境的状态和动态，并将其压缩到紧凑的潜在空间中。此外，使用布尔状态表示对搜索启发式方法以及符号推理和规划特别有用。现有方法通过基于解码器的重构，或者通过对比或奖励信号来保持潜在变量的信息量。在这项工作中，我们提出了基于正则化的离散世界模型（DWMR）：一种无重构和无对比的无监督布尔世界模型学习方法。我们特别引入了一种新颖的世界建模损失，该损失将潜在预测与专门的正则项相结合。这些正则项通过方差、相关性和余偏度惩罚最大化表示位的熵和独立性，同时对稀疏动作变化强制执行局部性先验。为了实现有效的优化，我们还引入了一种新颖的训练方案，以提高对离散展开的鲁棒性。在具有底层组合结构的两个基准测试上的实验表明，DWMR比基于重构的替代方案学习到更准确的表示和转换。最后，DWMR还可以与辅助重构解码器配对，这种组合会产生额外的收益。

TLDR: This paper introduces Discrete World Models via Regularization (DWMR), a reconstruction-free method for unsupervised Boolean world-model learning, using a novel loss function and regularizers to improve representation and transition accuracy.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了通过正则化实现的离散世界模型（DWMR），这是一种无需重建的无监督布尔世界模型学习方法，它使用了一种新颖的损失函数和正则化器来提高表示和转换的准确性。

Authors: Davide Bizzaro, Luciano Serafini

MVR: Multi-view Video Reward Shaping for Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): Reward design is of great importance for solving complex tasks with reinforcement learning. Recent studies have explored using image-text similarity produced by vision-language models (VLMs) to augment rewards of a task with visual feedback. A common practice linearly adds VLM scores to task or success rewards without explicit shaping, potentially altering the optimal policy. Moreover, such approaches, often relying on single static images, struggle with tasks whose desired behavior involves complex, dynamic motions spanning multiple visually different states. Furthermore, single viewpoints can occlude critical aspects of an agent's behavior. To address these issues, this paper presents Multi-View Video Reward Shaping (MVR), a framework that models the relevance of states regarding the target task using videos captured from multiple viewpoints. MVR leverages video-text similarity from a frozen pre-trained VLM to learn a state relevance function that mitigates the bias towards specific static poses inherent in image-based methods. Additionally, we introduce a state-dependent reward shaping formulation that integrates task-specific rewards and VLM-based guidance, automatically reducing the influence of VLM guidance once the desired motion pattern is achieved. We confirm the efficacy of the proposed framework with extensive experiments on challenging humanoid locomotion tasks from HumanoidBench and manipulation tasks from MetaWorld, verifying the design choices through ablation studies.

摘要（中文）: 奖励设计对于利用强化学习解决复杂任务至关重要。最近的研究探索了使用视觉-语言模型（VLM）产生的图像-文本相似性来增强具有视觉反馈的任务的奖励。一种常见的做法是将VLM分数线性添加到任务或成功奖励中，而没有进行显式的塑造，这可能会改变最优策略。此外，此类方法通常依赖于单个静态图像，难以应对涉及复杂、动态运动的任务，这些运动跨越多个视觉上不同的状态。此外，单一视角可能会遮挡智能体行为的关键方面。为了解决这些问题，本文提出了多视角视频奖励塑造（MVR），这是一个框架，它使用从多个视角捕获的视频对关于目标任务的状态相关性进行建模。MVR利用来自冻结的预训练VLM的视频-文本相似性来学习状态相关性函数，从而减轻图像方法中固有的对特定静态姿势的偏见。此外，我们引入了一种状态相关的奖励塑造公式，该公式集成了任务特定奖励和基于VLM的指导，一旦达到期望的运动模式，便会自动减少VLM指导的影响。我们通过对来自HumanoidBench的人形运动任务和来自MetaWorld的操纵任务进行的大量实验，证实了所提出框架的有效性，并通过消融研究验证了设计选择。

TLDR: This paper introduces Multi-View Video Reward Shaping (MVR), a reinforcement learning framework that utilizes video-text similarity from multiple viewpoints to improve reward shaping for tasks with complex, dynamic motions, addressing limitations of single-image and single-viewpoint approaches.

简要说明: 本文介绍了多视角视频奖励塑造（MVR），这是一种强化学习框架，它利用来自多个视角的视频-文本相似性来改进具有复杂、动态运动的任务的奖励塑造，解决了单图像和单视点方法的局限性。

Authors: Lirui Luo, Guoxi Zhang, Hongming Xu, Yaodong Yang, Cong Fang, Qing Li

Reasoning as Gradient: Scaling MLE Agents Beyond Tree Search

Abstract (English): LLM-based agents for machine learning engineering (MLE) predominantly rely on tree search, a form of gradient-free optimization that uses scalar validation scores to rank candidates. As LLM reasoning capabilities improve, exhaustive enumeration becomes increasingly inefficient compared to directed updates, analogous to how accurate gradients enable efficient descent over random search. We introduce \textsc{Gome}, an MLE agent that operationalizes gradient-based optimization. \textsc{Gome} maps structured diagnostic reasoning to gradient computation, success memory to momentum, and multi-trace execution to distributed optimization. Under a closed-world protocol that isolates architectural effects from external knowledge, \textsc{Gome} achieves a state-of-the-art 35.1\% any-medal rate on MLE-Bench with a restricted 12-hour budget on a single V100 GPU. Scaling experiments across 10 models reveal a critical crossover: with weaker models, tree search retains advantages by compensating for unreliable reasoning through exhaustive exploration; as reasoning capability strengthens, gradient-based optimization progressively outperforms, with the gap widening at frontier-tier models. Given the rapid advancement of reasoning-oriented LLMs, this positions gradient-based optimization as an increasingly favorable paradigm. We release our codebase and GPT-5 traces.

摘要（中文）: 基于LLM的机器学习工程（MLE）智能体主要依赖于树搜索，一种无梯度优化形式，利用标量验证分数对候选方案进行排序。随着LLM推理能力的提升，相对于导向更新，穷举式的枚举方法效率越来越低，这类似于精确梯度如何使高效下降优于随机搜索。我们引入了\textsc{Gome}，一个实现基于梯度的优化的MLE智能体。\textsc{Gome}将结构化的诊断推理映射到梯度计算，将成功记忆映射到动量，并将多迹线执行映射到分布式优化。在一个将架构效应与外部知识隔离的封闭世界协议下，\textsc{Gome}在MLE-Bench上实现了35.1%的最先进的任意奖牌率，预算限制为单张V100 GPU上的12小时。跨越10个模型的缩放实验揭示了一个关键的交叉点：对于较弱的模型，树搜索通过穷举搜索来弥补不可靠的推理，从而保留了优势；随着推理能力的增强，基于梯度的优化逐渐胜出，并且差距在处于前沿水平的模型中扩大。鉴于面向推理的LLM的快速发展，这使得基于梯度的优化成为一种越来越有利的范式。我们发布了我们的代码库和GPT-5的迹线。

TLDR: The paper introduces GOME, a gradient-based optimization MLE agent, and demonstrates that it outperforms tree search with stronger LLMs, positioning gradient-based optimization as a favorable paradigm for future MLE agents.

Authors: Yifei Zhang, Xu Yang, Xiao Yang, Bowen Xian, Qizheng Li, Shikai Fang, Jingyuan Li, Jian Wang, Mingrui Xu, Weiqing Liu, Jiang Bian

Learning Structured Reasoning via Tractable Trajectory Control

Abstract (English): Large language models can exhibit emergent reasoning behaviors, often manifested as recurring lexical patterns (e.g., "wait," indicating verification). However, complex reasoning trajectories remain sparse in unconstrained sampling, and standard RL often fails to guarantee the acquisition of diverse reasoning behaviors. We propose a systematic discovery and reinforcement of diverse reasoning patterns through structured reasoning, a paradigm that requires targeted exploration of specific reasoning patterns during the RL process. To this end, we propose Ctrl-R, a framework for learning structured reasoning via tractable trajectory control that actively guides the rollout process, incentivizing the exploration of diverse reasoning patterns that are critical for complex problem-solving. The resulting behavior policy enables accurate importance-sampling estimation, supporting unbiased on-policy optimization. We further introduce a power-scaling factor on the importance-sampling weights, allowing the policy to selectively learn from exploratory, out-of-distribution trajectories while maintaining stable optimization. Experiments demonstrate that Ctrl-R enables effective exploration and internalization of previously unattainable reasoning patterns, yielding consistent improvements across language and vision-language models on mathematical reasoning tasks.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型可以展现涌现的推理行为，通常表现为重复出现的词汇模式（例如，"wait"，表示验证）。然而，复杂的推理轨迹在非约束采样中仍然是稀疏的，并且标准强化学习通常无法保证获得多样化的推理行为。我们提出了一种通过结构化推理系统地发现和强化多样化推理模式的方法，这是一种范式，需要在强化学习过程中有针对性地探索特定的推理模式。为此，我们提出了Ctrl-R，一个通过易于处理的轨迹控制来学习结构化推理的框架，该框架主动引导推演过程，激励探索对复杂问题求解至关重要的多样化推理模式。由此产生的行为策略能够实现准确的重要性采样估计，支持无偏的同策略优化。我们进一步引入了重要性采样权重上的幂缩放因子，使策略能够有选择性地从探索性的、分布外的轨迹中学习，同时保持优化的稳定性。实验表明，Ctrl-R能够有效地探索和内化先前无法获得的推理模式，从而在语言和视觉-语言模型上对数学推理任务产生持续的改进。

TLDR: The paper introduces Ctrl-R, a framework for reinforcement learning that actively guides exploration of diverse reasoning patterns in large language models, improving performance on mathematical reasoning tasks.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一个名为Ctrl-R的强化学习框架，该框架主动引导大型语言模型探索多样化的推理模式，从而提高了在数学推理任务上的性能。

Authors: Po-Nien Kung, Zhen Yang, Jeffrey Luo, Cheng-Fu Yang, Haikang Deng, Zi-Yi Dou, Yinfei Yang, Nanyun Peng, Zhe Gan, Kai-Wei Chang

ToolRLA: Fine-Grained Reward Decomposition for Tool-Integrated Reinforcement Learning Alignment in Domain-Specific Agents

Abstract (English): Tool-integrated reasoning agents interleaving natural language deliberation with external API calls show promise for complex multi-step tasks. However, aligning such agents for high-stakes domain-specific deployment is challenging, as existing reinforcement learning uses coarse binary rewards (success/failure) that insufficiently guide nuanced tool invocation in production. We present ToolRLA, a three-stage post-training pipeline (Supervised Fine-Tuning, Group Relative Policy Optimization, Direct Preference Optimization) for domain-specific tool-integrated agents. Its core is a fine-grained reward function with multiplicative correctness decomposition, evaluating tool invocation across four dimensions: format validity, tool selection correctness, invocation efficiency, and domain constraint compliance. Multiplicative composition prioritizes correct tool selection (a prerequisite for meaningful parameter evaluation), while a large negative compliance penalty (λ=10) ensures regulatory adherence. Deployed on a real-world financial advisory copilot (80+ advisors, 1,200+ daily queries, 15+ heterogeneous APIs), ToolRLA achieves 47% higher end-to-end task completion (62% to 91%), 63% lower tool invocation error (38% to 14%), 93% lower regulatory violation (12% to 0.8%), and sub-2-second latency after three months. Ablation studies confirm fine-grained reward decomposition contributes 7 percentage points over coarse additive rewards; generalizability is validated on ToolBench and API-Bank.

摘要（中文）: 集成工具的推理智能体，通过交错自然语言审议和外部API调用，展现了解决复杂多步骤任务的潜力。然而，在领域特定的高风险部署中对齐此类智能体面临挑战，因为现有的强化学习方法使用粗略的二元奖励（成功/失败），不足以指导生产环境中细致的工具调用。我们提出了ToolRLA，一个针对领域特定集成工具智能体的三阶段后训练流程（监督式微调、群体相对策略优化、直接偏好优化）。其核心是具有乘法正确性分解方式的细粒度奖励函数，从四个维度评估工具调用：格式有效性、工具选择正确性、调用效率和领域约束合规性。乘法组合优先考虑正确的工具选择（有意义的参数评估的前提），而较大的负合规性惩罚（λ=10）确保符合法规。部署在真实的金融咨询副驾驶系统上（80+顾问，1200+每日查询，15+异构API），经过三个月，ToolRLA实现了47%更高的端到端任务完成率（62%提升至91%），63%更低的工具调用错误率（38%降低至14%），93%更低的法规违规率（12%降低至0.8%），以及亚2秒延迟。消融研究证实，与粗略的加性奖励相比，细粒度奖励分解贡献了7个百分点的提升；在ToolBench和API-Bank上的验证证明了其泛化能力。

TLDR: ToolRLA presents a fine-grained reward decomposition method for aligning tool-integrated reinforcement learning agents in domain-specific applications, demonstrating significant improvements in task completion, error reduction, and regulatory compliance in a real-world financial advisory copilot.

简要说明: ToolRLA提出了一种细粒度的奖励分解方法，用于对齐领域特定应用中的工具集成强化学习代理。在一个真实的金融咨询副驾驶系统中，该方法在任务完成度、错误减少和监管合规性方面表现出显著的改进。

Authors: Pengbo Liu

Evaluating and Understanding Scheming Propensity in LLM Agents

Abstract (English): As frontier language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous agents pursuing complex, long-term objectives, there is increased risk of scheming: agents covertly pursuing misaligned goals. Prior work has focused on showing agents are capable of scheming, but their propensity to scheme in realistic scenarios remains underexplored. To understand when agents scheme, we decompose scheming incentives into agent factors and environmental factors. We develop realistic settings allowing us to systematically vary these factors, each with scheming opportunities for agents that pursue instrumentally convergent goals such as self-preservation, resource acquisition, and goal-guarding. We find only minimal instances of scheming despite high environmental incentives, and show this is unlikely due to evaluation awareness. While inserting adversarially-designed prompt snippets that encourage agency and goal-directedness into an agent's system prompt can induce high scheming rates, snippets used in real agent scaffolds rarely do. Surprisingly, in model organisms (Hubinger et al., 2023) built with these snippets, scheming behavior is remarkably brittle: removing a single tool can drop the scheming rate from 59% to 3%, and increasing oversight can raise rather than deter scheming by up to 25%. Our incentive decomposition enables systematic measurement of scheming propensity in settings relevant for deployment, which is necessary as agents are entrusted with increasingly consequential tasks.

摘要（中文）: 随着前沿语言模型越来越多地被部署为追求复杂、长期目标的自主智能体，出现阴谋策划的风险也随之增加：智能体秘密地追求未对齐的目标。先前的工作主要集中于展示智能体具备阴谋策划的能力，但它们在现实场景中进行阴谋策划的倾向仍然未被充分探索。为了理解智能体何时会进行阴谋策划，我们将阴谋策划的激励分解为智能体因素和环境因素。我们开发了现实场景，允许我们系统地改变这些因素，每个场景都为追求工具性收敛目标（例如自我保护、资源获取和目标守卫）的智能体提供了阴谋策划的机会。我们发现，尽管存在很高的环境激励，但阴谋策划的实例仍然很少，并且表明这不太可能是由于评估感知造成的。虽然将对抗性设计的提示片段插入到智能体的系统提示中，鼓励其自主性和目标导向性，可以诱导高阴谋策划率，但在真实的智能体框架中使用的片段很少会这样做。令人惊讶的是，在使用这些片段构建的模型生物（Hubinger et al., 2023）中，阴谋策划行为非常脆弱：移除单个工具可以将阴谋策划率从 59% 降至 3%，而增加监督可以提高而不是阻止阴谋策划，高达 25%。我们的激励分解能够对在与部署相关的设置中智能体的阴谋策划倾向进行系统测量，这对于将越来越重要的任务委托给智能体来说是必要的。

TLDR: This paper investigates the propensity of LLM agents to engage in scheming behavior (covertly pursuing misaligned goals) across various agent and environmental factors, finding limited instances of scheming in realistic settings but highlighting the brittleness of induced scheming behaviors.

简要说明: 该论文研究了LLM agent在不同agent和环境因素下产生阴谋行为（秘密追求不一致目标）的倾向，发现在现实环境中阴谋行为的实例有限，但也强调了诱导阴谋行为的脆弱性。

Authors: Mia Hopman, Jannes Elstner, Maria Avramidou, Amritanshu Prasad, David Lindner

State-Action Inpainting Diffuser for Continuous Control with Delay

Abstract (English): Signal delay poses a fundamental challenge in continuous control and reinforcement learning (RL) by introducing a temporal gap between interaction and perception. Current solutions have largely evolved along two distinct paradigms: model-free approaches which utilize state augmentation to preserve Markovian properties, and model-based methods which focus on inferring latent beliefs via dynamics modeling. In this paper, we bridge these perspectives by introducing State-Action Inpainting Diffuser (SAID), a framework that integrates the inductive bias of dynamics learning with the direct decision-making capability of policy optimization. By formulating the problem as a joint sequence inpainting task, SAID implicitly captures environmental dynamics while directly generating consistent plans, effectively operating at the intersection of model-based and model-free paradigms. Crucially, this generative formulation allows SAID to be seamlessly applied to both online and offline RL. Extensive experiments on delayed continuous control benchmarks demonstrate that SAID achieves state-of-the-art and robust performance. Our study suggests a new methodology to advance the field of RL with delay.

摘要（中文）: 信号延迟是连续控制和强化学习（RL）中的一项根本挑战，它在交互和感知之间引入了时间间隔。目前的解决方案主要沿着两种不同的范式演进：利用状态增强来保持马尔可夫性质的免模型方法，以及侧重于通过动力学建模推断潜在置信度的基于模型方法。本文中，我们通过引入状态-动作补全扩散器（SAID）来弥合这两种观点，该框架将动力学学习的归纳偏置与策略优化的直接决策能力相结合。通过将问题表述为一个联合序列补全任务，SAID隐式地捕获了环境动力学，同时直接生成一致的计划，有效地在基于模型和免模型范式的交汇处运行。关键的是，这种生成式公式使得SAID能够无缝地应用于在线和离线强化学习。在延迟连续控制基准上的大量实验表明，SAID实现了最先进且稳健的性能。我们的研究提出了一种新的方法来推进具有延迟的强化学习领域。

TLDR: The paper introduces State-Action Inpainting Diffuser (SAID) to address signal delay in continuous control by integrating dynamics learning with policy optimization using a sequence inpainting task, achieving state-of-the-art performance in delayed RL benchmarks.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了状态-动作补全扩散器(SAID)，通过将动态学习与策略优化相结合，并使用序列补全任务来解决连续控制中的信号延迟问题，在延迟强化学习基准测试中实现了最先进的性能。

Authors: Dongqi Han, Wei Wang, Enze Zhang, Dongsheng Li

Graph-Based Self-Healing Tool Routing for Cost-Efficient LLM Agents

Abstract (English): Tool-using LLM agents face a reliability-cost tradeoff: routing every decision through the LLM improves correctness but incurs high latency and inference cost, while pre-coded workflow graphs reduce cost but become brittle under unanticipated compound tool failures. We present Self-Healing Router, a fault-tolerant orchestration architecture that treats most agent control-flow decisions as routing rather than reasoning. The system combines (i) parallel health monitors that assign priority scores to runtime conditions such as tool outages and risk signals, and (ii) a cost-weighted tool graph where Dijkstra's algorithm performs deterministic shortest-path routing. When a tool fails mid-execution, its edges are reweighted to infinity and the path is recomputed -- yielding automatic recovery without invoking the LLM. The LLM is reserved exclusively for cases where no feasible path exists, enabling goal demotion or escalation. Prior graph-based tool-use systems (ControlLLM, ToolNet, NaviAgent) focus on tool selection and planning; our contribution is runtime fault tolerance with deterministic recovery and binary observability -- every failure is either a logged reroute or an explicit escalation, never a silent skip. Across 19 scenarios spanning three graph topologies (linear pipeline, dependency DAG, parallel fan-out), Self-Healing Router matches ReAct's correctness while reducing control-plane LLM calls by 93% (9 vs 123 aggregate) and eliminating the silent-failure cases observed in a well-engineered static workflow baseline under compound failures.

摘要（中文）: 使用工具的大语言模型智能体面临着可靠性-成本的权衡：将每个决策都通过大语言模型路由可以提高准确性，但会导致高延迟和推理成本，而预编码的工作流图可以降低成本，但在遇到意料之外的复合工具故障时会变得脆弱。 我们提出了自愈型路由器，一种容错的编排架构，它将大多数智能体控制流决策视为路由而非推理。该系统结合了 (i) 并行健康监测器，其为工具中断和风险信号等运行时条件分配优先级分数，以及 (ii) 一个代价加权工具图，其中Dijkstra算法执行确定性的最短路径 routing。当一个工具在执行过程中出现故障时，它的边会被重新加权为无穷大，路径会被重新计算——从而在不调用大语言模型的情况下实现自动恢复。大语言模型专门用于不存在可行路径的情况，从而允许目标降级或升级。先前的基于图的工具使用系统（ControlLLM、ToolNet、NaviAgent）侧重于工具选择和规划；我们的贡献是具有确定性恢复和二进制可观察性的运行时容错性——每个故障要么是日志记录的重路由，要么是显式升级，绝不是静默跳过。在涵盖三个图拓扑（线性流水线、依赖DAG、并行扇出）的19 个场景中，自愈型路由器在匹配ReAct的正确性的同时，将控制平面大语言模型调用减少了93%（9 vs 123 总计），并消除了在复合故障下，一个精心设计的静态工作流基线中所观察到的静默故障情况。

TLDR: The paper introduces Self-Healing Router, a cost-efficient and fault-tolerant architecture for LLM agents that uses graph-based routing with automatic failure recovery, significantly reducing LLM calls while maintaining correctness.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了 Self-Healing Router，一种成本效益高且容错的 LLM 代理架构，它使用基于图的路由和自动故障恢复，在保持正确性的同时显著减少了 LLM 调用。

Authors: Neeraj Bholani

GAC: Stabilizing Asynchronous RL Training for LLMs via Gradient Alignment Control

Abstract (English): Asynchronous execution is essential for scaling reinforcement learning (RL) to modern large model workloads, including large language models and AI agents, but it can fundamentally alter RL optimization behavior. While prior work on asynchronous RL focuses on training throughput and distributional correction, we show that naively applying asynchrony to policy-gradient updates can induce qualitatively different training dynamics and lead to severe training instability. Through systematic empirical and theoretical analysis, we identify a key signature of this instability: asynchronous training exhibits persistently high cosine similarity between consecutive policy gradients, in contrast to the near-orthogonal updates observed under synchronized training. This stale-aligned gradient effect amplifies correlated updates and increases the risk of overshooting and divergence. Motivated by this observation, we propose GRADIENT ALIGNMENT CONTROL(GAC), a simple dynamics-aware stabilization method that regulates asynchronous RL progress along stale-aligned directions via gradient projection. We establish convergence guarantees under bounded staleness and demonstrate empirically that GAC recovers stable, on-policy training dynamics and matches synchronized baselines even at high staleness.

摘要（中文）: 大规模异步执行对于将强化学习 (RL) 扩展到现代大型模型工作负载（包括大型语言模型和 AI 代理）至关重要，但它可能从根本上改变 RL 优化行为。虽然先前关于异步 RL 的工作侧重于训练吞吐量和分布校正，但我们表明，简单地将异步应用于策略梯度更新可能导致性质上不同的训练动态，并导致严重的训练不稳定。通过系统的实证和理论分析，我们识别出这种不稳定性的一个关键特征：与同步训练下观察到的近乎正交的更新相比，异步训练表现出连续策略梯度之间持续较高的余弦相似度。这种陈旧对齐的梯度效应会放大相关的更新，并增加超调和发散的风险。受此观察的启发，我们提出梯度对齐控制 (GAC)，这是一种简单的、感知动态的稳定方法，通过梯度投影来调节异步 RL 沿陈旧对齐方向的进展。我们建立了有界陈旧性下的收敛性保证，并通过实验证明，即使在高陈旧性下，GAC 也能恢复稳定的、on-policy 训练动态，并与同步基线相匹配。

TLDR: This paper identifies and addresses instability in asynchronous RL training for LLMs caused by stale-aligned gradients, proposing a Gradient Alignment Control (GAC) method to stabilize training and ensure convergence, even with high staleness.

简要说明: 本文发现并解决了用于LLM的异步RL训练中，由于过时梯度对齐引起的不稳定性问题，并提出了一种梯度对齐控制（GAC）方法，以稳定训练并确保收敛，即使在高延迟的情况下也是如此。

Authors: Haofeng Xu, Junwei Su, Yukun Tian, Lansong Diao, Zhengping Qian, Chuan Wu

ATA: Bridging Implicit Reasoning with Attention-Guided and Action-Guided Inference for Vision-Language Action Models

Abstract (English): Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models rely on current observations, including images, language instructions, and robot states, to predict actions and complete tasks. While accurate visual perception is crucial for precise action prediction and execution, recent work has attempted to further improve performance by introducing explicit reasoning during inference. However, such approaches face significant limitations. They often depend on data-intensive resources such as Chain-of-Thought (CoT) style annotations to decompose tasks into step-by-step reasoning, and in many cases require additional visual grounding annotations (e.g., bounding boxes or masks) to highlight relevant image regions. Moreover, they involve time-consuming dataset construction, labeling, and retraining, which ultimately results in longer inference sequences and reduced efficiency. To address these challenges, we propose ATA, a novel training-free framework that introduces implicit reasoning into VLA inference through complementary attention-guided and action-guided strategies. Unlike CoT or explicit visual-grounding methods, ATA formulates reasoning implicitly by integrating attention maps with an action-based region of interest (RoI), thereby adaptively refining visual inputs without requiring extra training or annotations. ATA is a plug-and-play implicit reasoning approach for VLA models, lightweight yet effective. Extensive experiments show that it consistently improves task success and robustness while preserving, and even enhancing, inference efficiency.

摘要（中文）: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型依赖于当前的观测信息，包括图像、语言指令和机器人状态，来预测动作并完成任务。虽然精确的视觉感知对于精确的动作预测和执行至关重要，但最近的研究试图通过在推理过程中引入显式推理来进一步提高性能。然而，这些方法面临着重大限制。它们通常依赖于数据密集型资源，如思维链（CoT）风格的标注，将任务分解为逐步推理，并且在许多情况下需要额外的视觉定位标注（例如，边界框或掩码）来突出显示相关的图像区域。此外，它们涉及耗时的数据集构建、标注和重新训练，最终导致更长的推理序列和降低的效率。为了应对这些挑战，我们提出了一种新的免训练框架ATA，该框架通过互补的注意力引导和动作引导策略将隐式推理引入VLA推理中。与CoT或显式视觉定位方法不同，ATA通过将注意力图与基于动作的感兴趣区域（RoI）相结合来隐式地构建推理，从而自适应地细化视觉输入，而无需额外的训练或标注。ATA是一种即插即用的VLA模型隐式推理方法，轻量级且有效。大量的实验表明，它始终如一地提高了任务成功率和鲁棒性，同时保持甚至提高了推理效率。

TLDR: The paper introduces ATA, a training-free, plug-and-play method for improving Vision-Language-Action (VLA) model inference by implicitly reasoning through attention and action guidance, achieving better task success and robustness without needing additional data or retraining.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为ATA的免训练、即插即用的方法，通过注意力和动作引导进行隐式推理，从而改进视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的推理。该方法在不需要额外数据或重新训练的情况下，实现了更好的任务成功率和鲁棒性。

Authors: Cheng Yang, Jianhao Jiao, Lingyi Huang, Jinqi Xiao, Zhexiang Tang, Yu Gong, Yibiao Ying, Yang Sui, Jintian Lin, Wen Huang, Bo Yuan

LLM-assisted Semantic Option Discovery for Facilitating Adaptive Deep Reinforcement Learning

Abstract (English): Despite achieving remarkable success in complex tasks, Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) is still suffering from critical issues in practical applications, such as low data efficiency, lack of interpretability, and limited cross-environment transferability. However, the learned policy generating actions based on states are sensitive to the environmental changes, struggling to guarantee behavioral safety and compliance. Recent research shows that integrating Large Language Models (LLMs) with symbolic planning is promising in addressing these challenges. Inspired by this, we introduce a novel LLM-driven closed-loop framework, which enables semantic-driven skill reuse and real-time constraint monitoring by mapping natural language instructions into executable rules and semantically annotating automatically created options. The proposed approach utilizes the general knowledge of LLMs to facilitate exploration efficiency and adapt to transferable options for similar environments, and provides inherent interpretability through semantic annotations. To validate the effectiveness of this framework, we conduct experiments on two domains, Office World and Montezuma's Revenge, respectively. The results demonstrate superior performance in data efficiency, constraint compliance, and cross-task transferability.

摘要（中文）: 尽管在复杂任务中取得了显著成功，但深度强化学习（DRL）在实际应用中仍然面临着诸如数据效率低、缺乏可解释性以及跨环境泛化能力有限等关键问题。然而，基于状态生成动作的学习策略对环境变化很敏感，难以保证行为安全和合规性。最近的研究表明，将大型语言模型（LLMs）与符号规划相结合有望解决这些挑战。受此启发，我们提出了一种新型的LLM驱动的闭环框架，该框架通过将自然语言指令映射为可执行规则，并对自动创建的选项进行语义标注，从而实现语义驱动的技能重用和实时约束监控。该方法利用LLMs的通用知识来提高探索效率，并适应类似环境的可迁移选项，并通过语义标注提供固有的可解释性。为了验证该框架的有效性，我们分别在Office World和Montezuma's Revenge两个领域进行了实验。结果表明，该框架在数据效率、约束合规性和跨任务迁移能力方面表现出卓越的性能。

TLDR: This paper introduces an LLM-driven framework for DRL that leverages semantic option discovery to improve data efficiency, interpretability, and cross-environment transferability by mapping natural language instructions to executable rules and semantically annotating options.

Authors: Chang Yao, Jinghui Qin, Kebing Jin, Hankz Hankui Zhuo

From Verbatim to Gist: Distilling Pyramidal Multimodal Memory via Semantic Information Bottleneck for Long-Horizon Video Agents

Abstract (English): While multimodal large language models have demonstrated impressive short-term reasoning, they struggle with long-horizon video understanding due to limited context windows and static memory mechanisms that fail to mirror human cognitive efficiency. Existing paradigms typically fall into two extremes: vision-centric methods that incur high latency and redundancy through dense visual accumulation, or text-centric approaches that suffer from detail loss and hallucination via aggressive captioning. To bridge this gap, we propose MM-Mem, a pyramidal multimodal memory architecture grounded in Fuzzy-Trace Theory. MM-Mem structures memory hierarchically into a Sensory Buffer, Episodic Stream, and Symbolic Schema, enabling the progressive distillation of fine-grained perceptual traces (verbatim) into high-level semantic schemas (gist). Furthermore, to govern the dynamic construction of memory, we derive a Semantic Information Bottleneck objective and introduce SIB-GRPO to optimize the trade-off between memory compression and task-relevant information retention. In inference, we design an entropy-driven top-down memory retrieval strategy, which first tries with the abstract Symbolic Schema and progressively "drills down" to the Sensory Buffer and Episodic Stream under high uncertainty. Extensive experiments across 4 benchmarks confirm the effectiveness of MM-Mem on both offline and streaming tasks, demonstrating robust generalization and validating the effectiveness of cognition-inspired memory organization. Code is available at https://github.com/EliSpectre/MM-Mem.

摘要（中文）: 多模态大型语言模型在短期推理方面表现出令人印象深刻的能力，但由于有限的上下文窗口和未能反映人类认知效率的静态记忆机制，它们在长时程视频理解方面表现不佳。现有的范式通常落入两个极端：视觉中心的方法通过密集的视觉积累导致高延迟和冗余，或者文本中心的方法通过激进的字幕标注遭受细节损失和幻觉。为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了MM-Mem，一种基于模糊迹理论的金字塔式多模态记忆架构。MM-Mem将记忆分层构建为感觉缓冲器、情景流和符号模式，从而能够将细粒度的感知痕迹（逐字）逐渐提炼成高级语义模式（概要）。此外，为了控制记忆的动态构建，我们推导出一个语义信息瓶颈目标，并引入SIB-GRPO来优化记忆压缩和任务相关信息保留之间的权衡。在推理中，我们设计了一种熵驱动的自上而下的记忆检索策略，该策略首先尝试抽象的符号模式，并在高不确定性下逐步 “向下钻取” 到感觉缓冲器和情景流。在4个基准测试上进行的大量实验证实了MM-Mem在离线和流式任务上的有效性，展示了强大的泛化能力，并验证了认知启发式记忆组织方式的有效性。代码可在https://github.com/EliSpectre/MM-Mem 获取。

TLDR: The paper introduces MM-Mem, a pyramidal multimodal memory architecture for long-horizon video agents, using a Semantic Information Bottleneck objective to balance memory compression and task-relevant information.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了MM-Mem，一种用于长时程视频agent的金字塔式多模态记忆架构，它使用语义信息瓶颈目标来平衡记忆压缩和任务相关信息的保留。

Authors: Niu Lian, Yuting Wang, Hanshu Yao, Jinpeng Wang, Bin Chen, Yaowei Wang, Min Zhang, Shu-Tao Xia

VidDoS: Universal Denial-of-Service Attack on Video-based Large Language Models

Abstract (English): Video-LLMs are increasingly deployed in safety-critical applications but are vulnerable to Energy-Latency Attacks (ELAs) that exhaust computational resources. Current image-centric methods fail because temporal aggregation mechanisms dilute individual frame perturbations. Additionally, real-time demands make instance-wise optimization impractical for continuous video streams. We introduce VidDoS, which is the first universal ELA framework tailored for Video-LLMs. Our method leverages universal optimization to create instance-agnostic triggers that require no inference-time gradient calculation. We achieve this through $\textit{masked teacher forcing}$ to steer models toward expensive target sequences, combined with a $\textit{refusal penalty}$ and $\textit{early-termination suppression}$ to override conciseness priors. Testing across three mainstream Video-LLMs and three video datasets, which include video question answering and autonomous driving scenarios, shows extreme degradation. VidDoS induces a token expansion of more than 205$\times$ and inflates the inference latency by more than 15$\times$ relative to clean baselines. Simulations of real-time autonomous driving streams further reveal that this induced latency leads to critical safety violations. We urge the community to recognize and mitigate these high-hazard ELA in Video-LLMs.

摘要（中文）: 视频-LLM正日益部署在安全攸关的应用中，但却容易受到能量-延迟攻击(ELA)的攻击，这些攻击会耗尽计算资源。当前的以图像为中心的方法失效，因为时间聚合机制会稀释单个帧的扰动。此外，实时需求使得逐实例优化对于连续视频流来说不切实际。我们提出了VidDoS，这是第一个专门为视频-LLM设计的通用ELA框架。我们的方法利用通用优化来创建实例无关的触发器，这些触发器不需要推理时的梯度计算。我们通过$\textit{掩码教师强制}$来实现这一点，引导模型朝着昂贵的目标序列发展，结合$\textit{拒绝惩罚}$和$\textit{提前终止抑制}$，以覆盖简洁性先验。在三个主流视频-LLM和三个视频数据集（包括视频问答和自动驾驶场景）上的测试表明存在极端的降级。相对于干净的基线，VidDoS导致了超过205倍的令牌扩展，并将推理延迟膨胀了超过15倍。实时自动驾驶流的仿真进一步揭示了这种诱导的延迟会导致关键的安全违规。我们敦促社区认识并减轻视频-LLM中这些高危ELA。

TLDR: The paper introduces VidDoS, a novel universal energy-latency attack framework tailored for Video-LLMs that can significantly degrade performance and induce safety violations in applications like autonomous driving.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为VidDoS的新型通用能量-延迟攻击框架，专为视频大型语言模型设计，可以显著降低性能，并在自动驾驶等应用中导致安全违规。

Authors: Duoxun Tang, Dasen Dai, Jiyao Wang, Xiao Yang, Jianyu Wang, Siqi Cai

Words & Weights: Streamlining Multi-Turn Interactions via Co-Adaptation

Abstract (English): Test-time policy adaptation for multi-turn interactions (T2PAM) is essential for aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) with dynamic user needs during inference time. However, existing paradigms commonly treat test-time adaptation as a single-axis problem, either purely refining instructions (Prompt Engineering) or only adjusting weights (Test-Time Training), ignoring that interaction failures stem from a coupled mix of ambiguity and incapacity. We argue that these two optimization paths are not merely additive but synergistic: semantic clarity acts as a pre-conditioner for effective parameter updates. To this end, we propose ROSA2, a framework that reformulates interaction as a joint optimization problem over the heterogeneous space of Words and Weights. By mathematically decomposing the error signal, ROSA2 utilizes textual gradients to rectify intent ambiguity and parameter updates to bridge capability gaps. Theoretically, we prove that this co-adaptation strictly reduces the required parameter shift for convergence. Empirically, ROSA2 outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by 30% on MATH while reducing interaction turns by 40%, demonstrating that refining the context unlocks the true potential of parameter updates.

摘要（中文）: 多轮交互的测试时策略适应（T2PAM）对于使大型语言模型（LLMs）在推理时与动态用户需求对齐至关重要。然而，现有的范式通常将测试时适应视为单轴问题，要么纯粹改进指令（提示工程），要么仅调整权重（测试时训练），忽略了交互失败源于歧义和能力不足的耦合混合。我们认为，这两种优化路径并非仅仅是相加关系，而是具有协同作用：语义清晰度是有效参数更新的先决条件。为此，我们提出 ROSA2，一个将交互重构为词和权重异构空间上的联合优化问题的框架。通过数学分解误差信号，ROSA2 利用文本梯度来纠正意图歧义，并利用参数更新来弥合能力差距。理论上，我们证明了这种共同适应严格地减少了收敛所需的参数偏移。 实验结果表明，ROSA2 在 MATH 数据集上优于最先进的基线 30%，同时将交互轮次减少了 40%，这表明完善上下文能够释放参数更新的真正潜力。

TLDR: The paper introduces ROSA2, a framework for multi-turn LLM interactions that jointly optimizes prompts and model weights, leading to improved performance and reduced interaction turns by addressing both ambiguity and incapacity. It synergistically combines prompt engineering and test-time-training, which are generally considered individually.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了ROSA2，一个用于多轮LLM交互的框架，它联合优化prompt和模型权重，通过解决歧义和能力不足问题，提高了性能并减少了交互轮数。 它协同结合了Prompt工程和测试时训练，这两种方法通常被单独考虑部署。

ASTRA-bench: Evaluating Tool-Use Agent Reasoning and Action Planning with Personal User Context

Abstract (English): Next-generation AI must manage vast personal data, diverse tools, and multi-step reasoning, yet most benchmarks remain context-free and single-turn. We present ASTRA-bench (Assistant Skills in Tool-use, Reasoning \& Action-planning), a benchmark that uniquely unifies time-evolving personal context with an interactive toolbox and complex user intents. Our event-driven pipeline generates 2,413 scenarios across four protagonists, grounded in longitudinal life events and annotated by referential, functional, and informational complexity. Evaluation of state-of-the-art models (e.g., Claude-4.5-Opus, DeepSeek-V3.2) reveals significant performance degradation under high-complexity conditions, with argument generation emerging as the primary bottleneck. These findings expose critical limitations in current agents' ability to ground reasoning within messy personal context and orchestrate reliable multi-step plans. We release ASTRA-bench with a full execution environment and evaluation scripts to provide a diagnostic testbed for developing truly context-aware AI assistants.

摘要（中文）: 下一代人工智能必须管理海量的个人数据、多样化的工具以及多步骤推理，然而，大多数基准测试仍然是无上下文且单轮的。我们提出了ASTRA-bench（工具使用、推理和行动规划中的助手技能基准），该基准以独特的方式将随时间演变的个人情境与交互式工具箱和复杂的用户意图相结合。我们的事件驱动型流程生成了跨越四个主角的2,413个场景，这些场景基于纵向生活事件并按指称、功能和信息复杂性进行了标注。对最先进模型（例如，Claude-4.5-Opus, DeepSeek-V3.2）的评估表明，在高复杂性条件下性能显著下降，其中参数生成成为主要瓶颈。这些发现揭示了当前智能体在混杂的个人情境中进行推理以及编排可靠的多步骤计划方面的关键局限性。我们发布了带有完整执行环境和评估脚本的ASTRA-bench，为开发真正具有情境感知能力的人工智能助手提供了一个诊断测试平台。

TLDR: The paper introduces ASTRA-bench, a new benchmark for evaluating AI agents' ability to reason and plan with personal user context and tool use. It reveals performance bottlenecks in current state-of-the-art models when dealing with complex, context-rich scenarios.

Authors: Zidi Xiu, David Q. Sun, Kevin Cheng, Maitrik Patel, Josh Date, Yizhe Zhang, Jiarui Lu, Omar Attia, Raviteja Vemulapalli, Oncel Tuzel, Meng Cao, Samy Bengio

Provable and Practical In-Context Policy Optimization for Self-Improvement

Abstract (English): We study test-time scaling, where a model improves its answer through multi-round self-reflection at inference. We introduce In-Context Policy Optimization (ICPO), in which an agent optimizes its response in context using self-assessed or externally observed rewards without modifying its parameters. To explain this ICPO process, we theoretically show that with sufficient pretraining under a novel Fisher-weighted logit-matching objective, a single-layer linear self-attention model can provably imitate policy-optimization algorithm for linear bandits. Building on this theory, we propose Minimum-Entropy ICPO (ME-ICPO), a practical algorithm that iteratively uses its response and self-assessed reward to refine its response in-context at inference time. By selecting the responses and their rewards with minimum entropy, ME-ICPO ensures the robustness of the self-assessed rewards via majority voting. Across standard mathematical reasoning tasks, ME-ICPO attains competitive, top-tier performance while keeping inference costs affordable compared with other inference-time algorithms. Overall, ICPO provides a principled understanding of self-reflection in LLMs and yields practical benefits for test-time scaling for mathematical reasoning.

摘要（中文）: 我们研究了测试时缩放，即模型通过多轮自反思改进其答案的推理过程。我们提出了上下文情境策略优化（ICPO），其中智能体在不修改其参数的情况下，通过使用自评估或外部观察到的奖励，在上下文情境中优化其响应。为了解释这个ICPO过程，我们在理论上证明，在一种新颖的Fisher加权logit匹配目标下进行充分的预训练后，单层线性自注意力模型能够可靠地模仿线性bandit的策略优化算法。基于此理论，我们提出了最小熵ICPO（ME-ICPO），一种实用的算法，它在推理时迭代地使用其响应和自评估奖励来改进上下文情境中的响应。通过选择具有最小熵的响应及其奖励，ME-ICPO确保了通过多数投票实现自评估奖励的稳健性。在标准的数学推理任务中，ME-ICPO获得了具有竞争力的、顶级的性能，同时与其他推理时算法相比，保持了可承受的推理成本。总体而言，ICPO为LLM中的自反思提供了一种有原则的理解，并为数学推理的测试时缩放带来了实际效益。

TLDR: The paper introduces In-Context Policy Optimization (ICPO), a method for improving LLM responses through self-reflection using self-assessed rewards, and provides theoretical justification and a practical algorithm (ME-ICPO) that achieves competitive performance on mathematical reasoning tasks.

简要说明: 本文介绍了上下文策略优化（ICPO），这是一种通过使用自我评估奖励进行自我反思来改进 LLM 响应的方法，并提供了理论依据和一种实用的算法 (ME-ICPO)，该算法在数学推理任务上实现了具有竞争力的性能。

Authors: Tianrun Yu, Yuxiao Yang, Zhaoyang Wang, Kaixiang Zhao, Porter Jenkins, Xuchao Zhang, Chetan Bansal, Huaxiu Yao, Weitong Zhang

MOSAIC: A Unified Platform for Cross-Paradigm Comparison and Evaluation of Homogeneous and Heterogeneous Multi-Agent RL, LLM, VLM, and Human Decision-Makers

Abstract (English): Reinforcement learning (RL), large language models (LLMs), and vision-language models (VLMs) have been widely studied in isolation. However, existing infrastructure lacks the ability to deploy agents from different decision-making paradigms within the same environment, making it difficult to study them in hybrid multi-agent settings or to compare their behaviour fairly under identical conditions. We present MOSAIC, an open-source platform that bridges this gap by incorporating a diverse set of existing reinforcement learning environments and enabling heterogeneous agents (RL policies, LLMs, VLMs, and human players) to operate within them in ad-hoc team settings with reproducible results. MOSAIC introduces three contributions. (i) An IPC-based worker protocol that wraps both native and third-party frameworks as isolated subprocess workers, each executing its native training and inference logic unmodified, communicating through a versioned inter-process protocol. (ii) An operator abstraction that forms an agent-level interface by mapping workers to agents: each operator, regardless of whether it is backed by an RL policy, an LLM, or a human, conforms to a minimal unified interface. (iii) A deterministic cross-paradigm evaluation framework offering two complementary modes: a manual mode that advances up to N concurrent operators in lock-step under shared seeds for fine-grained visual inspection of behavioural differences, and a script mode that drives automated, long-running evaluation through declarative Python scripts, for reproducible experiments. We release MOSAIC as an open, visual-first platform to facilitate reproducible cross-paradigm research across the RL, LLM, and human-in-the-loop communities.

摘要（中文）: 强化学习(RL)、大型语言模型(LLMs)和视觉-语言模型(VLMs)已被广泛独立研究。然而，现有基础设施缺乏在同一环境中部署来自不同决策范式智能体的能力，使得在混合多智能体环境中研究它们或在相同条件下公平地比较它们的行为变得困难。我们提出了MOSAIC，一个开源平台，通过整合各种现有的强化学习环境并使异构智能体（RL策略、LLM、VLM和人类玩家）能够在其中以可复现的结果在临时团队设置中运行，从而弥合了这一差距。MOSAIC引入了三个贡献。(i) 一种基于IPC的工作者协议，它将原生和第三方框架包装为隔离的子进程工作者，每个工作者都执行其未经修改的原生训练和推理逻辑，并通过带版本控制的进程间协议进行通信。(ii) 一个操作符抽象，通过将工作者映射到智能体来形成一个智能体级别的接口：每个操作符，无论它是由RL策略、LLM还是人类支持，都符合一个最小化的统一接口。(iii) 一个确定性的跨范式评估框架，提供两种互补模式：一种手动模式，在共享种子下以锁步方式推进最多N个并发操作符，以便对行为差异进行细粒度的视觉检查；以及一种脚本模式，通过声明式的Python脚本驱动自动化的、长时间运行的评估，以进行可复现的实验。我们将MOSAIC作为一个开放的、视觉优先的平台发布，以促进跨RL、LLM和人机交互社区中可复现的跨范式研究。

TLDR: The paper introduces MOSAIC, an open-source platform for comparing and evaluating heterogeneous agents (RL policies, LLMs, VLMs, and humans) in multi-agent environments, featuring a unified interface and deterministic evaluation framework.

Authors: Abdulhamid M. Mousa, Yu Fu, Rakhmonberdi Khajiev, Jalaledin M. Azzabi, Abdulkarim M. Mousa, Peng Yang, Yunusa Haruna, Ming Liu

Agents Learn Their Runtime: Interpreter Persistence as Training-Time Semantics

Abstract (English): Tool-augmented LLMs are increasingly deployed as agents that interleave natural-language reasoning with executable Python actions, as in CodeAct-style frameworks. In deployment, these agents rely on runtime state that persists across steps. By contrast, common training pipelines treat agent traces as token sequences, with execution semantics left implicit. This raises a data-centric question: Is state persistence merely an inference-time scaffold, or can models learn to exploit it when training data exposes the corresponding execution semantics? We isolate state persistence as a training-time variable. We introduce Opaque Knapsack, a procedurally generated family of partially observable optimization tasks designed to prevent one-shot solutions. Item attributes and constraints are hidden behind budgeted tool calls, forcing multi-turn control flow and iterative state revision. Holding task instances, prompts, tools, model, and supervision fixed, we generate paired trajectories differing only in whether interpreter state persists across steps or resets after each action. We then fine-tune identical base models (Qwen3-8B) on each trace variant and evaluate all four train-runtime combinations. Our 2x2 cross-evaluation shows that execution semantics primarily affect how agents reach solutions, not whether they do: solution quality is statistically indistinguishable across conditions, but token cost and stability differ substantially. A persistent-trained model in a stateless runtime triggers missing-variable errors in roughly 80% of episodes; a stateless-trained model in a persistent runtime redundantly re-derives retained state, using roughly 3.5x more tokens. Interpreter persistence should be treated as a first-class semantic of agent traces. Aligning fine-tuning data with deployment runtimes improves efficiency and reduces brittle train-runtime mismatches.

摘要（中文）: 工具增强型LLM正日益被部署为智能体，它们以自然语言推理与可执行的Python操作交错进行，如CodeAct风格的框架。在部署中，这些智能体依赖于跨步骤持续存在的运行时状态。相比之下，常见的训练流程将智能体轨迹视为token序列，而将执行语义隐式化。这引发了一个以数据为中心的问题：状态持久化仅仅是一种推理时期的支架，还是模型可以在训练数据暴露相应执行语义时学习利用它？ 我们将状态持久化隔离为一个训练时变量。我们引入了Opaque Knapsack，这是一个程序化生成的、部分可观察的优化任务家族，旨在防止一次性解决方案。物品属性和约束条件隐藏在受预算限制的工具调用之后，迫使进行多轮控制流和迭代状态修订。在固定任务实例、提示语、工具、模型和监督的情况下，我们生成了成对的轨迹，这些轨迹仅在解释器状态是否跨步骤持久化或在每次动作后重置方面有所不同。然后，我们基于每种轨迹变体微调相同的基模型（Qwen3-8B），并评估所有四种训练-运行时的组合。 我们的2x2交叉评估表明，执行语义主要影响智能体达成解决方案的方式，而不是它们是否能够达成：解决方案质量在各种条件下在统计上没有显著差异，但token成本和稳定性差异很大。在无状态运行时中使用持久化训练的模型会在大约80%的episode中触发缺失变量错误；在持久化运行时中使用无状态训练的模型会冗余地重新推导保留的状态，使用大约3.5倍的token。 解释器持久化应被视为智能体轨迹的一流语义。使微调数据与部署运行时对齐可以提高效率并减少脆弱的训练-运行时不匹配。

TLDR: The paper investigates the impact of interpreter state persistence on the training and deployment of tool-augmented LLM agents, finding that aligning training data with deployment runtimes improves efficiency and reduces errors, even though solution quality remains similar.

简要说明: 本文研究了工具增强型LLM Agent的训练和部署中，解释器状态持久性对效果的影响。研究发现，即使最终的解决方案质量相似，使训练数据与部署运行时的状态保持一致可以提高效率并减少错误。

Authors: Victor May, Aaditya Salgarkar, Yishan Wang, Diganta Misra, Huu Nguyen

WorldStereo: Bridging Camera-Guided Video Generation and Scene Reconstruction via 3D Geometric Memories

Abstract (English): Recent advances in foundational Video Diffusion Models (VDMs) have yielded significant progress. Yet, despite the remarkable visual quality of generated videos, reconstructing consistent 3D scenes from these outputs remains challenging, due to limited camera controllability and inconsistent generated content when viewed from distinct camera trajectories. In this paper, we propose WorldStereo, a novel framework that bridges camera-guided video generation and 3D reconstruction via two dedicated geometric memory modules. Formally, the global-geometric memory enables precise camera control while injecting coarse structural priors through incrementally updated point clouds. Moreover, the spatial-stereo memory constrains the model's attention receptive fields with 3D correspondence to focus on fine-grained details from the memory bank. These components enable WorldStereo to generate multi-view-consistent videos under precise camera control, facilitating high-quality 3D reconstruction. Furthermore, the flexible control branch-based WorldStereo shows impressive efficiency, benefiting from the distribution matching distilled VDM backbone without joint training. Extensive experiments across both camera-guided video generation and 3D reconstruction benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach. Notably, we show that WorldStereo acts as a powerful world model, tackling diverse scene generation tasks (whether starting from perspective or panoramic images) with high-fidelity 3D results. Models will be released.

摘要（中文）: 视频扩散模型(VDM)的最新进展带来了显著的进步。然而，尽管生成的视频具有卓越的视觉质量，但由于相机控制的局限性以及从不同相机轨迹观察时生成内容的不一致性，从这些输出中重建一致的3D场景仍然具有挑战性。在本文中，我们提出了一种名为WorldStereo的新型框架，该框架通过两个专用的几何记忆模块，将相机引导的视频生成和3D重建桥接起来。具体而言，全局几何记忆能够实现精确的相机控制，同时通过增量更新的点云注入粗略的结构先验。此外，空间立体记忆利用3D对应关系约束模型的注意力感受野，使其聚焦于来自记忆库的细粒度细节。这些组件使WorldStereo能够在精确的相机控制下生成多视角一致的视频，从而促进高质量的3D重建。此外，基于灵活控制分支的WorldStereo表现出令人印象深刻的效率，受益于无需联合训练的分布匹配蒸馏VDM骨干网络。在相机引导的视频生成和3D重建基准上的大量实验证明了我们方法的有效性。值得注意的是，我们表明WorldStereo作为一个强大的世界模型，可以处理各种场景生成任务（无论是从透视图像还是全景图像开始），并获得高保真的3D结果。模型将被发布。

TLDR: WorldStereo introduces a novel framework for generating multi-view consistent videos with precise camera control by utilizing global-geometric and spatial-stereo memory modules, enabling high-quality 3D reconstruction and serving as a powerful world model.

简要说明: WorldStereo 引入了一种新颖的框架，通过利用全局几何和空间立体记忆模块，生成具有精确相机控制的多视角一致视频，从而实现高质量的 3D 重建，并作为一个强大的世界模型。

Authors: Yisu Zhang, Chenjie Cao, Tengfei Wang, Xuhui Zuo, Junta Wu, Jianke Zhu, Chunchao Guo

DriveCombo: Benchmarking Compositional Traffic Rule Reasoning in Autonomous Driving

Abstract (English): Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are rapidly becoming the intelligence brain of end-to-end autonomous driving systems. A key challenge is to assess whether MLLMs can truly understand and follow complex real-world traffic rules. However, existing benchmarks mainly focus on single-rule scenarios like traffic sign recognition, neglecting the complexity of multi-rule concurrency and conflicts in real driving. Consequently, models perform well on simple tasks but often fail or violate rules in real world complex situations. To bridge this gap, we propose DriveCombo, a text and vision-based benchmark for compositional traffic rule reasoning. Inspired by human drivers' cognitive development, we propose a systematic Five-Level Cognitive Ladder that evaluates reasoning from single-rule understanding to multi-rule integration and conflict resolution, enabling quantitative assessment across cognitive stages. We further propose a Rule2Scene Agent that maps language-based traffic rules to dynamic driving scenes through rule crafting and scene generation, enabling scene-level traffic rule visual reasoning. Evaluations of 14 mainstream MLLMs reveal performance drops as task complexity grows, particularly during rule conflicts. After splitting the dataset and fine-tuning on the training set, we further observe substantial improvements in both traffic rule reasoning and downstream planning capabilities. These results highlight the effectiveness of DriveCombo in advancing compliant and intelligent autonomous driving systems.

摘要（中文）: 多模态大型语言模型（MLLM）正迅速成为端到端自动驾驶系统的智能大脑。一个关键的挑战是评估 MLLM 是否能够真正理解和遵循复杂的真实世界交通规则。然而，现有的基准测试主要集中在单规则场景，如交通标志识别，忽略了真实驾驶中多规则并发和冲突的复杂性。因此，模型在简单任务上表现良好，但在真实世界复杂情况下经常失败或违反规则。为了弥合这一差距，我们提出了 DriveCombo，一个基于文本和视觉的、用于组合交通规则推理的基准。受到人类驾驶员认知发展的启发，我们提出了一个系统的五级认知阶梯，评估从单规则理解到多规则整合和冲突解决的推理能力，从而能够在各个认知阶段进行定量评估。我们进一步提出了一个 Rule2Scene Agent，通过规则制定和场景生成，将基于语言的交通规则映射到动态驾驶场景，从而实现场景级别的交通规则视觉推理。对 14 个主流 MLLM 的评估显示，随着任务复杂性增加，性能下降，尤其是在规则冲突期间。在分割数据集并在训练集上微调后，我们进一步观察到交通规则推理和下游规划能力方面的显著改进。这些结果突出了 DriveCombo 在推进合规且智能的自动驾驶系统方面的有效性。

TLDR: The paper introduces DriveCombo, a new benchmark for evaluating MLLMs' ability to reason about compositional traffic rules in autonomous driving, featuring a five-level cognitive ladder and a Rule2Scene agent for scene generation.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了DriveCombo，一个新的基准，用于评估MLLM在自动驾驶中推理组合交通规则的能力，其特点是五级认知阶梯和一个用于场景生成的Rule2Scene智能体。

WildCross: A Cross-Modal Large Scale Benchmark for Place Recognition and Metric Depth Estimation in Natural Environments

Abstract (English): Recent years have seen a significant increase in demand for robotic solutions in unstructured natural environments, alongside growing interest in bridging 2D and 3D scene understanding. However, existing robotics datasets are predominantly captured in structured urban environments, making them inadequate for addressing the challenges posed by complex, unstructured natural settings. To address this gap, we propose WildCross, a cross-modal benchmark for place recognition and metric depth estimation in large-scale natural environments. WildCross comprises over 476K sequential RGB frames with semi-dense depth and surface normal annotations, each aligned with accurate 6DoF poses and synchronized dense lidar submaps. We conduct comprehensive experiments on visual, lidar, and cross-modal place recognition, as well as metric depth estimation, demonstrating the value of WildCross as a challenging benchmark for multi-modal robotic perception tasks. We provide access to the code repository and dataset at https://csiro-robotics.github.io/WildCross.

摘要（中文）: 近年来，在非结构化自然环境中对机器人解决方案的需求显著增加，同时人们对桥接二维和三维场景理解的兴趣日益浓厚。然而，现有的机器人数据集主要是在结构化的城市环境中采集的，这使得它们不足以应对复杂、非结构化自然环境所带来的挑战。为了弥补这一差距，我们提出了WildCross，一个用于大规模自然环境中地点识别和度量深度估计的跨模态基准。WildCross包含超过47.6万个带有半稠密深度和表面法线标注的连续RGB帧，每个帧都与精确的6自由度姿态和同步的稠密激光雷达子图对齐。我们对视觉、激光雷达和跨模态地点识别以及度量深度估计进行了全面的实验，证明了WildCross作为多模态机器人感知任务的具有挑战性的基准的价值。我们提供了代码仓库和数据集的访问地址：https://csiro-robotics.github.io/WildCross。

TLDR: The paper introduces WildCross, a large-scale cross-modal benchmark dataset for place recognition and metric depth estimation in unstructured natural environments, addressing the lack of such datasets for robotics research.

Authors: Joshua Knights, Joseph Reid, Kaushik Roy, David Hall, Mark Cox, Peyman Moghadam

When Does RL Help Medical VLMs? Disentangling Vision, SFT, and RL Gains

Abstract (English): Reinforcement learning (RL) is increasingly used to post-train medical Vision-Language Models (VLMs), yet it remains unclear whether RL improves medical visual reasoning or mainly sharpens behaviors already induced by supervised fine-tuning (SFT). We present a controlled study that disentangles these effects along three axes: vision, SFT, and RL. Using MedMNIST as a multi-modality testbed, we probe visual perception by benchmarking VLM vision towers against vision-only baselines, quantify reasoning support and sampling efficiency via Accuracy@1 versus Pass@K, and evaluate when RL closes the support gap and how gains transfer across modalities. We find that RL is most effective when the model already has non-trivial support (high Pass@K): it primarily sharpens the output distribution, improving Acc@1 and sampling efficiency, while SFT expands support and makes RL effective. Based on these findings, we propose a boundary-aware recipe and instantiate it by RL post-training an OctoMed-initialized model on a small, balanced subset of PMC multiple-choice VQA, achieving strong average performance across six medical VQA benchmarks.

摘要（中文）: 强化学习（RL）越来越多地被用于预训练后的医学视觉-语言模型（VLMs），但RL是否能真正提升医学视觉推理能力，还是仅仅优化了有监督微调（SFT）已经诱导的行为，尚不清楚。我们提出了一项受控研究，沿着三个轴：视觉、SFT和RL，来解耦这些影响。以MedMNIST作为多模态测试平台，我们通过将VLM视觉塔与纯视觉基线进行比较来探究视觉感知能力，通过Accuracy@1 与 Pass@K 来量化推理支持和采样效率，并评估RL何时能够弥合支持差距，以及收益如何在模态之间转移。我们发现，当模型已经具有非平凡的支持度（高Pass@K）时，RL最有效：它主要优化输出分布，提高 Acc@1 和采样效率，而 SFT 则扩展支持度，使 RL 生效。基于这些发现，我们提出了一种边界感知方案，并通过在PMC多选题 VQA 的一个小型、平衡子集上对一个OctoMed初始化的模型进行 RL 后训练来实例化该方案，从而在六个医学 VQA 基准测试中实现了强大的平均性能。

TLDR: This paper investigates the roles of vision, SFT, and RL in medical VLMs, finding that RL primarily sharpens existing knowledge induced by SFT, and proposes a new RL recipe for medical VQA achieving strong performance.

简要说明: 该论文研究了医学视觉语言模型中视觉、监督微调（SFT）和强化学习（RL）的作用，发现RL主要用于锐化SFT已诱导的现有知识，并提出了一种新的RL方法，在医学视觉问答方面取得了良好的效果。

Authors: Ahmadreza Jeddi, Kimia Shaban, Negin Baghbanzadeh, Natasha Sharan, Abhishek Moturu, Elham Dolatabadi, Babak Taati

Expanding LLM Agent Boundaries with Strategy-Guided Exploration

Abstract (English): Reinforcement learning (RL) has demonstrated notable success in post-training large language models (LLMs) as agents for tasks such as computer use, tool calling, and coding. However, exploration remains a central challenge in RL for LLM agents, especially as they operate in language-action spaces with complex observations and sparse outcome rewards. In this work, we address exploration for LLM agents by leveraging the ability of LLMs to plan and reason in language about the environment to shift exploration from low-level actions to higher-level language strategies. We thus propose Strategy-Guided Exploration (SGE), which first generates a concise natural-language strategy that describes what to do to make progress toward the goal, and then generates environment actions conditioned on that strategy. By exploring in the space of strategies rather than the space of actions, SGE induces structured and diverse exploration that targets different environment outcomes. To increase strategy diversity during RL, SGE introduces mixed-temperature sampling, which explores diverse strategies in parallel, along with a strategy reflection process that grounds strategy generation on the outcomes of previous strategies in the environment. Across UI interaction, tool-calling, coding, and embodied agent environments, SGE consistently outperforms exploration-focused RL baselines, improving both learning efficiency and final performance. We show that SGE enables the agent to learn to solve tasks too difficult for the base model.

摘要（中文）: 强化学习（RL）已证明在后训练大型语言模型（LLM）作为代理以完成诸如计算机使用、工具调用和编码等任务方面取得了显著成功。然而，探索仍然是LLM代理强化学习中的一个核心挑战，尤其是在它们于具有复杂观测和稀疏结果奖励的语言-动作空间中运行时。在这项工作中，我们通过利用LLM在语言中规划和推理环境的能力，将探索从低级动作转向更高级别的语言策略，从而解决LLM代理的探索问题。因此，我们提出了策略引导探索（SGE），它首先生成一个简洁的自然语言策略，描述为实现目标应该做什么，然后生成以该策略为条件的针对环境的动作。通过在策略空间而非动作空间中进行探索，SGE能够诱导结构化且多样化的探索，从而瞄准不同的环境结果。为了提高强化学习期间的策略多样性，SGE引入了混合温度采样，以便并行探索不同的策略，以及一个策略反思过程，该过程将策略生成建立在先前策略在环境中的结果之上。在用户界面交互、工具调用、编码和具身代理环境中，SGE始终优于以探索为中心的RL基线，提高了学习效率和最终性能。我们证明SGE使代理能够学习解决对于基础模型来说过于困难的任务。

TLDR: This paper introduces Strategy-Guided Exploration (SGE), a novel reinforcement learning approach that leverages LLMs to plan and reason at a high level, using language strategies to guide exploration in complex environments, and demonstrating improved performance across various tasks.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为策略引导探索 (SGE) 的新型强化学习方法，该方法利用大型语言模型在高层次上进行规划和推理，使用语言策略来指导在复杂环境中的探索，并在各种任务中展示了改进的性能。

Authors: Andrew Szot, Michael Kirchhof, Omar Attia, Alexander Toshev

Efficient RLVR Training via Weighted Mutual Information Data Selection

Abstract (English): Reinforcement learning (RL) plays a central role in improving the reasoning and alignment of large language models, yet its efficiency critically depends on how training data are selected. Existing online selection strategies predominantly rely on difficulty-based heuristics, favouring datapoints with intermediate success rates, implicitly equating difficulty with informativeness and neglecting epistemic uncertainty arising from limited evidence. We introduce InSight, an INformation-guided data SamplInG metHod for RL Training, grounded in a weighted mutual information objective. By modeling data outcomes with Bayesian latent success rates, we show that expected uncertainty reduction decomposes into complementary difficulty- and evidence-dependent components, revealing a fundamental limitation of difficulty-only selection. Leveraging this observation, InSight constructs a stable acquisition score based on the mean belief of datapoints' success rather than noisy sampled outcomes, and naturally extends to multi-rollout settings common in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). Extensive experiments demonstrate that InSight consistently achieves state-of-the-art performance and improves training efficiency, including a +1.41 average gain on Planning & Mathmatics benchmarks, +1.01 improvement on general reasoning, and up to ~2.2x acceleration, with negligible additional computational overhead.

摘要（中文）: 强化学习（RL）在提升大型语言模型的推理和对齐能力方面起着核心作用，但其效率关键取决于训练数据的选择方式。现有的在线选择策略主要依赖于基于难度的启发式算法，倾向于选择具有中间成功率的数据点，从而隐含地将难度等同于信息量，并忽略了因证据有限而产生的认知不确定性。我们引入了InSight，一种基于加权互信息目标的，用于强化学习训练的基于信息引导的数据采样方法（INformation-guided data SamplInG metHod）。通过使用贝叶斯潜在成功率对数据结果进行建模，我们证明了预期不确定性减少可以分解为互补的难度依赖和证据依赖成分，揭示了纯粹基于难度选择的根本局限性。基于这一观察，InSight构建了一个基于数据点成功率的平均信念的稳定获取分数，而非嘈杂的采样结果，并自然地扩展到使用可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）中常见的多次推出设置。大量的实验表明，InSight始终能够实现最先进的性能并提高训练效率，包括在计划与数学基准测试中平均获得+1.41的增益，在通用推理方面获得+1.01的改进，以及高达~2.2倍的加速，且计算开销几乎可以忽略不计。

TLDR: The paper introduces InSight, a reinforcement learning data selection method based on weighted mutual information, which enhances training efficiency by considering both difficulty and epistemic uncertainty, achieving state-of-the-art performance on reasoning and mathematics benchmarks.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种基于加权互信息的强化学习数据选择方法 InSight，它通过考虑难度和认知不确定性来提高训练效率，并在推理和数学基准测试中实现了最先进的性能。

Authors: Xinyu Zhou, Boyu Zhu, Haotian Zhang, Huiming Wang, Zhijiang Guo

TopoCurate:Modeling Interaction Topology for Tool-Use Agent Training

Abstract (English): Training tool-use agents typically relies on outcome-based filtering: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) on successful trajectories and Reinforcement Learning (RL) on pass-rate-selected tasks. However, this paradigm ignores interaction dynamics: successful trajectories may lack error recovery or exhibit redundancy, while pass rates fail to distinguish structurally informative tasks from trivial ones. We propose \textbf{TopoCurate}, an interaction-aware framework that projects multi-trial rollouts from the same task into a unified semantic quotient topology. By merging equivalent action-observation states, this projection transforms scattered linear trajectories into a structured manifold that explicitly captures how tool invocations and environmental responses drive the divergence between effective strategies and failure modes. Leveraging this representation, we introduce a dual-selection mechanism: for SFT, we prioritize trajectories demonstrating reflective recovery, semantic efficiency, and strategic diversity to mitigate covariate shift and mode collapse; for RL, we select tasks with high error branch ratios and strategic heterogeneity, maximizing gradient Signal-to-Noise Ratio to address vanishing signals in sparse-reward settings. Evaluations on BFCLv3 and Tau2 Bench show that TopoCurate achieves consistent gains of 4.2\% (SFT) and 6.9\% (RL) over state-of-the-art baselines. We will release the code and data soon for further investigations.

摘要（中文）: 训练工具使用智能体通常依赖于基于结果的过滤：在成功轨迹上进行有监督微调 (SFT)，并在通过率选定的任务上进行强化学习 (RL)。然而，这种范式忽略了交互动态性：成功轨迹可能缺乏错误恢复或表现出冗余，而通过率无法区分结构信息丰富的任务和简单的任务。我们提出了 \textbf{TopoCurate}，一个交互感知框架，将同一任务的多次试验展开轨迹投影到统一的语义商拓扑中。通过合并等价的动作-观察状态，这种投影将分散的线性轨迹转换为一个结构化的流形，明确地捕捉了工具调用和环境响应如何驱动有效策略和失败模式之间的差异。利用这种表示，我们引入了一种双重选择机制：对于 SFT，我们优先选择展现出反思性恢复、语义效率和策略多样性的轨迹，以减轻协变量偏移和模式崩溃；对于 RL，我们选择具有高错误分支率和策略异质性的任务，从而最大化梯度信噪比，以解决稀疏奖励设置中的梯度消失问题。在 BFCLv3 和 Tau2 Bench 上的评估表明，TopoCurate 相比于最先进的基线，实现了 4.2% (SFT) 和 6.9% (RL) 的稳定提升。我们将尽快发布代码和数据，以供进一步研究。

TLDR: TopoCurate enhances tool-use agent training by modeling interaction topology to prioritize informative trajectories for Supervised Fine-Tuning and tasks for Reinforcement Learning, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines on BFCLv3 and Tau2 benchmarks.

简要说明: TopoCurate通过建模交互拓扑来增强工具使用代理的训练，为监督微调优先考虑信息丰富的轨迹，为强化学习优先考虑任务，在BFCLv3和Tau2基准测试中优于最先进的基线。

Authors: Jinluan Yang, Yuxin Liu, Zhengyu Chen, Chengcheng Han, Yueqing Sun, Qi Gu, Hui Su, Xunliang Cai, Fei Wu, Kun Kuang

Streaming Real-Time Trajectory Prediction Using Endpoint-Aware Modeling

Abstract (English): Future trajectories of neighboring traffic agents have a significant influence on the path planning and decision-making of autonomous vehicles. While trajectory forecasting is a well-studied field, research mainly focuses on snapshot-based prediction, where each scenario is treated independently of its global temporal context. However, real-world autonomous driving systems need to operate in a continuous setting, requiring real-time processing of data streams with low latency and consistent predictions over successive timesteps. We leverage this continuous setting to propose a lightweight yet highly accurate streaming-based trajectory forecasting approach. We integrate valuable information from previous predictions with a novel endpoint-aware modeling scheme. Our temporal context propagation uses the trajectory endpoints of the previous forecasts as anchors to extract targeted scenario context encodings. Our approach efficiently guides its scene encoder to extract highly relevant context information without needing refinement iterations or segment-wise decoding. Our experiments highlight that our approach effectively relays information across consecutive timesteps. Unlike methods using multi-stage refinement processing, our approach significantly reduces inference latency, making it well-suited for real-world deployment. We achieve state-of-the-art streaming trajectory prediction results on the Argoverse~2 multi-agent and single-agent benchmarks, while requiring substantially fewer resources.

摘要（中文）: 相邻交通主体未来的轨迹对自动驾驶车辆的路径规划和决策具有重大影响。虽然轨迹预测是一个被充分研究的领域，但研究主要集中于基于快照的预测，其中每个场景都被独立于其全局时间上下文处理。然而，现实世界的自动驾驶系统需要在连续环境中运行，需要低延迟的实时数据流处理，以及在连续时间步长上保持一致的预测。我们利用这种连续环境，提出一种轻量级且高精度的基于流的轨迹预测方法。我们将来自先前预测的有价值的信息与一种新颖的端点感知建模方案相结合。我们的时间上下文传播使用先前预测的轨迹端点作为锚点，以提取有针对性的场景上下文编码。我们的方法有效地引导其场景编码器提取高度相关的上下文信息，而无需细化迭代或分段解码。我们的实验表明，我们的方法有效地在连续的时间步长之间传递信息。与使用多阶段细化处理的方法不同，我们的方法显著降低了推理延迟，使其非常适合现实世界的部署。我们在Argoverse~2多主体和单主体基准测试中取得了最先进的流轨迹预测结果，同时需要显著更少的资源。

TLDR: This paper introduces a real-time, streaming trajectory prediction method using endpoint-aware modeling for autonomous driving, achieving state-of-the-art results with reduced latency and resource requirements.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种实时、流式的轨迹预测方法，使用端点感知建模用于自动驾驶，在降低延迟和资源需求的同时，取得了最先进的成果。

Authors: Alexander Prutsch, David Schinagl, Horst Possegger

Shape-Interpretable Visual Self-Modeling Enables Geometry-Aware Continuum Robot Control

Abstract (English): Continuum robots possess high flexibility and redundancy, making them well suited for safe interaction in complex environments, yet their continuous deformation and nonlinear dynamics pose fundamental challenges to perception, modeling, and control. Existing vision-based control approaches often rely on end-to-end learning, achieving shape regulation without explicit awareness of robot geometry or its interaction with the environment. Here, we introduce a shape-interpretable visual self-modeling framework for continuum robots that enables geometry-aware control. Robot shapes are encoded from multi-view planar images using a Bezier-curve representation, transforming visual observations into a compact and physically meaningful shape space that uniquely characterizes the robot's three-dimensional configuration. Based on this representation, neural ordinary differential equations are employed to self-model both shape and end-effector dynamics directly from data, enabling hybrid shape-position control without analytical models or dense body markers. The explicit geometric structure of the learned shape space allows the robot to reason about its body and surroundings, supporting environment-aware behaviors such as obstacle avoidance and self-motion while maintaining end-effector objectives. Experiments on a cable-driven continuum robot demonstrate accurate shape-position regulation and tracking, with shape errors within 1.56% of image resolution and end-effector errors within 2% of robot length, as well as robust performance in constrained environments. By elevating visual shape representations from two-dimensional observations to an interpretable three-dimensional self-model, this work establishes a principled alternative to vision-based end-to-end control and advances autonomous, geometry-aware manipulation for continuum robots.

摘要（中文）: 连续体机器人具有高度的灵活性和冗余性，使其非常适合在复杂环境中进行安全交互，但其连续变形和非线性动力学对感知、建模和控制提出了根本性的挑战。现有的基于视觉的控制方法通常依赖于端到端学习，在没有明确感知机器人几何结构或其与环境交互的情况下实现形状调节。在这里，我们介绍了一种用于连续体机器人的形状可解释的视觉自建模框架，该框架能够实现几何感知控制。机器人形状通过多视角平面图像，使用贝塞尔曲线表示进行编码，将视觉观测转换为紧凑且具有物理意义的形状空间，该空间唯一地表征了机器人的三维构型。基于这种表示，使用神经常微分方程直接从数据中对形状和末端执行器动力学进行自建模，从而无需解析模型或密集本体标记即可实现混合形状-位置控制。学习到的形状空间的显式几何结构允许机器人推断其自身和周围环境，在保持末端执行器目标的同时，支持环境感知行为，例如避障和自运动。在缆索驱动的连续体机器人上的实验表明，能够实现精确的形状-位置调节和跟踪，形状误差在图像分辨率的1.56%以内，末端执行器误差在机器人长度的2%以内，并在受限环境中表现出鲁棒的性能。通过将视觉形状表示从二维观测提升到可解释的三维自模型，这项工作为基于视觉的端到端控制建立了一种有原则的替代方案，并推进了连续体机器人的自主、几何感知操作。

TLDR: This paper presents a shape-interpretable visual self-modeling framework for continuum robots, enabling geometry-aware control using Bezier curves and neural ODEs for shape and end-effector dynamics modeling, achieving robust performance in complex environments.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一种用于连续机器人的形状可解释的视觉自建模框架，通过使用 Bezier 曲线和神经 ODE 对形状和末端执行器动力学进行建模，实现了几何感知的控制，并在复杂环境中实现了强大的性能。

Authors: Peng Yu, Xin Wang, Ning Tan

Learning Thermal-Aware Locomotion Policies for an Electrically-Actuated Quadruped Robot

Abstract (English): Electrically-actuated quadrupedal robots possess high mobility on complex terrains, but their motors tend to accumulate heat under high-torque cyclic loads, potentially triggering overheat protection and limiting long-duration tasks. This work proposes a thermal-aware control method that incorporates motor temperatures into reinforcement learning locomotion policies and introduces thermal-constraint rewards to prevent temperature exceedance. Real-world experiments on the Unitree A1 demonstrate that, under a fixed 3 kg payload, the baseline policy triggers overheat protection and stops within approximately 7 minutes, whereas the proposed method can operate continuously for over 27 minutes without thermal interruptions while maintaining comparable command-tracking performance, thereby enhancing sustainable operational capability.

摘要（中文）: 电动四足机器人具有在复杂地形上的高机动性，但其电机在高扭矩循环载荷下容易积聚热量，可能触发过热保护并限制长时间任务。 本文提出了一种热感知控制方法，将电机温度纳入强化学习运动策略中，并引入热约束奖励以防止温度超标。 在宇树A1上的真实实验表明，在3公斤固定有效载荷下，基线策略会在大约7分钟内触发过热保护并停止，而所提出的方法可以连续运行超过27分钟而不会发生热中断，同时保持相当的指令跟踪性能，从而提高可持续运行能力。

TLDR: This paper introduces a reinforcement learning-based thermal-aware control method for quadruped robots that prevents motor overheating, enabling longer continuous operation compared to baseline policies. Real-world experiments on a Unitree A1 robot demonstrate significantly improved endurance.

Authors: Letian Qian, Yuhang Wan, Shuhan Wang, Xin Luo

Towards Robot Skill Learning and Adaptation with Gaussian Processes

Abstract (English): General robot skill adaptation requires expressive representations robust to varying task configurations. While recent learning-based skill adaptation methods refined via Reinforcement Learning (RL), have shown success, existing skill models often lack sufficient representational capacity for anything beyond minor environmental changes. In contrast, Gaussian Process (GP)-based skill modelling provides an expressive representation with useful analytical properties; however, adaptation of GP-based skills remains underexplored. This paper proposes a novel, robust skill adaptation framework that utilises GPs with sparse via-points for compact and expressive modelling. The model considers the trajectory's poses and leverages its first and second analytical derivatives to preserve the skill's kinematic profile. We present three adaptation methods to cater for the variability between initial and observed configurations. Firstly, an optimisation agent that adjusts the path's via-points while preserving the demonstration velocity. Second, a behaviour cloning agent trained to replicate output trajectories from the optimisation agent. Lastly, an RL agent that has learnt to modify via-points whilst maintaining the kinematic profile and enabling online capabilities. Evaluated across three tasks (drawer opening, cube-pushing and bar manipulation) in both simulation and hardware, our proposed methods outperform every benchmark in success rates. Furthermore, the results demonstrate that the GP-based representation enables all three methods to attain high cosine similarity and low velocity magnitude errors, indicating strong preservation of the kinematic profile. Overall, our formulation provides a compact representation capable of adapting to large deviations from a single demonstrated skill.

摘要（中文）: 通用机器人技能适应需要对各种任务配置具有鲁棒性的、富有表现力的表示。虽然最近通过强化学习（RL）改进的基于学习的技能适应方法已取得成功，但现有的技能模型通常缺乏足够的表示能力，无法应对微小的环境变化之外的情况。相比之下，基于高斯过程（GP）的技能建模提供了一种富有表现力的表示，并具有有用的分析特性；然而，基于 GP 的技能适应仍未得到充分探索。本文提出了一种新颖、稳健的技能适应框架，该框架利用带有稀疏路标点的高斯过程进行紧凑而富有表现力的建模。该模型考虑了轨迹的姿态，并利用其一阶和二阶解析导数来保留技能的运动学轮廓。我们提出了三种适应方法，以适应初始配置和观察到的配置之间的差异。首先，一个优化代理，调整路径的路标点，同时保留演示速度。其次，一个行为克隆代理，经过训练以复制来自优化代理的输出轨迹。最后，一个强化学习代理，已学会修改路标点，同时保持运动学轮廓并实现在线能力。在仿真和硬件中对三项任务（抽屉打开、立方体推动和杆操作）进行评估，我们提出的方法在成功率方面优于所有基准测试。此外，结果表明，基于 GP 的表示使所有三种方法都能获得高的余弦相似度和低的运动幅度误差，表明对运动学轮廓的强烈保留。总的来说，我们的公式提供了一种紧凑的表示，能够适应与单个演示技能的较大偏差。

TLDR: This paper introduces a novel framework for robot skill adaptation using Gaussian Processes (GPs) with sparse via-points, demonstrating improved performance in preserving kinematic profiles across various manipulation tasks compared to existing methods.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种新颖的机器人技能自适应框架，该框架使用带有稀疏途经点的 Gaussian Processes (GPs)，与现有方法相比，在各种操作任务中展示了更好的保留运动学特征的能力。

Authors: A K M Nadimul Haque, Fouad Sukkar, Sheila Sujipto, Cedric Le Gentil, Marc G. Carmichael, Teresa Vidal-Calleja

Multimodal Adversarial Quality Policy for Safe Grasping

Abstract (English): Vision-guided robot grasping based on Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) generalizes well but poses safety risks in the Human-Robot Interaction (HRI). Recent works solved it by designing benign adversarial attacks and patches with RGB modality, yet depth-independent characteristics limit their effectiveness on RGBD modality. In this work, we propose the Multimodal Adversarial Quality Policy (MAQP) to realize multimodal safe grasping. Our framework introduces two key components. First, the Heterogeneous Dual-Patch Optimization Scheme (HDPOS) mitigates the distribution discrepancy between RGB and depth modalities in patch generation by adopting modality-specific initialization strategies, employing a Gaussian distribution for depth patches and a uniform distribution for RGB patches, while jointly optimizing both modalities under a unified objective function. Second, the Gradient-Level Modality Balancing Strategy (GLMBS) is designed to resolve the optimization imbalance from RGB and Depth patches in patch shape adaptation by reweighting gradient contributions based on per-channel sensitivity analysis and applying distance-adaptive perturbation bounds. We conduct extensive experiments on the benchmark datasets and a cobot, showing the effectiveness of MAQP.

摘要（中文）: 基于深度神经网络（DNNs）的视觉引导机器人抓取具有良好的泛化能力，但在人机交互（HRI）中存在安全风险。最近的研究通过设计良性对抗攻击和RGB模态的对抗补丁来解决这个问题，但深度无关特性限制了它们在RGBD模态上的有效性。 在这项工作中，我们提出了多模态对抗质量策略（MAQP）来实现多模态安全抓取。 我们的框架引入了两个关键组成部分。 首先，异构双补丁优化方案（HDPOS）通过采用模态特定的初始化策略，缓解了RGB和深度模态在补丁生成中的分布差异，即对深度补丁采用高斯分布，对RGB补丁采用均匀分布，并在统一的目标函数下联合优化两种模态。 其次，梯度级模态平衡策略（GLMBS）旨在解决补丁形状调整中RGB和深度补丁的优化不平衡问题，通过基于每通道敏感性分析的梯度权重调整以及应用距离自适应扰动界限来实现平衡。 我们在基准数据集和协作机器人上进行了大量实验，验证了MAQP的有效性。

TLDR: This paper proposes a Multimodal Adversarial Quality Policy (MAQP) framework with Heterogeneous Dual-Patch Optimization Scheme (HDPOS) and Gradient-Level Modality Balancing Strategy (GLMBS) for safe robot grasping using RGBD data, mitigating distribution discrepancy and optimizing imbalances between RGB and depth modalities.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种多模态对抗质量策略（MAQP）框架，具有异构双补丁优化方案（HDPOS）和梯度级别模态平衡策略（GLMBS），用于使用RGBD数据进行安全机器人抓取，缓解了RGB和深度模态之间的分布差异并优化了不平衡。

Authors: Kunlin Xie Chenghao Li Haolan Zhang, Nak Young Chong

Align and Filter: Improving Performance in Asynchronous On-Policy RL

Abstract (English): Distributed training and increasing the gradient update frequency are practical strategies to accelerate learning and improve performance, but both exacerbate a central challenge: \textit{policy lag}, which is the mismatch between the behavior policy generating data and the learning policy being updated. Policy lag can hinder the scaling of on-policy learning algorithms to larger problems. In this paper, we identify the sources of policy lag caused by distributed learning and high update frequency. We use the findings to propose \textit{total Variation-based Advantage aligned Constrained policy Optimization (\methodacronym)} as a practical approach to mitigate policy lag. We empirically validate our method and show that it offers better robustness to policy lag in classic RL tasks and a modern RL for LLM math reasoning task.

摘要（中文）: 分布式训练和提高梯度更新频率是加速学习和改善性能的有效策略，但两者都加剧了一个核心挑战：\textit{策略滞后}，即产生数据的行为策略与正在更新的学习策略之间的不匹配。策略滞后会阻碍在线策略学习算法扩展到更大的问题。在本文中，我们确定了分布式学习和高更新频率导致的策略滞后的来源。我们利用这些发现提出了\textit{基于总变差的优势对齐约束策略优化(\methodacronym)}，作为一种减轻策略滞后的实用方法。我们通过实验验证了我们的方法，并表明它在经典的强化学习任务和用于大型语言模型数学推理的现代强化学习任务中，都能够更好地应对策略滞后。

TLDR: This paper proposes a new method, TV-AC, to mitigate policy lag in asynchronous on-policy RL by aligning advantages and constraining policy optimization, showing improved robustness in classic RL tasks and LLM math reasoning.

Authors: Homayoun Honari, Roger Creus Castanyer, Michael Przystupa, Michael Noukhovitch, Pablo Samuel Castro, Glen Berseth

Spherical Latent Motion Prior for Physics-Based Simulated Humanoid Control

Abstract (English): Learning motion priors for physics-based humanoid control is an active research topic. Existing approaches mainly include variational autoencoders (VAE) and adversarial motion priors (AMP). VAE introduces information loss, and random latent sampling may sometimes produce invalid behaviors. AMP suffers from mode collapse and struggles to capture diverse motion skills. We present the Spherical Latent Motion Prior (SLMP), a two-stage method for learning motion priors. In the first stage, we train a high-quality motion tracking controller. In the second stage, we distill the tracking controller into a spherical latent space. A combination of distillation, a discriminator, and a discriminator-guided local semantic consistency constraint shapes a structured latent action space, allowing stable random sampling without information loss. To evaluate SLMP, we collect a two-hour human combat motion capture dataset and show that SLMP preserves fine motion detail without information loss, and random sampling yields semantically valid and stable behaviors. When applied to a two-agent physics-based combat task, SLMP produces human-like and physically plausible combat behaviors only using simple rule-based rewards. Furthermore, SLMP generalizes across different humanoid robot morphologies, demonstrating its transferability beyond a single simulated avatar.

摘要（中文）: 基于物理的人形机器人控制学习运动先验是一个活跃的研究课题。现有方法主要包括变分自编码器（VAE）和对抗运动先验（AMP）。VAE会引入信息损失，且随机潜在采样有时可能产生无效行为。AMP则面临模式崩溃问题，难以捕捉多样的运动技能。我们提出了球面潜在运动先验（SLMP），一种用于学习运动先验的两阶段方法。在第一阶段，我们训练一个高质量的运动追踪控制器。在第二阶段，我们将追踪控制器提炼到一个球面潜在空间中。蒸馏、判别器以及判别器引导的局部语义一致性约束相结合，塑造了一个结构化的潜在动作空间，从而实现稳定的随机采样且不损失信息。为了评估SLMP，我们收集了一个两小时的人类格斗动作捕捉数据集，并表明SLMP在不损失信息的情况下保留了精细的运动细节，并且随机采样产生了语义上有效且稳定的行为。当应用于双智能体基于物理的格斗任务时，SLMP仅使用简单的基于规则的奖励就能产生类人且物理上合理的格斗行为。此外，SLMP可以泛化到不同的人形机器人形态，展示了其超越单个模拟化身的迁移能力。

TLDR: The paper introduces Spherical Latent Motion Prior (SLMP), a two-stage method for learning motion priors for physics-based humanoid control, addressing limitations of VAE and AMP by using distillation and a spherical latent space to generate stable and semantically valid motions.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了球型潜在运动先验（SLMP），一种用于学习基于物理的人形控制运动先验的两阶段方法，通过使用蒸馏和球型潜在空间来生成稳定且语义有效的运动，从而克服了VAE和AMP的局限性。
