Authors: Jing Tan, Weisheng Xu, Xiangrui Jiang, Jiaxi Zhang, Kun Yang, Kai Wu, Jiaqi Xiong, Shiting Chen, Yangfan Li, Yixiao Feng, Yuetong Fang, Yujia Zou, Yiqun Song, Renjing Xu

Information-Theoretic Framework for Self-Adapting Model Predictive Controllers

Abstract (English): Model Predictive Control (MPC) is a vital technique for autonomous systems, like Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs), enabling optimized motion planning. However, traditional MPC struggles to adapt to real-time changes such as dynamic obstacles and shifting system dynamics, lacking inherent mechanisms for self-monitoring and adaptive optimization. Here, we introduce Entanglement Learning (EL), an information-theoretic framework that enhances MPC adaptability through an Information Digital Twin (IDT). The IDT monitors and quantifies, in bits, the information flow between MPC inputs, control actions, and UAV behavior. By introducing new information-theoretic metrics we call entanglement metrics, it tracks variations in these dependencies. These metrics measure the mutual information between the optimizer's input, its control actions, and the resulting UAV dynamics, enabling a deeper understanding of their interrelationships. This allows the IDT to detect performance deviations and generate real-time adaptive signals to recalibrate MPC parameters, preserving stability. Unlike traditional MPC, which relies on error-based feedback, this dual-feedback approach leverages information flow for proactive adaptation to evolving conditions. Scalable and leveraging existing infrastructure, this framework improves MPC reliability and robustness across diverse scenarios, extending beyond UAV control to any MPC implementation requiring adaptive performance.

摘要（中文）: 模型预测控制(MPC)是无人机(UAV)等自主系统的关键技术，能够实现优化的运动规划。然而，传统的MPC难以适应实时变化，例如动态障碍物和不断变化的系统动力学，缺乏用于自我监控和自适应优化的内在机制。在此，我们引入缠结学习(EL)，这是一种信息论框架，通过信息数字孪生(IDT)增强MPC的适应性。IDT以比特为单位，监控并量化MPC输入、控制行为和无人机行为之间的信息流动。通过引入我们称之为缠结度量的新型信息论指标，它可以跟踪这些依赖关系的变化。这些度量指标衡量了优化器的输入、其控制行为以及由此产生的无人机动力学之间的互信息，从而能够更深入地了解它们之间的相互关系。这使IDT能够检测性能偏差，并生成实时自适应信号以重新校准MPC参数，从而保持稳定性。与依赖于基于误差的反馈的传统MPC不同，这种双反馈方法利用信息流来主动适应不断变化的环境。该框架具有可扩展性并利用现有基础设施，提高了MPC在各种场景中的可靠性和鲁棒性，并将应用范围从无人机控制扩展到任何需要自适应性能的MPC实现。

TLDR: This paper introduces Entanglement Learning (EL), an information-theoretic framework using an Information Digital Twin (IDT) to enhance Model Predictive Control (MPC) adaptability by monitoring and quantifying information flow and generating adaptive signals to recalibrate MPC parameters.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为纠缠学习（EL）的信息论框架，该框架使用信息数字孪生 (IDT) 通过监控和量化信息流并生成自适应信号以重新校准 MPC 参数，从而增强模型预测控制 (MPC) 的适应性。

Authors: Wael Hafez, Amir Nazeri

Selection as Power: Constrained Reinforcement for Bounded Decision Authority

Abstract (English): Selection as Power argued that upstream selection authority, rather than internal objective misalignment, constitutes a primary source of risk in high-stakes agentic systems. However, the original framework was static: governance constraints bounded selection power but did not adapt over time. In this work, we extend the framework to dynamic settings by introducing incentivized selection governance, where reinforcement updates are applied to scoring and reducer parameters under externally enforced sovereignty constraints. We formalize selection as a constrained reinforcement process in which parameter updates are projected onto governance-defined feasible sets, preventing concentration beyond prescribed bounds. Across multiple regulated financial scenarios, unconstrained reinforcement consistently collapses into deterministic dominance under repeated feedback, especially at higher learning rates. In contrast, incentivized governance enables adaptive improvement while maintaining bounded selection concentration. Projection-based constraints transform reinforcement from irreversible lock-in into controlled adaptation, with governance debt quantifying the tension between optimization pressure and authority bounds. These results demonstrate that learning dynamics can coexist with structural diversity when sovereignty constraints are enforced at every update step, offering a principled approach to integrating reinforcement into high-stakes agentic systems without surrendering bounded selection authority.

摘要（中文）: 选择即权力理论认为，上游选择权而非内部目标错位是高风险代理系统风险的主要来源。然而，最初的框架是静态的：治理约束限制了选择权，但不会随时间适应。在这项工作中，我们通过引入激励性选择治理，将该框架扩展到动态环境，其中在外部强制的主权约束下，强化更新应用于评分和归约器参数。 我们将选择形式化为约束强化过程，其中参数更新被投影到治理定义的feasible sets上，从而防止超出规定范围的集中。在多个受监管的金融场景中，不受约束的强化在重复反馈下持续崩溃为确定性的优势，尤其是在更高的学习率下。相比之下，激励性治理能够在保持有界选择集中的同时实现自适应改进。 基于投影的约束将强化从不可逆转的锁定转变为受控适应，治理债务量化了优化压力和权力边界之间的张力。这些结果表明，当在每个更新步骤强制执行主权约束时，学习动态可以与结构多样性共存，从而为将强化整合到高风险代理系统中提供了一种原则性方法，而无需放弃有界选择权。

TLDR: This paper extends the "Selection as Power" framework by introducing incentivized selection governance with constrained reinforcement learning, preventing deterministic dominance and enabling adaptive improvement in high-stakes agentic systems.

简要说明: 本文扩展了“选择即权力”框架，引入了激励选择治理和约束强化学习，防止了确定性支配，并在高风险自主系统中实现了自适应改进。

Authors: Jose Manuel de la Chica Rodriguez, Juan Manuel Vera Díaz

Agentic Code Reasoning

Abstract (English): Can LLM agents explore codebases and reason about code semantics without executing the code? We study this capability, which we call agentic code reasoning, and introduce semi-formal reasoning: a structured prompting methodology that requires agents to construct explicit premises, trace execution paths, and derive formal conclusions. Unlike unstructured chain-of-thought, semi-formal reasoning acts as a certificate: the agent cannot skip cases or make unsupported claims. We evaluate across three tasks (patch equivalence verification, fault localization, and code question answering) and show that semi-formal reasoning consistently improves accuracy on all of them. For patch equivalence, accuracy improves from 78% to 88% on curated examples and reaches 93% on real-world agent-generated patches, approaching the reliability needed for execution-free RL reward signals. For code question answering on RubberDuckBench Mohammad et al. (2026), semi-formal reasoning achieves 87% accuracy. For fault localization on Defects4J Just et al. (2014), semi-formal reasoning improves Top-5 accuracy by 5 percentage points over standard reasoning. These results demonstrate that structured agentic reasoning enables meaningful semantic code analysis without execution, opening practical applications in RL training pipelines, code review, and static program analysis.

摘要（中文）: 大型语言模型代理能否在不执行代码的情况下探索代码库并推理代码语义？我们研究了这种能力，称之为代理式代码推理，并引入半形式化推理：一种结构化的提示方法，要求代理构建明确的前提、追踪执行路径并推导正式结论。与非结构化的思维链不同，半形式化推理充当一种证书：代理不能跳过案例或做出无根据的声明。我们在三个任务（补丁等价性验证、故障定位和代码问答）上进行了评估，结果表明半形式化推理始终提高所有任务的准确性。对于补丁等价性，在精心设计的示例上，准确率从 78% 提高到 88%，在现实世界的代理生成补丁上达到 93%，接近于无执行强化学习奖励信号所需的可靠性。对于 RubberDuckBench Mohammad et al. (2026) 上的代码问答，半形式化推理达到了 87% 的准确率。对于 Defects4J Just et al. (2014) 上的故障定位，半形式化推理比标准推理提高了 5 个百分点的 Top-5 准确率。这些结果表明，结构化的agent式推理能够在无需执行的情况下进行有意义的语义代码分析，为强化学习训练管道、代码审查和静态程序分析开辟了实际应用。

TLDR: This paper introduces "agentic code reasoning" using a structured prompting method called "semi-formal reasoning" to enable LLM agents to analyze code semantics without execution, showing improvements in patch equivalence, fault localization, and code QA.

简要说明: 这篇论文介绍了“代理式代码推理”，使用一种名为“半形式推理”的结构化提示方法，使 LLM 代理能够在不执行代码的情况下分析代码语义，并在补丁等价性、故障定位和代码问答方面显示出改进。

Authors: Shubham Ugare, Satish Chandra

Non-verbal Real-time Human-AI Interaction in Constrained Robotic Environments

Abstract (English): We study the ongoing debate regarding the statistical fidelity of AI-generated data compared to human-generated data in the context of non-verbal communication using full body motion. Concretely, we ask if contemporary generative models move beyond surface mimicry to participate in the silent, but expressive dialogue of body language. We tackle this question by introducing the first framework that generates a natural non-verbal interaction between Human and AI in real-time from 2D body keypoints. Our experiments utilize four lightweight architectures which run at up to 100 FPS on an NVIDIA Orin Nano, effectively closing the perception-action loop needed for natural Human-AI interaction. We trained on 437 human video clips and demonstrated that pretraining on synthetically-generated sequences reduces motion errors significantly, without sacrificing speed. Yet, a measurable reality gap persists. When the best model is evaluated on keypoints extracted from cutting-edge text-to-video systems, such as SORA and VEO, we observe that performance drops on SORA-generated clips. However, it degrades far less on VEO, suggesting that temporal coherence, not image fidelity, drives real-world performance. Our results demonstrate that statistically distinguishable differences persist between Human and AI motion.

摘要（中文）: 我们研究了关于人工智能生成数据与人类生成数据在非语言交流方面（使用全身动作）的统计保真度方面的持续争论。具体而言，我们探讨了当代的生成模型是否超越了表面模仿，参与到肢体语言这种无声但富有表现力的对话中。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了第一个框架，该框架能够从2D人体关键点实时生成人与AI之间的自然非语言互动。我们的实验使用了四种轻量级架构，它们在NVIDIA Orin Nano上以高达100 FPS的速度运行，有效闭环了自然人机互动所需的感知-行动循环。我们利用437个人类视频片段进行了训练，并证明对合成生成的序列进行预训练可以显著减少运动误差，同时不牺牲速度。然而，仍然存在可测量的现实差距。当在从最先进的文本到视频系统（如SORA和VEO）提取的关键点上评估最佳模型时，我们观察到SORA生成片段上的性能下降。然而，在VEO上的性能下降幅度远小于SORA，表明时间连贯性而非图像保真度驱动了现实世界中的性能。我们的结果表明，人类与AI运动之间仍然存在统计上可区分的差异。

TLDR: This paper introduces a real-time Human-AI interaction framework using non-verbal communication based on 2D body keypoints and shows performance differences when the AI interacts with real human motion versus motion generated by text-to-video models like SORA and VEO.

Federated Agentic AI for Wireless Networks: Fundamentals, Approaches, and Applications

Abstract (English): Agentic artificial intelligence (AI) presents a promising pathway toward realizing autonomous and self-improving wireless network services. However, resource-constrained, widely distributed, and data-heterogeneous nature of wireless networks poses significant challenges to existing agentic AI that relies on centralized architectures, leading to high communication overhead, privacy risks, and non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID) data. Federated learning (FL) has the potential to improve the overall loop of agentic AI through collaborative local learning and parameter sharing without exchanging raw data. This paper proposes new federated agentic AI approaches for wireless networks. We first summarize fundamentals of agentic AI and mainstream FL types. Then, we illustrate how each FL type can strengthen a specific component of agentic AI's loop. Moreover, we conduct a case study on using FRL to improve the performance of agentic AI's action decision in low-altitude wireless networks (LAWNs). Finally, we provide a conclusion and discuss future research directions.

摘要（中文）: 主动式人工智能（AI）为实现自主和自我改进的无线网络服务提供了一条充满希望的途径。然而，无线网络资源受限、广泛分布和数据异构的特性，对现有依赖集中式架构的主动式AI提出了重大挑战，导致了高通信开销、隐私风险以及非独立同分布（non-IID）数据。联邦学习（FL）有潜力通过协同本地学习和参数共享而不交换原始数据，来改善主动式AI的整体循环。本文提出了用于无线网络的新型联邦主动式AI方法。我们首先总结了主动式AI和主流FL类型的基础知识。然后，我们阐述了每种FL类型如何加强主动式AI循环中的特定组件。此外，我们进行了一个案例研究，探讨如何使用FRL来提高主动式AI在低空无线网络（LAWNs）中行动决策的性能。最后，我们给出了结论并讨论了未来的研究方向。

CeProAgents: A Hierarchical Agents System for Automated Chemical Process Development

Abstract (English): The development of chemical processes, a cornerstone of chemical engineering, presents formidable challenges due to its multi-faceted nature, integrating specialized knowledge, conceptual design, and parametric simulation. Capitalizing on this, we propose CeProAgents, a hierarchical multi-agent system designed to automate the development of chemical process through collaborative division of labor. Our architecture comprises three specialized agent cohorts focused on knowledge, concept, and parameter respectively. To effectively adapt to the inherent complexity of chemical tasks, each cohort employs a novel hybrid architecture that integrates dynamic agent chatgroups with structured agentic workflows. To rigorously evaluate the system, we establish CeProBench, a multi-dimensional benchmark structured around three core pillars of chemical engineering. We design six distinct types of tasks across these dimensions to holistically assess the comprehensive capabilities of the system in chemical process development. The results not only confirm the effectiveness and superiority of our proposed approach but also reveal the transformative potential as well as the current boundaries of Large Language Models (LLMs) for industrial chemical engineering.

S5-HES Agent: Society 5.0-driven Agentic Framework to Democratize Smart Home Environment Simulation

Abstract (English): The smart home is a key domain within the Society 5.0 vision for a human-centered society. Smart home technologies rapidly evolve, and research should diversify while remaining aligned with Society 5.0 objectives. Democratizing smart home research would engage a broader community of innovators beyond traditional limited experts. This shift necessitates inclusive simulation frameworks that support research across diverse fields in industry and academia. However, existing smart home simulators require significant technical expertise, offer limited adaptability, and lack automated evolution, thereby failing to meet the holistic needs of Society 5.0. These constraints impede researchers from efficiently conducting simulations and experiments for security, energy, health, climate, and socio-economic research. To address these challenges, this paper presents the Society 5.0-driven Smart Home Environment Simulator Agent (S5-HES Agent), an agentic simulation framework that transforms traditional smart home simulation through autonomous AI orchestration. The framework coordinates specialized agents through interchangeable large language models (LLMs), enabling natural-language-driven end-to-end smart home simulation configuration without programming expertise. A retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipeline with semantic, keyword, and hybrid search retrieves smart home knowledge. Comprehensive evaluation on S5-HES Agent demonstrates that the RAG pipeline achieves near-optimal retrieval fidelity, simulated device behaviour and threat scenarios align with real-world IoT datasets, and simulation engine scales predictably across home configurations, establishing a stable foundation for Society 5.0 smart home research. Source code is available under the MIT License at https://github.com/AsiriweLab/S5-HES-Agent.

摘要（中文）: 智能家居是面向以人为本社会的Society 5.0愿景中的一个关键领域。智能家居技术发展迅速，研究应该多样化，同时与Society 5.0的目标保持一致。普及智能家居研究将能够吸引更广泛的创新者社区，而不仅限于传统的有限专家。这种转变需要包容性的模拟框架，以支持工业界和学术界不同领域的研究。然而，现有的智能家居模拟器需要大量的技术专长，适应性有限，并且缺乏自动化演进，因此无法满足 Society 5.0 的整体需求。这些限制阻碍了研究人员有效进行安全、能源、健康、气候和社会经济研究的模拟和实验。为了应对这些挑战，本文提出了由Society 5.0驱动的智能家居环境模拟器代理（S5-HES Agent），这是一种基于代理的模拟框架，通过自主AI编排来变革传统的智能家居模拟。该框架通过可互换的大型语言模型（LLM）协调专业代理，无需编程专业知识即可实现自然语言驱动的端到端智能家居模拟配置。具有语义、关键词和混合搜索的检索增强生成（RAG）流水线用于检索智能家居知识。对S5-HES Agent的全面评估表明，RAG流水线实现了接近最优的检索保真度，模拟的设备行为和威胁场景与现实世界的物联网数据集一致，并且模拟引擎可以跨家庭配置进行可预测的扩展，从而为Society 5.0智能家居研究奠定了稳定的基础。源代码在 MIT 许可证下提供，网址为 https://github.com/AsiriweLab/S5-HES-Agent。

Authors: Akila Siriweera, Janani Rangila, Keitaro Naruse, Incheon Paik, Isuru Jayanada

Beyond Reward: A Bounded Measure of Agent Environment Coupling

Abstract (English): Real-world reinforcement learning (RL) agents operate in closed-loop systems where actions shape future observations, making reliable deployment under distribution shifts a persistent challenge. Existing monitoring relies on reward or task metrics, capturing outcomes but missing early coupling failures. We introduce bipredictability (P) as the ratio of shared information in the observation, action, outcome loop to the total available information, a principled, real time measure of interaction effectiveness with provable bounds, comparable across tasks. An auxiliary monitor, the Information Digital Twin (IDT), computes P and its diagnostic components from the interaction stream. We evaluate SAC and PPO agents on MuJoCo HalfCheetah under eight agent, and environment-side perturbations across 168 trials. Under nominal operation, agents exhibit P = 0.33 plus minus 0.02, below the classical bound of 0.5, revealing an informational cost of action selection. The IDT detects 89.3% of perturbations versus 44.0% for reward based monitoring, with 4.4x lower median latency. Bipredictability enables early detection of interaction degradation before performance drops and provides a prerequisite signal for closed loop self regulation in deployed RL systems.

摘要（中文）: 真实世界强化学习(RL)智能体在闭环系统中运行，其行为会塑造未来的观测，这使得在分布偏移下可靠部署成为一个持续的挑战。现有的监控依赖于奖励或任务指标，捕捉了结果，但遗漏了早期耦合失败。我们引入双向可预测性 (P)，作为观测、行为、结果循环中共享信息与可用总信息之比，它是一种有原则的、实时的交互有效性度量，具有可证明的界限，且在不同任务之间具有可比性。辅助监控，信息数字孪生 (IDT)，从交互流计算 P 及其诊断组件。我们评估了 SAC 和 PPO 智能体在 MuJoCo HalfCheetah 环境下，在 168 次试验中涉及八种智能体和环境侧扰动的情况。在标称运行下，智能体的 P 值为 0.33 正负 0.02，低于经典的 0.5 界限，表明了行为选择的信息成本。相对于基于奖励的监控（检测率 44.0%），IDT 检测到 89.3% 的扰动，中值延迟降低了 4.4 倍。双向可预测性能够在性能下降之前，提前检测到交互退化，并为已部署 RL 系统中的闭环自调节提供必要的信号。

TLDR: This paper introduces 'bipredictability' (P) as a novel metric to monitor the effectiveness of agent-environment interaction in RL, demonstrating its utility in detecting performance degradation earlier and more reliably than reward-based monitoring.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种名为“双向可预测性 (P)”的新指标，用于监测 RL 中智能体与环境交互的有效性，并展示了其在检测性能下降方面比基于奖励的监测更早、更可靠的效用。

Authors: Wael Hafez, Cameron Reid, Amit Nazeri

OnlineX: Unified Online 3D Reconstruction and Understanding with Active-to-Stable State Evolution

Abstract (English): Recent advances in generalizable 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have enabled rapid 3D scene reconstruction within seconds, eliminating the need for per-scene optimization. However, existing methods primarily follow an offline reconstruction paradigm, lacking the capacity for continuous reconstruction, which limits their applicability to online scenarios such as robotics and VR/AR. In this paper, we introduce OnlineX, a feed-forward framework that reconstructs both 3D visual appearance and language fields in an online manner using only streaming images. A key challenge in online formulation is the cumulative drift issue, which is rooted in the fundamental conflict between two opposing roles of the memory state: an active role that constantly refreshes to capture high-frequency local geometry, and a stable role that conservatively accumulates and preserves the long-term global structure. To address this, we introduce a decoupled active-to-stable state evolution paradigm. Our framework decouples the memory state into a dedicated active state and a persistent stable state, and then cohesively fuses the information from the former into the latter to achieve both fidelity and stability. Moreover, we jointly model visual appearance and language fields and incorporate an implicit Gaussian fusion module to enhance reconstruction quality. Experiments on mainstream datasets demonstrate that our method consistently outperforms prior work in novel view synthesis and semantic understanding, showcasing robust performance across input sequences of varying lengths with real-time inference speed.

摘要（中文）: 近年来，可泛化3D高斯溅射(3DGS)的进展使得在数秒内快速重建3D场景成为可能，无需针对每个场景进行优化。然而，现有方法主要遵循离线重建范式，缺乏连续重建能力，这限制了它们在机器人技术和VR/AR等在线场景中的适用性。在本文中，我们提出了OnlineX，一个前馈框架，仅使用流式图像以在线方式重建3D视觉外观和语言场。在线公式化的一个关键挑战是累积漂移问题，它源于内存状态的两个相反角色之间的根本冲突：一个积极的角色，不断刷新以捕获高频局部几何信息，以及一个稳定的角色，保守地积累和保存长期全局结构。为了解决这个问题，我们引入了一种解耦的主动到稳定状态演化范式。我们的框架将内存状态解耦为专门的主动状态和持久的稳定状态，然后将来自前者的信息有凝聚力地融合到后者中，以实现保真度和稳定性。此外，我们联合建模视觉外观和语言场，并结合一个隐式高斯融合模块来提高重建质量。在主流数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法在新的视角合成和语义理解方面始终优于先前的工作，展示了在不同长度的输入序列中具有实时推理速度的强大性能。

TLDR: The paper introduces OnlineX, a novel online 3D reconstruction and understanding framework that addresses the cumulative drift issue by decoupling the memory state into active and stable components. It demonstrates superior performance in novel view synthesis and semantic understanding compared to existing offline methods.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为OnlineX的新型在线3D重建和理解框架，通过将内存状态解耦为active和stable组件来解决累积漂移问题。与现有的离线方法相比，该方法在novel view synthesis和语义理解方面表现出更优越的性能。

Authors: Chong Xia, Fangfu Liu, Yule Wang, Yize Pang, Yueqi Duan

SimRecon: SimReady Compositional Scene Reconstruction from Real Videos

Abstract (English): Compositional scene reconstruction seeks to create object-centric representations rather than holistic scenes from real-world videos, which is natively applicable for simulation and interaction. Conventional compositional reconstruction approaches primarily emphasize on visual appearance and show limited generalization ability to real-world scenarios. In this paper, we propose SimRecon, a framework that realizes a "Perception-Generation-Simulation" pipeline towards cluttered scene reconstruction, which first conducts scene-level semantic reconstruction from video input, then performs single-object generation, and finally assembles these assets in the simulator. However, naively combining these three stages leads to visual infidelity of generated assets and physical implausibility of the final scene, a problem particularly severe for complex scenes. Thus, we further propose two bridging modules between the three stages to address this problem. To be specific, for the transition from Perception to Generation, critical for visual fidelity, we introduce Active Viewpoint Optimization, which actively searches in 3D space to acquire optimal projected images as conditions for single-object completion. Moreover, for the transition from Generation to Simulation, essential for physical plausibility, we propose a Scene Graph Synthesizer, which guides the construction from scratch in 3D simulators, mirroring the native, constructive principle of the real world. Extensive experiments on the ScanNet dataset validate our method's superior performance over previous state-of-the-art approaches.

摘要（中文）: 组合场景重建旨在从真实世界的视频中创建以对象为中心的表示，而非整体场景，这天然适用于仿真和交互。传统的组合重建方法主要强调视觉外观，并且在现实世界场景中的泛化能力有限。在本文中，我们提出SimRecon，一个实现“感知-生成-仿真”流水线的框架，用于杂乱场景重建。该框架首先从视频输入进行场景级语义重建，然后执行单对象生成，最后在仿真器中组装这些资源。然而，简单地组合这三个阶段会导致生成资源的视觉失真和最终场景的物理不可信，对于复杂场景而言，这个问题尤其严重。因此，我们进一步提出了两个连接这三个阶段的桥接模块来解决这个问题。具体而言，对于从感知到生成的过渡，这对于视觉保真度至关重要，我们引入了主动视点优化，它在三维空间中主动搜索以获取最佳投影图像，作为单对象补全的条件。此外，对于从生成到仿真的过渡，这对于物理合理性至关重要，我们提出了一个场景图合成器，它指导在3D仿真器中从头开始构建，镜像了真实世界的原生、构造性原则。在ScanNet数据集上的大量实验验证了我们的方法优于先前最先进的方法。

TLDR: SimRecon introduces a "Perception-Generation-Simulation" pipeline for compositional scene reconstruction from real-world videos, using active viewpoint optimization and a scene graph synthesizer to improve visual fidelity and physical plausibility.

简要说明: SimRecon提出了一种“感知-生成-模拟”流水线，用于从真实视频中进行组合场景重建，通过主动视点优化和场景图合成器来提高视觉保真度和物理合理性。

Authors: Chong Xia, Kai Zhu, Zizhuo Wang, Fangfu Liu, Zhizheng Zhang, Yueqi Duan

MMNavAgent: Multi-Magnification WSI Navigation Agent for Clinically Consistent Whole-Slide Analysis

Abstract (English): Recent AI navigation approaches aim to improve Whole-Slide Image (WSI) diagnosis by modeling spatial exploration and selecting diagnostically relevant regions, yet most operate at a single fixed magnification or rely on predefined magnification traversal. In clinical practice, pathologists examine slides across multiple magnifications and selectively inspect only necessary scales, dynamically integrating global and cellular evidence in a sequential manner. This mismatch prevents existing methods from modeling cross-magnification interactions and adaptive magnification selection inherent to real diagnostic workflows. To these, we propose a clinically consistent Multi-Magnification WSI Navigation Agent (MMNavAgent) that explicitly models multi magnification interaction and adaptive magnification selection. Specifically, we introduce a Cross-Magnification navigation Tool (CMT) that aggregates contextual information from adjacent magnifications to enhance discriminative representations along the navigation path. We further introduce a Magnification Selection Tool (MST) that leverages memory-driven reasoning within the agent framework to enable interactive and adaptive magnification selection, mimicking the sequential decision process of pathologists. Extensive experiments on a public dataset demonstrate improved diagnostic performance, with 1.45% gain of AUC and 2.93% gain of BACC over a non-agent baseline. Code will be public upon acceptance.

摘要（中文）: 近年来，人工智能导航方法旨在通过建模空间探索和选择诊断相关区域来改进全视野扫描图像 (WSI) 的诊断，但大多数方法都在单一固定放大倍率下运行，或依赖于预定义的放大倍率遍历。在临床实践中，病理学家会检查多个放大倍率下的切片，并选择性地检查必要的尺度，以连续的方式动态整合全局和细胞证据。这种不匹配使得现有方法无法建模真实诊断流程中固有的跨倍率交互和自适应倍率选择。为此，我们提出了一种临床一致的多倍率 WSI 导航代理 (MMNavAgent)，该代理显式地建模了多倍率交互和自适应倍率选择。具体而言，我们引入了一种跨倍率导航工具 (CMT)，该工具聚合来自相邻放大倍率的上下文信息，以增强沿导航路径的鉴别性表示。我们进一步引入了一种倍率选择工具 (MST)，该工具在代理框架内利用记忆驱动的推理，以实现交互式和自适应的倍率选择，从而模拟病理学家的顺序决策过程。在公共数据集上进行的大量实验表明，诊断性能得到了提高，与非代理基线相比，AUC 增益为 1.45%，BACC 增益为 2.93%。代码将在接收后公开。

The agent achieves improved diagnostic performance compared to a non-agent baseline.

Authors: Zhengyang Xu, Han Li, Jingsong Liu, Linrui Xie, Xun Ma, Xin You, Shihui Zu, Ayako Ito, Xinyu Hao, Hongming Xu, Shaohua Kevin Zhou, Nassir Navab, Peter J. Schüffler

ORGAN: Object-Centric Representation Learning using Cycle Consistent Generative Adversarial Networks

Abstract (English): Although data generation is often straightforward, extracting information from data is more difficult. Object-centric representation learning can extract information from images in an unsupervised manner. It does so by segmenting an image into its subcomponents: the objects. Each object is then represented in a low-dimensional latent space that can be used for downstream processing. Object-centric representation learning is dominated by autoencoder architectures (AEs). Here, we present ORGAN, a novel approach for object-centric representation learning, which is based on cycle-consistent Generative Adversarial Networks instead. We show that it performs similarly to other state-of-the-art approaches on synthetic datasets, while at the same time being the only approach tested here capable of handling more challenging real-world datasets with many objects and low visual contrast. Complementing these results, ORGAN creates expressive latent space representations that allow for object manipulation. Finally, we show that ORGAN scales well both with respect to the number of objects and the size of the images, giving it a unique edge over current state-of-the-art approaches.

摘要（中文）: 虽然数据生成通常很简单，但从数据中提取信息却更困难。以对象为中心的表征学习可以以无监督的方式从图像中提取信息。它通过将图像分割成其子组件：对象，来实现这一点。然后，每个对象都以低维潜在空间表示，可用于下游处理。以对象为中心的表征学习主要由自编码器架构 (AE) 主导。在这里，我们提出 ORGAN，一种用于以对象为中心的表征学习的新方法，它基于循环一致的生成对抗网络。我们表明，在合成数据集上，它的性能与其他最先进的方法相似，同时也是这里测试的唯一能够处理更具挑战性的真实世界数据集的方法，这些数据集具有许多对象和低视觉对比度。作为对这些结果的补充，ORGAN 创建了富有表现力的潜在空间表示，可以进行对象操作。最后，我们表明 ORGAN 在对象数量和图像大小方面都具有良好的可扩展性，这使其相对于目前最先进的方法具有独特的优势。

TLDR: The paper introduces ORGAN, a novel object-centric representation learning approach based on CycleGANs, demonstrating its effectiveness in handling real-world datasets with multiple objects and low contrast, while scaling well with object number and image size.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为ORGAN的新型基于CycleGAN的面向对象的表征学习方法，展示了其在处理具有多个对象和低对比度的真实世界数据集方面的有效性，并能很好地扩展对象数量和图像大小。

Authors: Joël Küchler, Ellen van Maren, Vaiva Vasiliauskaitė, Katarina Vulić, Reza Abbasi-Asl, Stephan J. Ihle

WhisperNet: A Scalable Solution for Bandwidth-Efficient Collaboration

Abstract (English): Collaborative perception is vital for autonomous driving yet remains constrained by tight communication budgets. Earlier work reduced bandwidth by compressing full feature maps with fixed-rate encoders, which adapts poorly to a changing environment, and it further evolved into spatial selection methods that improve efficiency by focusing on salient regions, but this object-centric approach often sacrifices global context, weakening holistic scene understanding. To overcome these limitations, we introduce \textit{WhisperNet}, a bandwidth-aware framework that proposes a novel, receiver-centric paradigm for global coordination across agents. Senders generate lightweight saliency metadata, while the receiver formulates a global request plan that dynamically budgets feature contributions across agents and features, retrieving only the most informative features. A collaborative feature routing module then aligns related messages before fusion to ensure structural consistency. Extensive experiments show that WhisperNet achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving AP@0.7 on OPV2V by 2.4\% with only 0.5\% of the communication cost. As a plug-and-play component, it boosts strong baselines with merely 5\% of full bandwidth while maintaining robustness under localization noise. These results demonstrate that globally-coordinated allocation across \textit{what} and \textit{where} to share is the key to achieving efficient collaborative perception.

摘要（中文）: 协同感知对于自动驾驶至关重要，但仍然受到严格的通信预算的限制。早期工作通过使用固定速率编码器压缩完整特征图来降低带宽，但这难以适应不断变化的环境。后来发展到空间选择方法，通过关注显著区域来提高效率，但这种以对象为中心的方法经常牺牲全局上下文，削弱整体场景理解。为了克服这些局限性，我们引入了\textit{WhisperNet}，这是一个带宽感知的框架，提出了一种新颖的、以接收者为中心的范式，用于跨代理的全局协调。发送者生成轻量级的显著性元数据，而接收者制定一个全局请求方案，该方案动态地预算跨代理和特征的特征贡献，只检索最具信息量的特征。然后，一个协作特征路由模块在融合之前对齐相关的消息，以确保结构一致性。大量的实验表明，WhisperNet实现了最先进的性能，在仅有0.5%的通信成本下，在OPV2V上将AP@0.7提高了2.4%。作为一个即插即用的组件，它仅使用完整带宽的5%就能提升强大的基线，同时保持在定位噪声下的鲁棒性。这些结果表明，全局协调地分配\textit{共享什么}和\textit{共享在哪里}是实现高效协同感知的关键。

TLDR: WhisperNet introduces a receiver-centric, bandwidth-aware approach to collaborative perception for autonomous driving, achieving state-of-the-art performance with significantly reduced communication costs by dynamically allocating feature sharing across agents and feature types.

简要说明: WhisperNet提出了一种以接收者为中心的、带宽感知的协同感知方法，用于自动驾驶，通过在代理和特征类型之间动态分配特征共享，以显著降低的通信成本实现了最先进的性能。

Authors: Gong Chen, Chaokun Zhang, Xinyan Zhao

CoopDiff: A Diffusion-Guided Approach for Cooperation under Corruptions

Abstract (English): Cooperative perception lets agents share information to expand coverage and improve scene understanding. However, in real-world scenarios, diverse and unpredictable corruptions undermine its robustness and generalization. To address these challenges, we introduce CoopDiff, a diffusion-based cooperative perception framework that mitigates corruptions via a denoising mechanism. CoopDiff adopts a teacher-student paradigm: the Quality-Aware Teacher performs voxel-level early fusion with Quality of Interest weighting and semantic guidance, then produces clean supervision features via a diffusion denoiser. The Dual-Branch Diffusion Student first separates ego and cooperative streams in encoding to reconstruct the teacher's clean targets. And then, an Ego-Guided Cross-Attention mechanism facilitates balanced decoding under degradation by adaptively integrating ego and cooperative features. We evaluate CoopDiff on two constructed multi-degradation benchmarks, OPV2Vn and DAIR-V2Xn, each incorporating six corruption types, including environmental and sensor-level distortions. Benefiting from the inherent denoising properties of diffusion, CoopDiff consistently outperforms prior methods across all degradation types and lowers the relative corruption error. Furthermore, it offers a tunable balance between precision and inference efficiency.

摘要（中文）: 合作感知允许智能体共享信息以扩展覆盖范围和改进场景理解。然而，在现实场景中，各种各样且不可预测的损坏会削弱其鲁棒性和泛化能力。为了应对这些挑战，我们引入了CoopDiff，一种基于扩散的合作感知框架，它通过去噪机制减轻损坏。CoopDiff采用教师-学生范式：质量感知教师执行体素级早期融合，利用感兴趣质量加权和语义引导，然后通过扩散去噪器生成干净的监督特征。双分支扩散学生首先在编码中分离自我和合作流，以重建教师的干净目标。然后，自我引导的交叉注意力机制通过自适应地整合自我和合作特征，促进在退化情况下的平衡解码。我们在两个构建的多重损坏基准测试OPV2Vn和DAIR-V2Xn上评估CoopDiff，每个基准测试都包含六种损坏类型，包括环境和传感器级别的失真。得益于扩散固有的去噪特性，CoopDiff在所有损坏类型上始终优于现有方法，并降低了相对损坏误差。此外，它还在精度和推理效率之间提供了可调节的平衡。

TLDR: CoopDiff is a diffusion-based cooperative perception framework designed to improve robustness and generalization in multi-agent systems operating under corruptions by using a teacher-student paradigm with denoising and attention mechanisms.

Authors: Gong Chen, Chaokun Zhang, Pengcheng Lv

QCAgent: An agentic framework for quality-controllable pathology report generation from whole slide image

Abstract (English): Recent methods for pathology report generation from whole-slide image (WSI) are capable of producing slide-level diagnostic descriptions but fail to ground fine-grained statements in localized visual evidence. Furthermore, they lack control over which diagnostic details to include and how to verify them. Inspired by emerging agentic analysis paradigms and the diagnostic workflow of pathologists,who selectively examine multiple fields of view, we propose QCAgent, an agentic framework for quality-controllable WSI report generation. The core innovations of this framework are as follows: (i) it incorporates a customized critique mechanism guided by a user-defined checklist specifying required diagnostic details and constraints; (ii) it re-identifies informative regions in the WSI based on the critique feedback and text-patch semantic retrieval, a process that iteratively enriches and reconciles the report. Experiments demonstrate that by making report requirements explicitly prompt-defined, constraint-aware, and verifiable through evidence-grounded refinement, QCAgent enables controllable generation of clinically meaningful and high-coverage pathology reports from WSI.

摘要（中文）: 用于从全切片图像(WSI)生成病理报告的最新方法虽然能够生成切片级别的诊断描述，但未能将细粒度的陈述基于局部化的视觉证据。 此外，它们缺乏对包含哪些诊断细节以及如何验证这些细节的控制。受到新兴的Agentic分析范式和病理学家的诊断流程的启发，病理学家有选择性地检查多个视野，我们提出了QCAgent，一个用于质量可控WSI报告生成的Agentic框架。该框架的核心创新如下：（i）它整合了一种定制的评论机制，该机制由用户定义的清单指导，指定所需的诊断细节和约束；（ii）它基于评论反馈和文本-图像块语义检索重新识别WSI中的信息区域，这个过程迭代地丰富和协调报告。实验表明，通过使报告要求明确地通过提示定义、感知约束并通过基于证据的提炼来进行验证，QCAgent能够从WSI中可控地生成具有临床意义且高覆盖率的病理报告。

TLDR: The paper introduces QCAgent, an agentic framework for generating pathology reports from whole slide images (WSI) with user-defined checklists for quality control and iterative refinement based on critique and semantic retrieval of image regions.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一种名为QCAgent的agentic框架，它可以通过用户定义的检查清单来控制质量，并基于批判性反馈和图像区域的语义检索进行迭代优化，从而从全切片图像（WSI）生成病理报告。

Coarse-to-Fine Monocular Re-Localization in OpenStreetMap via Semantic Alignment

Abstract (English): Monocular re-localization plays a crucial role in enabling intelligent agents to achieve human-like perception. However, traditional methods rely on dense maps, which face scalability limitations and privacy risks. OpenStreetMap (OSM), as a lightweight map that protects privacy, offers semantic and geometric information with global scalability. Nonetheless, there are still challenges in using OSM for localization: the inherent cross-modal discrepancies between natural images and OSM, as well as the high computational cost of global map-based localization. Authors: Yuchen Zou, Xiao Hu, Dexing Zhong, Yuqing Tang

Preference Score Distillation: Leveraging 2D Rewards to Align Text-to-3D Generation with Human Preference

Abstract (English): Human preference alignment presents a critical yet underexplored challenge for diffusion models in text-to-3D generation. Existing solutions typically require task-specific fine-tuning, posing significant hurdles in data-scarce 3D domains. To address this, we propose Preference Score Distillation (PSD), an optimization-based framework that leverages pretrained 2D reward models for human-aligned text-to-3D synthesis without 3D training data. Our key insight stems from the incompatibility of pixel-level gradients: due to the absence of noisy samples during reward model training, direct application of 2D reward gradients disturbs the denoising process. Noticing that similar issue occurs in the naive classifier guidance in conditioned diffusion models, we fundamentally rethink preference alignment as a classifier-free guidance (CFG)-style mechanism through our implicit reward model. Furthermore, recognizing that frozen pretrained diffusion models constrain performance, we introduce an adaptive strategy to co-optimize preference scores and negative text embeddings. By incorporating CFG during optimization, online refinement of negative text embeddings dynamically enhances alignment. To our knowledge, we are the first to bridge human preference alignment with CFG theory under score distillation framework. Experiments demonstrate the superiority of PSD in aesthetic metrics, seamless integration with diverse pipelines, and strong extensibility.

摘要（中文）: 人类偏好对齐是文本到3D生成中扩散模型面临的一项关键但未被充分探索的挑战。现有的解决方案通常需要特定任务的微调，这在数据稀缺的3D领域构成了重大障碍。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了偏好分数蒸馏 (PSD)，这是一种基于优化的框架，利用预训练的2D奖励模型进行人类对齐的文本到3D合成，而无需3D训练数据。我们的关键洞察力源于像素级梯度的不兼容性：由于奖励模型训练过程中缺少噪声样本，直接应用2D奖励梯度会干扰去噪过程。注意到类似的问题发生在条件扩散模型中朴素的分类器引导中，我们通过隐式奖励模型从根本上将偏好对齐重新思考为一种无分类器引导 (CFG) 风格的机制。此外，认识到冻结的预训练扩散模型会限制性能，我们引入了一种自适应策略来共同优化偏好分数和负文本嵌入。通过在优化过程中整合CFG，负文本嵌入的在线优化动态地增强了对齐。据我们所知，我们是第一个在分数蒸馏框架下将人类偏好对齐与CFG理论联系起来的人。实验表明了PSD在审美指标上的优越性、与各种流程的无缝集成以及强大的可扩展性。

TLDR: This paper introduces Preference Score Distillation (PSD), a method for aligning text-to-3D generation with human preferences by leveraging 2D reward models and classifier-free guidance, avoiding the need for 3D training data and improving aesthetic quality.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了偏好分数蒸馏（PSD），一种通过利用2D奖励模型和无分类器引导来使text-to-3D生成与人类偏好对齐的方法，避免了对3D训练数据的需求，并提高了美学质量。

Authors: Jiaqi Leng, Shuyuan Tu, Haidong Cao, Sicheng Xie, Daoguo Dong, Zuxuan Wu, Yu-Gang Jiang

Training-Free Spatio-temporal Decoupled Reasoning Video Segmentation with Adaptive Object Memory

Abstract (English): Reasoning Video Object Segmentation (ReasonVOS) is a challenging task that requires stable object segmentation across video sequences using implicit and complex textual inputs. Previous methods fine-tune Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) to produce segmentation outputs, which demand substantial resources. Additionally, some existing methods are coupled in the processing of spatio-temporal information, which affects the temporal stability of the model to some extent. To address these issues, we propose Training-Free \textbf{S}patio-temporal \textbf{D}ecoupled Reasoning Video Segmentation with \textbf{A}daptive Object \textbf{M}emory (SDAM). We aim to design a training-free reasoning video segmentation framework that outperforms existing methods requiring fine-tuning, using only pre-trained models. Meanwhile, we propose an Adaptive Object Memory module that selects and memorizes key objects based on motion cues in different video sequences. Finally, we propose Spatio-temporal Decoupling for stable temporal propagation. In the spatial domain, we achieve precise localization and segmentation of target objects, while in the temporal domain, we leverage key object temporal information to drive stable cross-frame propagation. Our method achieves excellent results on five benchmark datasets, including Ref-YouTubeVOS, Ref-DAVIS17, MeViS, ReasonVOS, and ReVOS.

摘要（中文）: 推理视频对象分割 (ReasonVOS) 是一项具有挑战性的任务，它需要利用隐式且复杂的文本输入，在视频序列中实现稳定的对象分割。 先前的方法微调多模态大型语言模型 (MLLM) 以生成分割输出，这需要大量的资源。 此外，一些现有方法在时空信息的处理中是耦合的，这在一定程度上影响了模型的时间稳定性。 为了解决这些问题，我们提出了一种免训练的、具有自适应对象记忆的空时解耦推理视频分割方法 (SDAM)。 我们的目标是设计一个免训练的推理视频分割框架，仅使用预训练模型，就可以胜过需要微调的现有方法。 同时，我们提出了一种自适应对象记忆模块，该模块基于不同视频序列中的运动线索来选择和记忆关键对象。 最后，我们提出了空时解耦以实现稳定的时间传播。 在空间域中，我们实现了目标对象的精确定位和分割，而在时间域中，我们利用关键对象的时间信息来驱动稳定的跨帧传播。 我们的方法在五个基准数据集上取得了优异的成果，包括 Ref-YouTubeVOS、Ref-DAVIS17、MeViS、ReasonVOS 和 ReVOS。

TLDR: This paper introduces SDAM, a training-free approach for reasoning video object segmentation that decouples spatial and temporal processing and uses an adaptive object memory. It claims state-of-the-art results on several benchmarks.

Authors: Zhengtong Zhu, Jiaqing Fan, Zhixuan Liu, Fanzhang Li

FoSS: Modeling Long Range Dependencies and Multimodal Uncertainty in Trajectory Prediction via Fourier State Space Integration

Abstract (English): Accurate trajectory prediction is vital for safe autonomous driving, yet existing approaches struggle to balance modeling power and computational efficiency. Attention-based architectures incur quadratic complexity with increasing agents, while recurrent models struggle to capture long-range dependencies and fine-grained local dynamics. Building upon this, we present FoSS, a dual-branch framework that unifies frequency-domain reasoning with linear-time sequence modeling. The frequency-domain branch performs a discrete Fourier transform to decompose trajectories into amplitude components encoding global intent and phase components capturing local variations, followed by a progressive helix reordering module that preserves spectral order; two selective state-space submodules, Coarse2Fine-SSM and SpecEvolve-SSM, refine spectral features with O(N) complexity. In parallel, a time-domain dynamic selective SSM reconstructs self-attention behavior in linear time to retain long-range temporal context. A cross-attention layer fuses temporal and spectral representations, while learnable queries generate multiple candidate trajectories, and a weighted fusion head expresses motion uncertainty. Experiments on Argoverse 1 and Argoverse 2 benchmarks demonstrate that FoSS achieves state-of-the-art accuracy while reducing computation by 22.5% and parameters by over 40%. Comprehensive ablations confirm the necessity of each component.

摘要（中文）: 精准的轨迹预测对于安全的自动驾驶至关重要，然而现有方法难以平衡建模能力和计算效率。 基于注意力的架构随着智能体数量的增加产生平方复杂度，而循环模型难以捕捉长程依赖关系和精细的局部动态。 基于此，我们提出了FoSS，一个双分支框架，它将频域推理与线性时间序列建模相结合。 频域分支执行离散傅里叶变换，将轨迹分解为编码全局意图的幅度分量和捕捉局部变化的相位分量，然后通过渐进螺旋重排序模块来保持频谱顺序； 两个选择性状态空间子模块，Coarse2Fine-SSM和SpecEvolve-SSM，以O(N)的复杂度细化频谱特征。 同时，一个时域动态选择性SSM在线性时间内重建自注意力行为，以保留远程时间上下文。 一个交叉注意力层融合时间和频谱表示，而可学习的查询生成多个候选轨迹，加权融合头表达运动不确定性。 在Argoverse 1和Argoverse 2基准测试上的实验表明，FoSS实现了最先进的精度，同时计算量减少了22.5%，参数减少了40%以上。 全面的消融实验证实了每个组件的必要性。

TLDR: FoSS is a novel trajectory prediction framework that leverages frequency-domain reasoning and linear-time sequence modeling to achieve state-of-the-art accuracy with reduced computation and parameters, demonstrated on autonomous driving datasets.

简要说明: FoSS是一种新颖的轨迹预测框架，它利用频域推理和线性时间序列建模，在自动驾驶数据集上实现了最先进的准确率，并降低了计算复杂度和参数规模。

Authors: Yizhou Huang, Gengze Jiang, Yihua Cheng, Kezhi Wang

Towards Policy-Adaptive Image Guardrail: Benchmark and Method

Abstract (English): Accurate rejection of sensitive or harmful visual content, i.e., harmful image guardrail, is critical in many application scenarios. This task must continuously adapt to the evolving safety policies and content across various domains and over time. However, traditional classifiers, confined to fixed categories, require frequent retraining when new policies are introduced. Vision-language models (VLMs) offer a more adaptable and generalizable foundation for dynamic safety guardrails. Despite this potential, existing VLM-based safeguarding methods are typically trained and evaluated under only a fixed safety policy. We find that these models are heavily overfitted to the seen policy, fail to generalize to unseen policies, and even lose the basic instruction-following ability and general knowledge. To address this issue, in this paper we make two key contributions. First, we benchmark the cross-policy generalization performance of existing VLMs with SafeEditBench, a new evaluation suite. SafeEditBench leverages image-editing models to convert unsafe images into safe counterparts, producing policy-aligned datasets where each safe-unsafe image pair remains visually similar except for localized regions violating specific safety rules. Human annotators then provide accurate safe/unsafe labels under five distinct policies, enabling fine-grained assessment of policy-aware generalization. Second, we introduce SafeGuard-VL, a reinforcement learning-based method with verifiable rewards (RLVR) for robust unsafe-image guardrails. Instead of relying solely on supervised fine-tuning (SFT) under fixed policies, SafeGuard-VL explicitly optimizes the model with policy-grounded rewards, promoting verifiable adaptation across evolving policies. Extensive experiments verify the effectiveness of our method for unsafe image guardrails across various policies.

摘要（中文）: 准确拒绝敏感或有害的视觉内容，即有害图像安全护栏，在许多应用场景中至关重要。这项任务必须不断适应不断演变的安全性策略和跨越不同领域和随时间变化的内容。然而，传统的分类器受限于固定的类别，当引入新的策略时需要频繁地重新训练。视觉-语言模型(VLM)为动态安全防护栏提供了一个更具适应性和泛化性的基础。尽管具有这种潜力，但现有的基于VLM的安全防护方法通常仅在固定的安全策略下进行训练和评估。我们发现这些模型过度拟合已知的策略，无法泛化到未知的策略，甚至丧失了基本的指令遵循能力和通用知识。为了解决这个问题，在本文中，我们做出了两个关键贡献。首先，我们使用SafeEditBench（一种新的评估套件）来衡量现有的VLM的跨策略泛化性能。SafeEditBench利用图像编辑模型将不安全的图像转换为安全的对应图像，生成策略对齐的数据集，其中每个安全/不安全图像对除了违反特定安全规则的局部区域外，在视觉上保持相似。然后，人工标注者在五种不同的策略下提供准确的安全/不安全标签，从而能够对策略感知泛化进行细粒度的评估。其次，我们引入了SafeGuard-VL，一种基于具有可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）的方法，用于鲁棒的不安全图像安全护栏。SafeGuard-VL 并非仅仅依赖于固定策略下的监督微调(SFT)，而是通过策略相关的奖励显式地优化模型，从而促进跨越不断演变的策略的可验证的适应。大量的实验验证了我们的方法在各种策略下对不安全图像安全护栏的有效性。

TLDR: This paper introduces SafeEditBench, a benchmark for evaluating cross-policy generalization in VLM-based image guardrails, and SafeGuard-VL, a reinforcement learning method for adapting to evolving safety policies by optimizing policy-grounded rewards.

简要说明: 本文介绍了SafeEditBench，一个用于评估基于VLM图像防护栏中跨策略泛化能力的基准，以及SafeGuard-VL，一种通过优化基于策略的奖励来适应不断演变的安全策略的强化学习方法。

Authors: Caiyong Piao, Zhiyuan Yan, Haoming Xu, Yunzhen Zhao, Kaiqing Lin, Feiyang Xu, Shuigeng Zhou

PAC Guarantees for Reinforcement Learning: Sample Complexity, Coverage, and Structure

Abstract (English): When data is scarce or mistakes are costly, average-case metrics fall short. What a practitioner needs is a guarantee: with probability at least $1-δ$, the learned policy is $\varepsilon$-close to optimal after $N$ episodes. This is the PAC promise, and between 2018 and 2025 the RL theory community made striking progress on when such promises can be kept. We survey that progress. Our organizing tool is the Coverage-Structure-Objective (CSO) framework, proposed here, which decomposes nearly every PAC sample complexity result into three factors: coverage (how data were obtained), structure (intrinsic MDP or function-class complexity), and objective (what the learner must deliver). CSO is not a theorem but an interpretive template that identifies bottlenecks and makes cross-setting comparison immediate. The technical core covers tight tabular baselines and the uniform-PAC bridge to regret; structural complexity measures (Bellman rank, witness rank, Bellman-Eluder dimension) governing learnability with function approximation; results for linear, kernel/NTK, and low-rank models; reward-free exploration as upfront coverage investment; and pessimistic offline RL where inherited coverage is the binding constraint. We provide practitioner tools: rate lookup tables indexed by CSO coordinates, Bellman residual diagnostics, coverage estimation with deployment gates, and per-episode policy certificates. A final section catalogs open problems, separating near-term targets from frontier questions where coverage, structure, and computation tangle in ways current theory cannot resolve.

摘要（中文）: 当数据稀缺或错误代价高昂时，平均情况指标就显得不足。实践者需要的是一种保证：以至少$1-δ$的概率，学习到的策略在经历$N$个episode后是$\varepsilon$接近最优的。这就是PAC承诺，并且在2018年至2025年间，强化学习理论界在何时能够保持这种承诺方面取得了显著进展。我们对这些进展进行综述。我们采用的组织工具是Coverage-Structure-Objective（CSO）框架，这是本文提出的，它将几乎每个PAC样本复杂度结果分解为三个因素：覆盖率（数据如何获得）、结构（内在MDP或函数类复杂度）和目标（学习者必须交付的内容）。CSO不是一个定理，而是一个解释模版，用于识别瓶颈并使跨场景比较立即可行。技术核心涵盖严格的表格基线和到遗憾值的均匀PAC桥梁；控制函数逼近可学习性的结构复杂度度量（贝尔曼秩、见证秩、贝尔曼-Eluder维度）；线性、核/NTK和低秩模型的结果；作为前期覆盖投资的无奖励探索；以及悲观离线RL，其中继承的覆盖率具有约束性。我们提供实践者工具：按CSO坐标索引的速率查找表、贝尔曼残差诊断、带部署门的覆盖率估计和每个episode的策略证书。最后一节列出了未解决的问题，将近期目标与前沿问题区分开来，在这些前沿问题中，覆盖率、结构和计算以当前理论无法解决的方式纠缠在一起。

TLDR: This paper surveys recent progress on PAC guarantees for reinforcement learning, introducing the Coverage-Structure-Objective (CSO) framework to analyze sample complexity results and providing practical tools for practitioners.

简要说明: 本文综述了强化学习中PAC保证的最新进展，引入了覆盖-结构-目标（CSO）框架来分析样本复杂度结果，并为从业者提供了实用工具。

Authors: Joshua Steier

Real-Time Thermal-Inertial Odometry on Embedded Hardware for High-Speed GPS-Denied Flight

Abstract (English): We present a real-time monocular thermal-inertial odometry system designed for high-velocity, GPS-denied flight on embedded hardware. The system fuses measurements from a FLIR Boson+ 640 longwave infrared camera, a high-rate IMU, a laser range finder, a barometer, and a magnetometer within a fixed-lag factor graph. To sustain reliable feature tracks under motion blur, low contrast, and rapid viewpoint changes, we employ a lightweight thermal-optimized front-end with multi-stage feature filtering. Laser range finder measurements provide per-feature depth priors that stabilize scale during weakly observable motion. High-rate inertial data is first pre-filtered using a Chebyshev Type II infinite impulse response (IIR) filter and then preintegrated, improving robustness to airframe vibrations during aggressive maneuvers. To address barometric altitude errors induced at high airspeeds, we train an uncertainty-aware gated recurrent unit (GRU) network that models the temporal dynamics of static pressure distortion, outperforming polynomial and multi-layer perceptron (MLP) baselines. Integrated on an NVIDIA Jetson Xavier NX, the complete system supports closed-loop quadrotor flight at 30 m/s with drift under 2% over kilometer-scale trajectories. These contributions expand the operational envelope of thermal-inertial navigation, enabling reliable high-speed flight in visually degraded and GPS-denied environments.

摘要（中文）: 我们提出了一种实时单目热惯性里程计系统，专为嵌入式硬件上进行高速、无GPS环境下的飞行而设计。该系统将来自FLIR Boson+ 640长波红外相机、高频率IMU、激光测距仪、气压计和磁力计的测量结果融合到固定延迟因子图中。为了在运动模糊、低对比度和快速视点变化的情况下维持可靠的特征跟踪，我们采用了一种轻量级的、热优化前端，并配备多级特征过滤。激光测距仪的测量结果为每个特征提供深度先验，从而在弱可观运动期间稳定尺度。高频率惯性数据首先使用 Chebyshev Type II 无限脉冲响应 (IIR) 滤波器进行预滤波，然后进行预积分，从而提高了在剧烈机动过程中对机身振动的鲁棒性。为了解决高速空速下引起的气压高度误差，我们训练了一个具有不确定性感知的门控循环单元 (GRU) 网络，该网络能够模拟静态压力畸变的时间动态，性能优于多项式和多层感知器 (MLP) 基线。该完整系统集成在 NVIDIA Jetson Xavier NX 上，支持四旋翼飞行器在 30 米/秒的速度下进行闭环飞行，在公里级的轨迹上的漂移小于 2%。这些贡献扩展了热惯性导航的运行范围，从而能够在视觉退化和无 GPS 环境中实现可靠的高速飞行。

TLDR: This paper presents a real-time thermal-inertial odometry system for high-speed, GPS-denied flight on embedded systems, achieving closed-loop quadrotor flight at 30 m/s with low drift using a sensor fusion approach and a GRU network for pressure distortion modeling.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种用于嵌入式系统上高速、无GPS飞行环境的实时热惯性里程计系统。该系统采用传感器融合方法和一个GRU网络进行压力失真建模，实现了30米/秒的闭环四旋翼飞行，且漂移较低。

Authors: Austin Stone, Mark Petersen, Cammy Peterson

Goal-Oriented Semantic Communication for ISAC-Enabled Robotic Obstacle Avoidance

Abstract (English): We investigate an integrated sensing and communication (ISAC)-enabled BS for the unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) obstacle avoidance task, and propose a goal-oriented semantic communication (GOSC) framework for the BS to transmit sensing and command and control (C&C) signals efficiently and effectively. Our GOSC framework establishes a closed loop for sensing-C&C generation-sensing and C&C transmission: For sensing, a Kalman filter (KF) is applied to continuously predict UAV positions, mitigating the reliance of UAV position acquisition on continuous sensing signal transmission, and enhancing position estimation accuracy through sensing-prediction fusion. Based on the refined estimation position provided by the KF, we develop a Mahalanobis distance-based dynamic window approach (MD-DWA) to generate precise C&C signals under uncertainty, in which we derive the mathematical expression of the minimum Mahalanobis distance required to guarantee collision avoidance. Finally, for efficient sensing and C&C signal transmission, we propose an effectiveness-aware deep Q-network (E-DQN) to determine the transmission of sensing and C&C signals based on their value of information (VoI). The VoI of sensing signals is quantified by the reduction in uncertainty entropy of UAV's position estimation, while the VoI of C&C signals is measured by their contribution to UAV navigation improvement. Extensive simulations validate the effectiveness of our proposed GOSC framework. Compared to the conventional ISAC transmission framework that transmits sensing and C&C signals at every time slot, GOSC achieves the same 100% task success rate while reducing the number of transmitted sensing and C&C signals by 92.4% and the number of transmission time slots by 85.5%.

摘要（中文）: 我们研究了支持无人机（UAV）避障任务的集成感知与通信（ISAC）基站，并提出了一种面向目标的语义通信（GOSC）框架，以便基站高效且有效地传输感知信号以及指挥与控制（C&C）信号。我们的GOSC框架建立了一个感知-C&C生成-感知及C&C传输的闭环：对于感知，应用卡尔曼滤波器（KF）持续预测UAV位置，从而减轻UAV位置获取对连续感知信号传输的依赖，并通过感知-预测融合提高位置估计精度。基于KF提供的精细估计位置，我们开发了一种基于马氏距离的动态窗口方法（MD-DWA），以在不确定性下生成精确的C&C信号，其中我们推导了保证避碰所需的最小马氏距离的数学表达式。最后，为了高效地传输感知信号和C&C信号，我们提出了一种有效性感知深度Q网络（E-DQN），以基于感知信号和C&C信号的信息价值（VoI）来确定它们的传输。感知信号的VoI通过降低UAV位置估计的不确定性熵来量化，而C&C信号的VoI则通过它们对UAV导航改进的贡献来衡量。大量仿真验证了我们提出的GOSC框架的有效性。与在每个时隙传输感知信号和C&C信号的传统ISAC传输框架相比，GOSC在实现相同100%任务成功率的同时，将传输的感知信号和C&C信号数量减少了92.4%，并将传输时隙数量减少了85.5%。

TLDR: This paper proposes a goal-oriented semantic communication (GOSC) framework for ISAC-enabled UAV obstacle avoidance, using a Kalman filter, Mahalanobis distance-based DWA, and effectiveness-aware DQN to efficiently transmit sensing and C&C signals, significantly reducing transmission overhead while maintaining task success.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种面向目标的语义通信 (GOSC) 框架，用于支持 ISAC 的无人机避障，使用卡尔曼滤波器、基于马哈拉诺比斯距离的 DWA 和功效感知 DQN 来有效地传输传感和 C&C 信号，在保持任务成功率的同时显著降低传输开销。

Authors: Wenjie Liu, Yansha Deng, Henk Wymeersch

LEAR: Learning Edge-Aware Representations for Event-to-LiDAR Localization

Abstract (English): Event cameras offer high-temporal-resolution sensing that remains reliable under high-speed motion and challenging lighting, making them promising for localization from LiDAR point clouds in GPS-denied and visually degraded environments. However, aligning sparse, asynchronous events with dense LiDAR maps is fundamentally ill-posed, as direct correspondence estimation suffers from modality gaps. We propose LEAR, a dual-task learning framework that jointly estimates edge structures and dense event-depth flow fields to bridge the sensing-modality divide. Instead of treating edges as a post-hoc aid, LEAR couples them with flow estimation through a cross-modal fusion mechanism that injects modality-invariant geometric cues into the motion representation, and an iterative refinement strategy that enforces mutual consistency between the two tasks over multiple update steps. This synergy produces edge-aware, depth-aligned flow fields that enable more robust and accurate pose recovery via Perspective-n-Point (PnP) solvers. On several popular and challenging datasets, LEAR achieves superior performance over the best prior method. The source code, trained models, and demo videos are made publicly available online.

摘要（中文）: 事件相机提供高时间分辨率的感知能力，在高速度运动和具有挑战性的光照条件下仍能保持可靠性，使其在GPS受限和视觉退化环境中利用激光雷达点云进行定位方面具有前景。然而，将稀疏、异步的事件数据与稠密的激光雷达地图对齐本质上是不适定的，因为直接的对应关系估计会受到模态差异的影响。我们提出了一种双任务学习框架LEAR，它联合估计边缘结构和稠密事件-深度光流场，以弥合传感模态之间的鸿沟。LEAR没有将边缘视为后处理辅助，而是通过一种跨模态融合机制将边缘与光流估计耦合在一起，该机制将模态不变的的几何线索注入到运动表示中，并通过一种迭代细化策略，在多个更新步骤中强制执行两个任务之间的相互一致性。这种协同作用产生了边缘感知、深度对齐的光流场，从而可以通过透视N点（PnP）求解器实现更稳健、更准确的姿态恢复。在几个流行且具有挑战性的数据集上，LEAR取得了优于现有最佳方法的性能。源代码、训练好的模型和演示视频已在网上公开提供。

TLDR: The paper introduces LEAR, a dual-task learning framework that leverages event cameras and LiDAR data for localization in challenging environments by jointly estimating edge structures and dense event-depth flow fields, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了 LEAR，一个双任务学习框架，它利用事件相机和激光雷达数据，通过联合估计边缘结构和密集事件深度流场，来实现具有挑战性环境中的定位，并取得了最先进的性能。

Authors: Kuangyi Chen, Jun Zhang, Yuxi Hu, Yi Zhou, Friedrich Fraundorfer

Certifiable Estimation with Factor Graphs

Abstract (English): Factor graphs provide a convenient modular modeling language that enables practitioners to design and deploy high-performance robotic state estimation systems by composing simple, reusable building blocks. However, inference in these models is typically performed using local optimization methods that can converge to suboptimal solutions, a serious reliability concern in safety-critical applications. Conversely, certifiable estimators based on convex relaxation can recover verifiably globally optimal solutions in many practical settings, but the computational cost of solving their large-scale relaxations necessitates specialized, structure-exploiting solvers that require substantial expertise to implement, significantly hampering practical deployment. In this paper, we show that these two paradigms, which have thus far been treated as independent in the literature, can be naturally synthesized into a unified framework for certifiable factor graph optimization. The key insight is that factor graph structure is preserved under Shor's relaxation and Burer-Monteiro factorization: applying these transformations to a QCQP with an associated factor graph representation yields a lifted problem admitting a factor graph model with identical connectivity, in which variables and factors are simple one-to-one algebraic transformations of those in the original QCQP. This structural preservation enables the Riemannian Staircase methodology for certifiable estimation to be implemented using the same mature, highly-performant factor graph libraries and workflows already ubiquitously employed throughout robotics and computer vision, making certifiable estimation as straightforward to design and deploy as conventional factor graph inference.

摘要（中文）: 因子图提供了一种便捷的模块化建模语言，使从业者能够通过组合简单的、可复用的构建块来设计和部署高性能的机器人状态估计系统。然而，在这些模型中进行推断通常使用可能收敛到次优解的局部优化方法，这对于安全关键应用来说是一个严重可靠性问题。相反，基于凸松弛的可验证估计器可以在许多实际场景中恢复可验证的全局最优解，但求解其大规模松弛的计算成本需要专门的、利用结构的求解器，这需要大量的专业知识来实现，从而极大地阻碍了实际部署。 在本文中，我们表明这两种范式（迄今为止在文献中被视为独立的）可以自然地合成为一个用于可验证因子图优化的统一框架。关键的洞察在于，因子图结构在Shor松弛和Burer-Monteiro因子分解下得以保留：将这些变换应用于具有相关因子图表示的QCQP会产生一个提升后的问题，该问题允许一个具有相同连接性的因子图模型，其中变量和因子是原始QCQP中变量和因子的简单一对一代数变换。这种结构保留使得用于可验证估计的黎曼楼梯方法能够使用与机器人和计算机视觉中普遍采用的相同的成熟、高性能因子图库和工作流程来实现，使得可验证估计的设计和部署与传统的因子图推断一样简单。

TLDR: The paper presents a unified framework for certifiable factor graph optimization by showing that factor graph structure is preserved under specific transformations, enabling the use of existing factor graph libraries for certifiable estimation.

简要说明: 本文提出了一个统一的认证因子图优化框架，通过证明因子图结构在特定变换下得以保留，从而允许使用现有的因子图库进行认证估计。

Authors: Zhexin Xu, Nikolas R. Sanderson, Hanna Jiamei Zhang, David M. Rosen

LiftAvatar: Kinematic-Space Completion for Expression-Controlled 3D Gaussian Avatar Animation

Abstract (English): We present LiftAvatar, a new paradigm that completes sparse monocular observations in kinematic space (e.g., facial expressions and head pose) and uses the completed signals to drive high-fidelity avatar animation. LiftAvatar is a fine-grained, expression-controllable large-scale video diffusion Transformer that synthesizes high-quality, temporally coherent expression sequences conditioned on single or multiple reference images. The key idea is to lift incomplete input data into a richer kinematic representation, thereby strengthening both reconstruction and animation in downstream 3D avatar pipelines. To this end, we introduce (i) a multi-granularity expression control scheme that combines shading maps with expression coefficients for precise and stable driving, and (ii) a multi-reference conditioning mechanism that aggregates complementary cues from multiple frames, enabling strong 3D consistency and controllability. As a plug-and-play enhancer, LiftAvatar directly addresses the limited expressiveness and reconstruction artifacts of 3D Gaussian Splatting-based avatars caused by sparse kinematic cues in everyday monocular videos. By expanding incomplete observations into diverse pose-expression variations, LiftAvatar also enables effective prior distillation from large-scale video generative models into 3D pipelines, leading to substantial gains. Extensive experiments show that LiftAvatar consistently boosts animation quality and quantitative metrics of state-of-the-art 3D avatar methods, especially under extreme, unseen expressions.

摘要（中文）: 我们提出了LiftAvatar，一种新的范式，它补全运动学空间中的稀疏单目观测（例如，面部表情和头部姿态），并利用补全后的信号来驱动高保真度化身动画。LiftAvatar是一个细粒度、表情可控的大规模视频扩散Transformer，它基于单张或多张参考图像生成高质量、时间上连贯的表情序列。核心思想是将不完整的输入数据提升到更丰富的运动学表示，从而加强下游3D化身流水线中的重建和动画效果。为此，我们引入了（i）一种多粒度的表情控制方案，该方案结合了阴影贴图和表情系数，以实现精确且稳定的驱动，以及（ii）一种多参考条件机制，该机制聚合来自多个帧的互补线索，从而实现强大的3D一致性和可控性。作为一种即插即用增强器，LiftAvatar直接解决了基于3D高斯溅射的化身由于日常单目视频中稀疏的运动学线索而导致的有限的表达能力和重建伪影问题。通过将不完整的观测扩展到多种姿势-表情变化，LiftAvatar还能够将大规模视频生成模型中的有效先验知识提取到3D流水线中，从而带来显著的收益。大量的实验表明，LiftAvatar始终如一地提高了最先进的3D化身方法的动画质量和定量指标，特别是在极端、未见过的表情下。

TLDR: LiftAvatar uses a video diffusion Transformer to complete sparse monocular kinematic data and drive high-fidelity 3D avatar animation, improving expressiveness and reducing artifacts in Gaussian Splatting avatars.

简要说明: LiftAvatar使用视频扩散Transformer来补全稀疏的单目运动学数据，并驱动高保真3D虚拟形象动画，从而提高基于高斯splatting虚拟形象的表现力并减少伪影。

Authors: Hualiang Wei, Shunran Jia, Jialun Liu, Wenhui Li

physfusion: A Transformer-based Dual-Stream Radar and Vision Fusion Framework for Open Water Surface Object Detection

Abstract (English): Detecting water-surface targets for Unmanned Surface Vehicles (USVs) is challenging due to wave clutter, specular reflections, and weak appearance cues in long-range observations. Although 4D millimeter-wave radar complements cameras under degraded illumination, maritime radar point clouds are sparse and intermittent, with reflectivity attributes exhibiting heavy-tailed variations under scattering and multipath, making conventional fusion designs struggle to exploit radar cues effectively. We propose PhysFusion, a physics-informed radar-image detection framework for water-surface perception. The framework integrates: (1) a Physics-Informed Radar Encoder (PIR Encoder) with an RCS Mapper and Quality Gate, transforming per-point radar attributes into compact scattering priors and predicting point-wise reliability for robust feature learning under clutter; (2) a Radar-guided Interactive Fusion Module (RIFM) performing query-level radar-image fusion between semantically enriched radar features and multi-scale visual features, with the radar branch modeled by a dual-stream backbone including a point-based local stream and a transformer-based global stream using Scattering-Aware Self-Attention (SASA); and (3) a Temporal Query Aggregation module (TQA) aggregating frame-wise fused queries over a short temporal window for temporally consistent representations. Experiments on WaterScenes and FLOW demonstrate that PhysFusion achieves 59.7% mAP50:95 and 90.3% mAP50 on WaterScenes (T=5 radar history) using 5.6M parameters and 12.5G FLOPs, and reaches 94.8% mAP50 and 46.2% mAP50:95 on FLOW under radar+camera setting. Ablation studies quantify the contributions of PIR Encoder, SASA-based global reasoning, and RIFM.

摘要（中文）: 由于波浪杂波、镜面反射以及远距离观测中微弱的表观线索，为无人水面艇（USV）检测水面目标极具挑战性。尽管4D毫米波雷达能够补充在光照退化条件下的摄像头，但海事雷达点云是稀疏且间歇性的，其反射率属性在散射和多径条件下呈现重尾分布，使得传统的融合设计难以有效利用雷达线索。 我们提出PhysFusion，一个用于水面感知的物理信息雷达-图像检测框架。该框架集成了：（1）一个带有雷达截面积映射器（RCS Mapper）和质量门（Quality Gate）的物理信息雷达编码器（PIR Encoder），将逐点雷达属性转换为紧凑的散射先验，并预测逐点可靠性，以在杂波下进行鲁棒的特征学习；（2）一个雷达引导交互式融合模块（RIFM），在语义丰富的雷达特征和多尺度视觉特征之间执行查询级别的雷达-图像融合，雷达分支由一个双流骨干网络建模，包括一个基于点的局部流和一个基于Transformer的使用散射感知自注意力（SASA）的全局流；（3）一个时间查询聚合模块（TQA），在短的时间窗口内聚合逐帧融合的查询，以获得时间上一致的表示。 在WaterScenes和FLOW数据集上的实验表明，PhysFusion使用560万参数和125亿FLOPs在WaterScenes上实现了59.7%的mAP50:95和90.3%的mAP50（T=5雷达历史)，在雷达+摄像头设置下在FLOW上达到了94.8%的mAP50和46.2%的mAP50:95。消融研究量化了PIR编码器、基于SASA的全局推理和RIFM的贡献。

TLDR: The paper introduces PhysFusion, a transformer-based radar and vision fusion framework for detecting objects on water surfaces, addressing the challenges of sparse and noisy radar data in maritime environments with physics-informed encoding and attention mechanisms.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了PhysFusion，一个基于Transformer的雷达和视觉融合框架，用于检测水面上的物体。该框架通过物理信息编码和注意力机制解决了海上环境中雷达数据稀疏和嘈杂的挑战。

Authors: Yuting Wan, Liguo Sun, Jiuwu Hao, Zao Zhang, Pin LV

Dream2Learn: Structured Generative Dreaming for Continual Learning

Abstract (English): Continual learning requires balancing plasticity and stability while mitigating catastrophic forgetting. Inspired by human dreaming as a mechanism for internal simulation and knowledge restructuring, we introduce Dream2Learn (D2L), a framework in which a model autonomously generates structured synthetic experiences from its own internal representations and uses them for self-improvement. Rather than reconstructing past data as in generative replay, D2L enables a classifier to create novel, semantically distinct dreamed classes that are coherent with its learned knowledge yet do not correspond to previously observed data. These dreamed samples are produced by conditioning a frozen diffusion model through soft prompt optimization driven by the classifier itself. The generated data are not used to replace memory, but to expand and reorganize the representation space, effectively allowing the network to self-train on internally synthesized concepts. By integrating dreamed classes into continual training, D2L proactively structures latent features to support forward knowledge transfer and adaptation to future tasks. This prospective self-training mechanism mirrors the role of sleep in consolidating and reorganizing memory, turning internal simulations into a tool for improved generalization. Experiments on Mini-ImageNet, FG-ImageNet, and ImageNet-R demonstrate that D2L consistently outperforms strong rehearsal-based baselines and achieves positive forward transfer, confirming its ability to enhance adaptability through internally generated training signals.

摘要（中文）: 持续学习需要在可塑性和稳定性之间取得平衡，同时缓解灾难性遗忘。受到人类梦境作为内部模拟和知识重构机制的启发，我们introduces Dream2Learn (D2L)，一个框架，其中模型自主地从其自身的内部表示中生成结构化的合成经验，并利用这些经验进行自我提升。与生成回放中重建过去数据不同，D2L使分类器能够创建新颖、语义上不同的“梦境类别”，这些类别与其已学知识相一致，但并不对应于先前观察到的数据。这些“梦境样本”是通过分类器本身驱动的，利用软提示优化来调节一个冻结的扩散模型而产生的。生成的数据并不用于替代记忆，而是扩展和重组表示空间，有效地允许网络在内部合成的概念上进行自训练。通过将“梦境类别”整合到持续训练中，D2L主动地构建潜在特征，以支持前向知识迁移和适应未来任务。这种前瞻性的自训练机制反映了睡眠在巩固和重组记忆中的作用，将内部模拟转化为改进泛化的工具。在Mini-ImageNet、FG-ImageNet和ImageNet-R上的实验表明，D2L始终优于强大的基于回放模型的基线，并实现了正向迁移，证实了其通过内部生成的训练信号来增强适应性的能力。

TLDR: The paper introduces Dream2Learn (D2L), a continual learning framework inspired by human dreaming, which uses internally generated, structured synthetic data from a diffusion model to improve a classifier's knowledge and adaptability. It outperforms rehearsal-based baselines in continual learning settings.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了Dream2Learn (D2L)，一个受人类做梦启发的持续学习框架，它使用diffusion模型内部生成并结构化的合成数据来提高分类器的知识和适应性。在持续学习设置中，该方法优于基于排练的基线。

Authors: Salvatore Calcagno, Matteo Pennisi, Federica Proietto Salanitri, Amelia Sorrenti, Simone Palazzo, Concetto Spampinato, Giovanni Bellitto

Learning Shortest Paths with Generative Flow Networks

Abstract (English): In this paper, we present a novel learning framework for finding shortest paths in graphs utilizing Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets). First, we examine theoretical properties of GFlowNets in non-acyclic environments in relation to shortest paths. We prove that, if the total flow is minimized, forward and backward policies traverse the environment graph exclusively along shortest paths between the initial and terminal states. Building on this result, we show that the pathfinding problem in an arbitrary graph can be solved by training a non-acyclic GFlowNet with flow regularization. We experimentally demonstrate the performance of our method in pathfinding in permutation environments and in solving Rubik's Cubes. For the latter problem, our approach shows competitive results with state-of-the-art machine learning approaches designed specifically for this task in terms of the solution length, while requiring smaller search budget at test-time.

摘要（中文）: 本文提出了一种利用生成流网络（GFlowNets）在图中寻找最短路径的新型学习框架。首先，我们研究了GFlowNets在非有向无环环境中的理论性质，并将其与最短路径联系起来。我们证明，如果总流量最小化，前向和后向策略将仅沿着初始状态和终止状态之间的最短路径遍历环境图。基于此结果，我们展示了通过训练具有流量正则化的非有向无环GFlowNet可以解决任意图中的路径寻找问题。我们通过实验证明了我们的方法在排列环境中的路径寻找以及解决魔方问题上的性能。对于后者，我们的方法在解的长度方面显示出与专门为该任务设计的最先进机器学习方法相比具有竞争力的结果，同时在测试时需要更小的搜索预算。

TLDR: This paper introduces a GFlowNet-based method for finding shortest paths in graphs, including non-acyclic environments. It demonstrates competitive performance on Rubik's Cubes compared to specialized ML approaches.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种基于GFlowNet的方法，用于寻找图中的最短路径，包括非循环环境。 在魔方问题上，它展示了与针对该任务的专门机器学习方法相比具有竞争力的性能。

Authors: Nikita Morozov, Ian Maksimov, Daniil Tiapkin, Sergey Samsonov

Chain-of-Context Learning: Dynamic Constraint Understanding for Multi-Task VRPs

Abstract (English): Multi-task Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) aim to minimize routing costs while satisfying diverse constraints. Existing solvers typically adopt a unified reinforcement learning (RL) framework to learn generalizable patterns across tasks. However, they often overlook the constraint and node dynamics during the decision process, making the model fail to accurately react to the current context. To address this limitation, we propose Chain-of-Context Learning (CCL), a novel framework that progressively captures the evolving context to guide fine-grained node adaptation. Specifically, CCL constructs step-wise contextual information via a Relevance-Guided Context Reformulation (RGCR) module, which adaptively prioritizes salient constraints. This context then guides node updates through a Trajectory-Shared Node Re-embedding (TSNR) module, which aggregates shared node features from all trajectories' contexts and uses them to update inputs for the next step. By modeling evolving preferences of the RL agent, CCL captures step-by-step dependencies in sequential decision-making. We evaluate CCL on 48 diverse VRP variants, including 16 in-distribution and 32 out-of-distribution (with unseen constraints) tasks. Experimental results show that CCL performs favorably against the state-of-the-art baselines, achieving the best performance on all in-distribution tasks and the majority of out-of-distribution tasks.

摘要（中文）: 多任务车辆路径问题（VRPs）旨在最小化路径成本，同时满足各种约束条件。现有的求解器通常采用统一的强化学习（RL）框架来学习跨任务的通用模式。然而，它们常常忽略决策过程中的约束和节点动态，导致模型无法准确地对当前上下文做出反应。为了解决这一局限性，我们提出了一种新的Chain-of-Context Learning（CCL）框架，该框架逐步捕捉演化的上下文，以指导细粒度的节点自适应。具体来说，CCL通过相关性引导的上下文重构（RGCR）模块构建逐步的上下文信息，该模块自适应地优先考虑显著的约束条件。然后，该上下文通过轨迹共享的节点重嵌入（TSNR）模块来指导节点更新，该模块聚合来自所有轨迹上下文的共享节点特征，并使用它们来更新下一步的输入。通过建模RL代理的演化偏好，CCL捕捉了顺序决策中的逐步依赖关系。我们在48个不同的VRP变体上评估了CCL，包括16个同分布和32个异分布（具有未见约束）任务。实验结果表明，CCL的表现优于最先进的基线方法，在所有同分布任务和大多数异分布任务上都取得了最佳性能。

TLDR: This paper introduces Chain-of-Context Learning (CCL), a novel reinforcement learning framework for multi-task Vehicle Routing Problems (VRPs) that captures evolving contextual information to improve performance, especially on out-of-distribution tasks.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为情境链学习 (CCL) 的新型强化学习框架，用于多任务车辆路径问题 (VRP)。该框架捕获不断演变的情境信息，从而提高性能，尤其是在分布外任务上。

Authors: Shuangchun Gui, Suyu Liu, Xuehe Wang, Zhiguang Cao

The Observer-Situation Lattice: A Unified Formal Basis for Perspective-Aware Cognition

Abstract (English): Autonomous agents operating in complex, multi-agent environments must reason about what is true from multiple perspectives. Existing approaches often struggle to integrate the reasoning of different agents, at different times, and in different contexts, typically handling these dimensions in separate, specialized modules. This fragmentation leads to a brittle and incomplete reasoning process, particularly when agents must understand the beliefs of others (Theory of Mind). We introduce the Observer-Situation Lattice (OSL), a unified mathematical structure that provides a single, coherent semantic space for perspective-aware cognition. OSL is a finite complete lattice where each element represents a unique observer-situation pair, allowing for a principled and scalable approach to belief management. We present two key algorithms that operate on this lattice: (i) Relativized Belief Propagation, an incremental update algorithm that efficiently propagates new information, and (ii) Minimal Contradiction Decomposition, a graph-based procedure that identifies and isolates contradiction components. We prove the theoretical soundness of our framework and demonstrate its practical utility through a series of benchmarks, including classic Theory of Mind tasks and a comparison with established paradigms such as assumption-based truth maintenance systems. Our results show that OSL provides a computationally efficient and expressive foundation for building robust, perspective-aware autonomous agents.

摘要（中文）: 在复杂的多智能体环境中运行的自主体必须从多个视角推断真实信息。现有方法通常难以整合不同智能体、不同时间以及不同环境下的推理，通常将这些维度分割成独立的、专门的模块处理。这种碎片化导致推理过程脆弱且不完整，尤其是在智能体必须理解他人信念（心智理论）时。我们引入了观察者-情境格（OSL），一种统一的数学结构，为视角感知的认知提供了一个单一、连贯的语义空间。OSL是一个有限完备格，其中每个元素代表一个唯一的观察者-情境对，从而实现了一种原则性且可扩展的信念管理方法。我们提出了两个基于该格运算的关键算法：（i）相对化信念传播，一种高效传播新信息的增量更新算法，以及（ii）最小矛盾分解，一种识别和隔离矛盾分量的基于图的过程。我们证明了我们框架的理论可靠性，并通过一系列基准测试展示了其在实践中的效用，包括经典的心智理论任务以及与基于假设的真值维护系统等已建立范式的比较。 我们的结果表明，OSL为构建稳健的、视角感知的自主体提供了一个计算高效且富有表现力的基础。

TLDR: The paper introduces the Observer-Situation Lattice (OSL), a unified framework for perspective-aware reasoning, offering a scalable approach to manage beliefs in multi-agent environments and improve Theory of Mind capabilities. It demonstrates its effectiveness through benchmarks.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了观察者-情境格 (OSL)，一个统一的视角感知推理框架，为管理多智能体环境中的信念提供了一个可扩展的方法，并提高了心智理论的能力。并通过基准测试证明了其有效性。

Authors: Saad Alqithami

SubstratumGraphEnv: Reinforcement Learning Environment (RLE) for Modeling System Attack Paths

Abstract (English): Automating network security analysis, particularly the identification of potential attack paths, presents significant challenges. Due in part to the sequential, interconnected, and evolutionary nature of system events which most artificial intelligence (AI) techniques struggle to model effectively. This paper proposes a Reinforcement Learning (RL) environment generation framework that simulates the sequence of processes executed on a Windows operating system, enabling dynamic modeling of malicious processes on a system. This methodology models operating system state and transitions using a graph representation. This graph is derived from open-source System Monitor (Sysmon) logs. To address the variety in system event types, fields, and log formats, a mechanism was developed to capture and model parent-child processes from Sysmon logs. A Gymnasium environment (SubstratumGraphEnv) was constructed to establish the perceptible basis for an RL environment, and a customized PyTorch interface was also built (SubstratumBridge) to translate Gymnasium graphs into Deep Reinforcement Learning (DRL) observations and discrete actions. Graph Convolutional Networks (GCNs) concretize the graph's local and global state, which feed the distinct policy and critic heads of an Advantage Actor-Critic (A2C) model. This work's central contribution lies in the design of a novel deep graphical RL environment that automates translation of sequential user and system events, furnishing crucial context for cybersecurity analysis. This work provides a foundation for future research into shaping training parameters and advanced reward shaping, while also offering insight into which system events attributes are critical to training autonomous RL agents.

摘要（中文）: 自动化网络安全分析，尤其是潜在攻击路径的识别，面临着重大挑战。部分原因是系统事件的序列性、互连性和演化性，使得大多数人工智能 (AI) 技术难以有效建模。本文提出了一种强化学习 (RL) 环境生成框架，该框架模拟 Windows 操作系统上执行的进程序列，从而能够对系统上的恶意进程进行动态建模。该方法使用图表示来建模操作系统状态和转换。该图源自开源系统监视器 (Sysmon) 日志。为了解决系统事件类型、字段和日志格式的多样性，开发了一种机制来捕获和建模来自 Sysmon 日志的父子进程。构建了一个 Gymnasium 环境 (SubstratumGraphEnv) 以建立 RL 环境的感知基础，并且还构建了一个定制的 PyTorch 接口 (SubstratumBridge) 以将 Gymnasium 图转换为深度强化学习 (DRL) 观察和离散动作。图卷积网络 (GCN) 将图的局部和全局状态具体化，为其优势行动者-评论家 (A2C) 模型的不同策略和评论头提供输入。这项工作的核心贡献在于设计了一种新颖的深度图形 RL 环境，该环境能够自动翻译连续的用户和系统事件，为网络安全分析提供关键的上下文信息。这项工作为未来研究训练参数的塑造和高级奖励塑造奠定了基础，同时还提供了关于哪些系统事件属性对于训练自主 RL 代理至关重要的见解。

TLDR: This paper introduces SubstratumGraphEnv, a reinforcement learning environment (RLE) framework for modeling system attack paths using system logs and graph convolutional networks, offering a novel approach for automated cybersecurity analysis through deep graphical reinforcement learning.

简要说明: 本文介绍了SubstratumGraphEnv，这是一个强化学习环境（RLE）框架，用于使用系统日志和图卷积网络对系统攻击路径进行建模，通过深度图形强化学习为自动化网络安全分析提供了一种新颖的方法。

Authors: Bahirah Adewunmi, Edward Raff, Sanjay Purushotham

Opponent State Inference Under Partial Observability: An HMM-POMDP Framework for 2026 Formula 1 Energy Strategy

Abstract (English): The 2026 Formula 1 technical regulations introduce a fundamental change to energy strategy: under a 50/50 internal combustion engine / battery power split with unlimited regeneration and a driver-controlled Override Mode (abbreviated MOM throughout), the optimal energy deployment policy depends not only on a driver's own state but on the hidden state of rival cars. This creates a Partially Observable Stochastic Game that cannot be solved by single-agent optimisation methods. We present a tractable two-layer inference and decision framework. The first layer is a 30-state Hidden Markov Model (HMM) that infers a probability distribution over each rival's ERS charge level, Override Mode status, and tyre degradation state from five publicly observable telemetry signals. The second layer is a Deep Q-Network (DQN) policy that takes the HMM belief state as input and selects between energy deployment strategies. We formally characterise the counter-harvest trap -- a deceptive strategy in which a car deliberately suppresses observable deployment signals to induce a rival into a failed attack -- and show that detecting it requires belief-state inference rather than reactive threshold rules. On synthetic races generated from the model's own assumptions, the HMM achieves 92.3% ERS inference accuracy (random baseline: 33.3%) and detects counter-harvest trap conditions with 95.7% recall. Pre-registration -- empirical validation begins Australian Grand Prix, 8 March 2026.

摘要（中文）: 2026年一级方程式技术规则对能量策略引入了根本性变革：在内燃机/电池动力50/50分配、无限能量再生和车手控制的超越模式(文中简称MOM)下，最优的能量部署策略不仅取决于车手自身状态，还取决于竞争对手赛车的隐藏状态。这构成了一个部分可观测随机博弈，无法通过单智能体优化方法解决。我们提出一种易于处理的两层推理和决策框架。第一层是具有30个状态的隐马尔可夫模型(HMM)，它通过五个公开可观测的遥测信号推断每个竞争对手的能量回收系统(ERS)充电水平、超越模式状态和轮胎衰减状态的概率分布。第二层是一个深度Q网络(DQN)策略，它将HMM置信状态作为输入并在能量部署策略之间进行选择。我们正式描述了反向能量回收陷阱——一种欺骗性策略，其中赛车故意抑制可观测的能量部署信号以诱导竞争对手进行失败的攻击——并表明检测它需要置信状态推理而不是反应式阈值规则。在使用模型自身假设生成的合成比赛中，HMM实现了92.3%的ERS推理精度（随机基线：33.3%），并以95.7%的召回率检测反向能量回收陷阱条件。预注册——经验验证始于2026年3月8日澳大利亚大奖赛。

TLDR: The paper presents a Hidden Markov Model - Deep Q-Network framework to infer opponent states and optimize energy deployment in 2026 Formula 1 races, addressing the partial observability introduced by new regulations. It claims to detect counter-harvest traps with high accuracy.

简要说明: 该论文提出了一个隐马尔可夫模型 - 深度Q网络框架，用于推断2026年一级方程式比赛中的对手状态并优化能量部署，解决了新规则引入的部分可观测性问题。 它声称能以高精度检测反收割陷阱。
