Authors: Kalliopi Kleisarchaki

Process Over Outcome: Cultivating Forensic Reasoning for Generalizable Multimodal Manipulation Detection

Abstract (English): Recent advances in generative AI have significantly enhanced the realism of multimodal media manipulation, thereby posing substantial challenges to manipulation detection. Existing manipulation detection and grounding approaches predominantly focus on manipulation type classification under result-oriented supervision, which not only lacks interpretability but also tends to overfit superficial artifacts. In this paper, we argue that generalizable detection requires incorporating explicit forensic reasoning, rather than merely classifying a limited set of manipulation types, which fails to generalize to unseen manipulation patterns. To this end, we propose REFORM, a reasoning-driven framework that shifts learning from outcome fitting to process modeling. REFORM adopts a three-stage curriculum that first induces forensic rationales, then aligns reasoning with final judgments, and finally refines logical consistency via reinforcement learning. To support this paradigm, we introduce ROM, a large-scale dataset with rich reasoning annotations. Extensive experiments show that REFORM establishes new state-of-the-art performance with superior generalization, achieving 81.52% ACC on ROM, 76.65% ACC on DGM4, and 74.9 F1 on MMFakeBench.

摘要（中文）: 生成式人工智能的最新进展极大地提高了多模态媒体操纵的真实性，从而对操纵检测提出了严峻挑战。现有的操纵检测和定位方法主要侧重于结果导向监督下的操纵类型分类，这不仅缺乏可解释性，而且容易过度拟合表面的伪影。在本文中，我们认为，可泛化的检测需要纳入显式取证推理，而不是仅仅对有限的操纵类型进行分类，后者无法泛化到未见的操纵模式。为此，我们提出了REFORM，一个推理驱动的框架，该框架将学习从结果拟合转移到过程建模。REFORM采用三阶段课程，首先诱导取证理由，然后将推理与最终判断对齐，最后通过强化学习来完善逻辑一致性。为了支持这种范式，我们引入了ROM，一个具有丰富推理注释的大规模数据集。大量实验表明，REFORM建立了新的最先进的性能，并具有卓越的泛化能力，在ROM上实现了81.52%的准确率(ACC)，在DGM4上实现了76.65%的准确率(ACC)，在MMFakeBench上实现了74.9的F1值。

TLDR: The paper introduces REFORM, a reasoning-driven framework for multimodal manipulation detection that uses a three-stage curriculum and a new dataset (ROM) with reasoning annotations to improve generalization performance compared to existing methods.

简要说明: 本文介绍了一种名为 REFORM 的多模态操纵检测框架，该框架采用三阶段课程和一个新的带有推理注释的数据集 (ROM)，与现有方法相比，提高了泛化性能。

Authors: Yuchen Zhang, Yaxiong Wang, Kecheng Han, Yujiao Wu, Lianwei Wu, Li Zhu, Zhedong Zheng

GroupEnsemble: Efficient Uncertainty Estimation for DETR-based Object Detection

Abstract (English): Detection Transformer (DETR) and its variants show strong performance on object detection, a key task for autonomous systems. However, a critical limitation of these models is that their confidence scores only reflect semantic uncertainty, failing to capture the equally important spatial uncertainty. This results in an incomplete assessment of the detection reliability. On the other hand, Deep Ensembles can tackle this by providing high-quality spatial uncertainty estimates. However, their immense memory consumption makes them impractical for real-world applications. A cheaper alternative, Monte Carlo (MC) Dropout, suffers from high latency due to the need of multiple forward passes during inference to estimate uncertainty. To address these limitations, we introduce GroupEnsemble, an efficient and effective uncertainty estimation method for DETR-like models. GroupEnsemble simultaneously predicts multiple individual detection sets by feeding additional diverse groups of object queries to the transformer decoder during inference. Each query group is transformed by the shared decoder in isolation and predicts a complete detection set for the same input. An attention mask is applied to the decoder to prevent inter-group query interactions, ensuring each group detects independently to achieve reliable ensemble-based uncertainty estimation. By leveraging the decoder's inherent parallelism, GroupEnsemble efficiently estimates uncertainty in a single forward pass without sequential repetition. We validated our method under autonomous driving scenes and common daily scenes using the Cityscapes and COCO datasets, respectively. The results show that a hybrid approach combining MC-Dropout and GroupEnsemble outperforms Deep Ensembles on several metrics at a fraction of the cost. The code is available at https://github.com/yutongy98/GroupEnsemble.

摘要（中文）: 检测Transformer (DETR) 及其变体在目标检测 (自动驾驶系统的关键任务) 上表现出强大的性能。然而，这些模型的一个关键限制是，它们的置信度分数仅反映语义不确定性，未能捕捉到同样重要的空间不确定性。 这导致对检测可靠性的评估不完整。另一方面，深度集成可以通过提供高质量的空间不确定性估计来解决这个问题。然而，它们巨大的内存消耗使得它们在实际应用中不切实际。蒙特卡洛 (MC) Dropout是一种更廉价的替代方案，但由于需要在推理期间进行多次前向传递才能估计不确定性，因此存在高延迟问题。 为了解决这些限制，我们引入了GroupEnsemble，一种用于类DETR模型的高效且有效的不确定性估计方法。GroupEnsemble通过在推理期间向Transformer解码器提供额外的多样化的目标查询组，同时预测多个单独的检测集。每个查询组由共享解码器独立转换，并为相同的输入预测一个完整的检测集。注意力掩码被应用于解码器，以防止组间查询交互，确保每个组独立检测，从而实现可靠的基于集成的的不确定性估计。通过利用解码器的固有并行性，GroupEnsemble在单次前向传播中有效地估计不确定性，而无需顺序重复。我们使用Cityscapes和COCO数据集分别在自动驾驶场景和常见日常场景下验证了我们的方法。结果表明，混合MC-Dropout和GroupEnsemble 的方法在几个指标上优于深度集成，且成本仅为其一小部分。代码可在https://github.com/yutongy98/GroupEnsemble 获取。

TLDR: The paper introduces GroupEnsemble, an efficient uncertainty estimation method for DETR-like object detection models, addressing the limitations of existing approaches like Deep Ensembles and MC-Dropout.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了GroupEnsemble，一种用于DETR类目标检测模型的高效不确定性估计方法，旨在解决现有方法（如Deep Ensembles和MC-Dropout）的局限性。

Authors: Yutong Yang, Katarina Popović, Julian Wiederer, Markus Braun, Vasileios Belagiannis, Bin Yang

Downstream Task Inspired Underwater Image Enhancement: A Perception-Aware Study from Dataset Construction to Network Design

Abstract (English): In real underwater environments, downstream image recognition tasks such as semantic segmentation and object detection often face challenges posed by problems like blurring and color inconsistencies. Underwater image enhancement (UIE) has emerged as a promising preprocessing approach, aiming to improve the recognizability of targets in underwater images. However, most existing UIE methods mainly focus on enhancing images for human visual perception, frequently failing to reconstruct high-frequency details that are critical for task-specific recognition. To address this issue, we propose a Downstream Task-Inspired Underwater Image Enhancement (DTI-UIE) framework, which leverages human visual perception model to enhance images effectively for underwater vision tasks. Specifically, we design an efficient two-branch network with task-aware attention module for feature mixing. The network benefits from a multi-stage training framework and a task-driven perceptual loss. Additionally, inspired by human perception, we automatically construct a Task-Inspired UIE Dataset (TI-UIED) using various task-specific networks. Experimental results demonstrate that DTI-UIE significantly improves task performance by generating preprocessed images that are beneficial for downstream tasks such as semantic segmentation, object detection, and instance segmentation. The codes are publicly available at https://github.com/oucailab/DTIUIE.

摘要（中文）: 在真实水下环境中，语义分割和目标检测等下游图像识别任务经常面临模糊和颜色不一致等问题带来的挑战。水下图像增强（UIE）作为一种有前景的预处理方法应运而生，旨在提高水下图像中目标的可识别性。然而，现有的大多数UIE方法主要侧重于为人类视觉感知增强图像，经常无法重建对于特定任务识别至关重要的高频细节。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种下游任务驱动的水下图像增强（DTI-UIE）框架，该框架利用人类视觉感知模型来有效地为水下视觉任务增强图像。具体来说，我们设计了一个高效的双分支网络，带有任务感知注意力模块，用于特征融合。该网络受益于多阶段训练框架和任务驱动的感知损失。此外，受到人类感知的启发，我们使用各种特定任务网络自动构建了一个任务驱动的UIE数据集（TI-UIED）。实验结果表明，DTI-UIE通过生成有利于语义分割、目标检测和实例分割等下游任务的预处理图像，显著提高了任务性能。代码已公开在https://github.com/oucailab/DTIUIE。

TLDR: The paper introduces a Downstream Task-Inspired Underwater Image Enhancement (DTI-UIE) framework that utilizes human visual perception models and task-aware attention to enhance underwater images for downstream tasks like semantic segmentation and object detection. They also construct a Task-Inspired UIE Dataset (TI-UIED) automatically.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了一个下游任务驱动的水下图像增强（DTI-UIE）框架，该框架利用人类视觉感知模型和任务感知注意机制来增强水下图像，以用于语义分割和对象检测等下游任务。他们还自动构建了一个任务驱动的UIE数据集（TI-UIED）。

Authors: Bosen Lin, Feng Gao, Yanwei Yu, Junyu Dong, Qian Du

Action-Guided Attention for Video Action Anticipation

Abstract (English): Anticipating future actions in videos is challenging, as the observed frames provide only evidence of past activities, requiring the inference of latent intentions to predict upcoming actions. Existing transformer-based approaches, which rely on dot-product attention over pixel representations, often lack the high-level semantics necessary to model video sequences for effective action anticipation. As a result, these methods tend to overfit to explicit visual cues present in the past frames, limiting their ability to capture underlying intentions and degrading generalization to unseen samples. To address this, we propose Action-Guided Attention (AGA), an attention mechanism that explicitly leverages predicted action sequences as queries and keys to guide sequence modeling. Our approach fosters the attention module to emphasize relevant moments from the past based on the upcoming activity and combine this information with the current frame embedding via a dedicated gating function. The design of AGA enables post-training analysis of the knowledge discovered from the training set. Experiments on the widely adopted EPIC-Kitchens-100 benchmark demonstrate that AGA generalizes well from validation to unseen test sets. Post-training analysis can further examine the action dependencies captured by the model and the counterfactual evidence it has internalized, offering transparent and interpretable insights into its anticipative predictions.

摘要（中文）: 在视频中预测未来的动作极具挑战性，因为观察到的帧仅提供过去活动的信息，需要推断潜在意图才能预测即将发生的动作。现有的基于Transformer的方法依赖于像素表示上的点积注意力机制，但通常缺乏对视频序列进行建模以有效预测动作所需的高级语义信息。因此，这些方法倾向于过度拟合过去帧中存在的显式视觉线索，从而限制了它们捕捉潜在意图的能力，并降低了对未见样本的泛化能力。为了解决这个问题，我们提出了一种动作引导注意力（AGA）机制，该机制明确地利用预测的动作序列作为查询和键来指导序列建模。我们的方法促进了注意力模块，使其能够基于即将发生的活动来强调过去的相关时刻，并通过专门的门控函数将此信息与当前帧嵌入相结合。AGA的设计使得能够在训练后分析从训练集中发现的知识。在广泛使用的EPIC-Kitchens-100基准上的实验表明，AGA能够很好地从验证集泛化到未见的测试集。训练后的分析可以进一步检验模型捕获的动作依赖关系以及其内化的反事实证据，从而为预测性预测提供透明且可解释的见解。

TLDR: The paper introduces Action-Guided Attention (AGA), a novel attention mechanism for video action anticipation that uses predicted action sequences to guide sequence modeling, improving generalization and interpretability compared to existing transformer-based methods.

简要说明: 本文提出了一种新的视频行为预测注意力机制Action-Guided Attention (AGA)，它使用预测的行为序列来指导序列建模，与现有的基于Transformer的方法相比，提高了泛化能力和可解释性。

Authors: Tsung-Ming Tai, Sofia Casarin, Andrea Pilzer, Werner Nutt, Oswald Lanz

UltraStar: Semantic-Aware Star Graph Modeling for Echocardiography Navigation

Abstract (English): Echocardiography is critical for diagnosing cardiovascular diseases, yet the shortage of skilled sonographers hinders timely patient care, due to high operational difficulties. Consequently, research on automated probe navigation has significant clinical potential. To achieve robust navigation, it is essential to leverage historical scanning information, mimicking how experts rely on past feedback to adjust subsequent maneuvers. Practical scanning data collected from sonographers typically consists of noisy trajectories inherently generated through trial-and-error exploration. However, existing methods typically model this history as a sequential chain, forcing models to overfit these noisy paths, leading to performance degradation on long sequences. In this paper, we propose UltraStar, which reformulates probe navigation from path regression to anchor-based global localization. By establishing a Star Graph, UltraStar treats historical keyframes as spatial anchors connected directly to the current view, explicitly modeling geometric constraints for precise positioning. We further enhance the Star Graph with a semantic-aware sampling strategy that actively selects the representative landmarks from massive history logs, reducing redundancy for accurate anchoring. Extensive experiments on a dataset with over 1.31 million samples demonstrate that UltraStar outperforms baselines and scales better with longer input lengths, revealing a more effective topology for history modeling under noisy exploration.

摘要（中文）: 超声心动图对于诊断心血管疾病至关重要，但由于操作难度高，熟练超声医师的短缺阻碍了及时的患者护理。因此，自动化探头导航的研究具有重要的临床潜力。为了实现稳健的导航，利用历史扫描信息至关重要，这模仿了专家如何依靠过去的反馈来调整后续操作。从超声医师处收集的实际扫描数据通常包含噪声轨迹，这些轨迹本质上是通过试错探索产生的。然而，现有方法通常将此历史记录建模为序列链，迫使模型过度拟合这些噪声路径，从而导致长序列上的性能下降。在本文中，我们提出了UltraStar，它将探头导航从路径回归重新定义为基于锚点的全局定位。通过建立星图，UltraStar将历史关键帧视为直接连接到当前视图的空间锚点，显式地对几何约束进行建模以实现精确定位。我们进一步使用一种语义感知采样策略来增强星图，该策略主动地从大量历史日志中选择具有代表性的地标，从而减少冗余以实现准确的锚定。在一个包含超过131万个样本的数据集上进行的大量实验表明，UltraStar的性能优于基线，并且在更长的输入长度下能够更好地扩展，这揭示了一种在噪声探索下进行历史建模的更有效的拓扑结构。

TLDR: The paper introduces UltraStar, a semantic-aware star graph modeling approach for echocardiography navigation, addressing the challenge of noisy historical data by using keyframes as spatial anchors for robust probe localization.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了UltraStar，一种用于超声心动图导航的语义感知星图建模方法，通过使用关键帧作为空间锚点，解决了噪声历史数据的问题，从而实现稳健的探头定位。

Authors: Teng Wang, Haojun Jiang, Chenxi Li, Diwen Wang, Yihang Tang, Zhenguo Sun, Yujiao Deng, Shiji Song, Gao Huang

Accelerating PDE Surrogates via RL-Guided Mesh Optimization

Abstract (English): Deep surrogate models for parametric partial differential equations (PDEs) can deliver high-fidelity approximations but remain prohibitively data-hungry: training often requires thousands of fine-grid simulations, each incurring substantial computational cost. To address this challenge, we introduce RLMesh, an end-to-end framework for efficient surrogate training under limited simulation budget. The key idea is to use reinforcement learning (RL) to adaptively allocate mesh grid points non-uniformly within each simulation domain, focusing numerical resolution in regions most critical for accurate PDE solutions. A lightweight proxy model further accelerates RL training by providing efficient reward estimates without full surrogate retraining. Experiments on PDE benchmarks demonstrate that RLMesh achieves competitive accuracy to baselines but with substantially fewer simulation queries. These results show that solver-level spatial adaptivity can dramatically improve the efficiency of surrogate training pipelines, enabling practical deployment of learning-based PDE surrogates across a wide range of problems.

摘要（中文）: 用于参数偏微分方程（PDEs）的深度代理模型可以提供高保真的近似，但仍然存在数据需求过高的问题：训练通常需要数千次精细网格模拟，每次都会产生大量的计算成本。为了解决这一挑战，我们引入了RLMesh，这是一个在有限模拟预算下进行高效代理训练的端到端框架。其关键思想是使用强化学习（RL）在每个模拟域内非均匀地自适应分配网格点，将数值分辨率集中在对精确PDE解至关重要的区域。一个轻量级的代理模型通过提供高效的奖励估计，而无需完全重新训练代理模型，进一步加速了RL训练。在PDE基准测试上的实验表明，RLMesh在精度上与基线方法具有竞争力，但所需的模拟查询次数却大大减少。这些结果表明，求解器级别的空间自适应可以显著提高代理训练管道的效率，从而能够在广泛的问题中实际部署基于学习的PDE代理。

TLDR: The paper introduces RLMesh, an RL-guided framework for adaptive mesh refinement in PDE surrogate training, significantly reducing the number of required simulations while maintaining competitive accuracy.

简要说明: 该论文介绍了RLMesh，一个通过强化学习指导的自适应网格细化框架，用于PDE代理模型的训练，显著减少了所需的模拟次数，同时保持了具有竞争力的准确性。

PromptStereo: Zero-Shot Stereo Matching via Structure and Motion Prompts

TLDR-CN: 该论文介绍了一种名为PromptStereo的新型零样本立体匹配方法，该方法利用Prompt循环单元（PRU）通过将结构和运动线索集成到单目深度基础模型的解码器中来细化视差估计，从而实现最先进的性能。

Boosting AI Reliability with an FSM-Driven Streaming Inference Pipeline: An Industrial Case

Authors: Yutian Zhang, Zhongyi Pei, Yi Mao, Chen Wang, Lin Liu, Jianmin Wang

TLDR-CN: 本文提出了一种使用有限状态机（FSM）的流式推理管道，以提高人工智能模型在工业应用中的可靠性，特别是从监控视频中计数挖掘机的工作量。

SCATR: Mitigating New Instance Suppression in LiDAR-based Tracking-by-Attention via Second Chance Assignment and Track Query Dropout

Authors: Brian Cheong, Letian Wang, Sandro Papais, Steven L. Waslander

TLDR-CN: 本文提出了SCATR，一种新颖的基于LiDAR的跟踪-注意模型，具有两种架构无关的训练策略（第二次机会分配和跟踪查询退出），该模型在nuScenes数据集上实现了最先进的性能，并缩小了与跟踪-检测方法的性能差距。

SeaVIS: Sound-Enhanced Association for Online Audio-Visual Instance Segmentation

Authors: Yingjian Zhu, Ying Wang, Yuyang Hong, Ruohao Guo, Kun Ding, Xin Gu, Bin Fan, Shiming Xiang

TLDR-CN: 该论文介绍了SeaVIS，这是第一个在线的音视频实例分割框架。它通过使用因果注意力和音频引导的对比学习来改善连续视频流中的实例关联，解决了离线方法的局限性，尤其是在区分发声和静默状态方面。

UETrack: A Unified and Efficient Framework for Single Object Tracking

Authors: Ben Kang, Jie Zhao, Xin Chen, Wanting Geng, Bin Zhang, Lu Zhang, Dong Wang, Huchuan Lu

TLDR-CN: UETrack是一个新的高效单目标跟踪框架，它使用基于Token-Pooling 的混合专家模型和目标感知的自适应蒸馏来处理多模态数据（RGB、深度、热感、事件、语言），从而实现了卓越的速度-精度平衡。

Causal Effects with Unobserved Unit Types in Interacting Human-AI Systems

Authors: William Overman, Sadegh Shirani, Mohsen Bayati

TLDR-CN: 本文提出了一种在人机交互系统中估计人类特定因果效应的方法，其中个体类型（人类与人工智能）和交互网络均未被观察到，该方法利用种群组成分布知识和因果消息传递框架。

B$^2$F-Map: Crowd-sourced Mapping with Bayesian B-spline Fusion

Authors: Yiping Xie, Yuxuan Xia, Erik Stenborg, Junsheng Fu, Axel Beauvisage, Gabriel E. Garcia, Tianyu Wu, Gustaf Hendeby

TLDR-CN: 本文提出了B$^2$F-Map，一个使用众包数据生成高精度地图的流水线，并利用贝叶斯B样条融合来处理单目相机和消费级传感器车道线估计中的不确定性。

PreSight: Preoperative Outcome Prediction for Parkinson's Disease via Region-Prior Morphometry and Patient-Specific Weighting

Authors: Yand Wang, Chen Zhang, Lanyun Zhu, Yixin Chen, Qunbo Wang, Yutong Bai, Jurgen Germann, Yinghong Wen, Shuai Shao

TLDR-CN: 该论文提出了 PreSight，一个用于预测帕金森病手术术前结果的模型，它利用 MRI、基于形变的形态测量学以及患者特异性权重，在高精度预测术后运动功能改善方面表现出色。

AutoFFS: Adversarial Deformations for Facial Feminization Surgery Planning

Authors: Paul Friedrich, Florentin Bieder, Florian M. Thieringer, Philippe C. Cattin

TLDR-CN: 该论文介绍了一个名为AutoFFS的框架，它使用对抗性形变来生成颅骨形态，用于面部女性化手术的规划，旨在为该手术提供定量指导。

Affine Correspondences in Stereo Vision: Theory, Practice, and Limitations

Authors: Levente Hajder

TLDR-CN: 本文提出了一种在立体视觉中估计仿射变换的技术，以提高三维重建的精度，特别是对于表面法线，并在合成数据和真实数据上评估了性能。

Preoperative-to-intraoperative Liver Registration for Laparoscopic Surgery via Latent-Grounded Correspondence Constraints

Authors: Ruize Cui, Jialun Pei, Haiqiao Wang, Jun Zhou, Jeremy Yuen-Chun Teoh, Pheng-Ann Heng, Jing Qin

TLDR-CN: 该论文介绍了一种名为Land-Reg的新框架，用于腹腔镜肝脏手术，它使用潜在的2D-3D地标对应来改善术前到术中的肝脏配准，并在P2ILF数据集上表现出优越的性能。

PPEDCRF: Privacy-Preserving Enhanced Dynamic CRF for Location-Privacy Protection for Sequence Videos with Minimal Detection Degradation

Authors: Bo Ma, Jinsong Wu, Weiqi Yan, Catherine Shi, Minh Nguyen

TLDR-CN: 该论文提出了PPEDCRF，一个隐私保护框架，使用动态条件随机场和校准扰动来保护行车记录仪视频中的位置隐私，同时保持目标检测的准确性，解决基于位置推断的攻击。

TopoMaskV3: 3D Mask Head with Dense Offset and Height Predictions for Road Topology Understanding

Authors: Muhammet Esat Kalfaoglu, Halil Ibrahim Ozturk, Ozsel Kilinc, Alptekin Temizel

TLDR-CN: TopoMaskV3 提出了一种基于 3D 掩码的道路拓扑理解方法，通过密集的偏移和高度预测，并引入地理上分离的基准来解决评估中的地理数据泄露问题，实现了最先进的结果。

Security Risks in Machining Process Monitoring: Sequence-to-Sequence Learning for Reconstruction of CNC Axis Positions

Authors: Lukas Krupp, Rickmar Stahlschmidt, Norbert Wehn

TLDR-CN: 本文证明了序列到序列的学习模型可以从加速度计数据中重建数控轴的位置，强调了加工过程监控中潜在的安全风险。与传统方法相比，该方法显着降低了重建误差。

Randomized Kiring Believer for Parallel Bayesian Optimization with Regret Bounds

Authors: Shuhei Sugiura, Ichiro Takeuchi, Shion Takeno

TLDR-CN: 本文提出了一种并行贝叶斯优化(PBO)方法，称为随机克里金信徒(KB)，并提供了后悔界限，旨在提高优化昂贵的黑盒函数的性能和理论严谨性。

From GEV to ResLogit: Spatially Correlated Discrete Choice Models for Pedestrian Movement Prediction

Authors: Rulla Al-Haideri, Bilal Farooq

TLDR-CN: 该论文比较了空间GEV和残差神经网络logit（ResLogit）模型在行人运动预测方面的表现，发现ResLogit在捕捉密集、高频空间选择中的邻近诱导相关性方面优于空间GEV结构，同时保持了可解释性。
